Izinkonzo Kubantu
Ukubhekana nomthetho
Isondlo
Isicelo sesondlo
Isimo
Umsebenzi wokondla ubekwa ngokulandlela ubuhlobo begazi, ukwamukela ingane engeyona eyakho ngokuzalwa (adoption), noma ukuthi abantu baganene.
Ingane kumele yondliwe noma inakekelwe ngu/ngo abazali bayo, ngisho noma bashadile noma kabashadile, abahlalisanayo, abehlukene, abadivosile, ababandakanya abazali abamukele ingane okungeyona eyabo ngokuzalwa ukuyinakekela; kanye/noma ogogo noma omkhulu wabyo, ngisho noma abazali babeshadile noma babengashadile. Kodwa lokhu, kuyehluka kudaba nodaba.
Umsebenzi wokondla ilunga lomndeni alipheleli nje ekondleni ingane. Ilunga lomndeni, ngisho noma lineminyaka emingaki, lingacela noma yiliphi ilunga lomndeni ukulondla, kodwa uma kuhlangatshezwane nezimo ezilandelayo:
Ilunga lomndeni elikleyima isondlo, lingakwazi ukuzinakekela ngokwalo.
Ilunga lomndeni lapho okufunwa kulo isondlo, likwazi ukuba kwisimo sokondla.
Ungafuna ukusekelwa ngesizathu esibambekayo ukunikeza ingane noma umuntu onelungelo lokondliwa ukuthi aphile kwisimo esifanele nokukhula okufanele. Lokhu kubandakanya ukuhlinzeka ngezinto ezifanele ezifana nokudla, okokwembatha nendawo yokuhlala kanye nokukhokhela imali yemfundo yangempela. Inkantolo ingakhipha umyalelo wokuthi ubaba akhiphe imali yezindleko zonakekelo lwengane ukusukela usuku eyazalwa ngalo ukufinyelela kusuku umyalelo okhishwa ngalo. Inkantolo inganikeza umyalelo wokuthi kukhokhelwe izindleko zemithi, noma inganikeza umyalelo wokuthi ingane ibhaliswe kwisikimu sonakekelo lwempilo ngemithi i-medical scheme somunye wabathintekayo kulolu daba. Ukuze inkantolo ikwazi ukukhipha umyalelo ofanele nolungile, bobabili kumele banikeze inkantolo ubufakazi bezindleko zabo.
Umbono wakho ngokuziphatha komunye umzali akunamthelela kwilungelo lengane lokunakekelwa. Kusafanele ukhokhe isondlo, ngisho noma ngabe omunye umzali usebuye washada;
akakuvumeli ukuthi ubone izingane; noma omunye kwababili ubuye waba nezinye izingane.
Umsebenzi wakho wokunakekela ingane awunamthelela kwilungelo lakho lokubona ingane, lokhu kuphatha njengezinto ezehlukene. Nangaphezu kwalokho, izingane ngakolunye uhlangothi azinamthelela womsebenzi wokudla. Kodwa, inani lesondlo okumele likhokhelwe lingashintshwa yinkantolo uma omunye kwababili elatha isicelo sokwenza lokho.
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi ngena kwi-website yoMnyango wezoBulungisa nokuThuthukiswa koMthethosisekelo.
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yenza isicelo sesondlo enkantolo kamantshi kudistriki ohlala kuyo.
Uma ungabaza, inkantolo yendawo izokutshela ukuthi yiyiphi inkantolo okumele wenze kuyo isicelo sesondlo.
Nangaphezulu kwefomu eligcwalisiwe, nikeza ubufakazi bengeniso (bomholo) wakho wenyanga kanye nezindleko, okufane namarisidi okuthenga ukudla, ugesi kanye/noma ukukhokhela irente.
Inkantolo izobeka usuku lapho wena kanye nommangalelayo (umuntu ofisa ukuthi akhokhele isondlo) okumele niye ngalo enkantolo.
Isikhulu sezondlo kanye nomphenyisisi uzophenya ikleyimu yakho kanye nokubheka izimo zakho.
Inkantolo izokhipha amasamanisi (incwadi eyalela umuntu ukuyovela enkantolo) kummangalelwa (umuntu okufuneka kuye isondlo) ukuze avele ekantolo ngosuku oluthile ukuxoxisana ngodaba.
Ummangalelwa angakhetha uuthi avume ukukhokhela isondlo esifunwayo noma ukuya enkantolo ukuyophikisana nalolu daba.
Uma ummangalelwa evuma ukukhokha isondlo esifunwayo, umantshi uzobuyekeza amadokhumende afanele. Ebese ekhipha umyalelo, kanti futhi angathatha isinqumo sokwenza lokho ngaphandle kokuthi abathintekayo bobabili bavele enkantolo.
Uma umuntu okufanele akhokhe isondlo engavumi ukukhipha isondlo, kumele avele enkantolo, lapho khona okuzolalelwa ubufakazi obuvela kubo bobabili abathintekayo.
Uma ikantolo ithola ukuthi kumele akhokhe isondlo, ikantolo izokwenza umyalelo wokuthi akhipha malini. Inkantolo izobeka nokuthi isondlo sizokhishwa nini futhi kanjani.
Inkantolo ingakhipha umyalelo wokuthi imali ikhishwe ngenye yezindlela ezilandelayo:
Ehhovisi likamantshi wendawo, noma kwamanye amahhovisi ombuso abekelwe lenhloso
Kwi-akhawunti yebhangi noma kwi-building society yalowo muntu othintekayo
Ikhishwe iqondiswe ngqo kulowo muntu okumele athole imali
Ngomyalelo wokuthi imali kumele idonswe emholweni wommangalelwa, idonswe ngumqashi wommangalelwa, okumele akhokhele isondlo, ngokulandela umthetho wezondlo we-Maintanance Act, 1998
Imithetho nemigomo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Nizophathwa ngokulinganayo, ngobuntu kanye nesithunzi.
Izikhalazo zizobonelelwa ngokushesha ngendlela enomphumela ngendlela okungakhonakala ngayo.
Asikho isikhathi esibekiwe (esinqunyiwe) sale nkonzo (sale sevisi). Yonke inqubo, ukusukela ekufakelweni kwesicelo ukufinyelela lapho uthola khona imali yesondlo, ingathatha amaviki ambalwa, kuye ngokusebenzisana kwezinhlangothi zombili zabathintekayo.
Ofakazi abahambela ukulalelwa kodaba, okubandkanya abantu abafuna isondlo, bangathola imali ebekiwe yesondlo kanye nezindleko zokuhamba. Kodwa, inkantolo izothatha isinqumo ngokubona kwayo ukuthi ngabe lawo ma-alawensi azokhokhelwa umuntu owenze isimangalo nesicelo somyalelo.
Umuntu ocela isondlo kumele enze isicelo kusikhulu sezondlo ngokukhokhela imali yofakazi.
Izindleko (Imali ekhokhwayo)
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Izincazelo
Le nkonzo isebenza kumuntu owemukelwe njengombaleki odinga ukukhosela eNingizimu Afrika. Izicelo zingenziwa ehhovisi eliseduzane labezindaba zaseKhaya kwisifundazwe (kwiprovinsi) ohlala kuyo Kumele ube usebe nesimo sokuba ngumkhoseli iminyake emihlanu ukuze wenze isicelo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Fakela incwadi efungelwe kwabomthetho i-afidavithi yawo wonke amagama owasebenzisayo.
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo, i-BI-947 no-BI-29E.

Izimvume namalayisense
Ukukhiqiza izitshalo
Isicelo samalungelo okwandisa izinhlobo zezitshalo
Isicelo samalungelo okwandisa izinhlobo zezitshalo
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Izingxenye zezitshalo ezisetshenziswa ukwandisa ezingakaze zithengiswe eNingizimu Afrika isikhathi esingaphezulu konyaka.
Zikwisimo sezinhlobo zesihlahla, noma umvini, izingxenye ezisetshenziswa ukwandisa, ezingekho kwelinye izwe, ngokuhweba noma kwimiphakathi esikhathini esingaphezulu kweminyaka eyisithupha, noma kwisimo sanoma yiyiphi inhlobo yesitshalo, kwiminyaka emine.
o kumele zibe ngezihlukene kwezinye ngendlela ecacile kunenye inhlobo ecishe ifane nalena o kumele ibesabuyunifomu, okusho ukuthi zonke izitshalo ezitshalwa ngasikhathi sinye kumele zibukeke zifana kanti futhi zibe ngezezinhlobo efanayo (same characteristics).
o kumele kube ngezizinzile (stable), izitshalo zohlobo oluthile, ngemuva kokutshalwa kaningi-ningi (repeated cultivation), kumele zihlale zisefana nezitshalo zakudala.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Uma uhlobo lwesitshalo lukwithebuli, kuzofuneka ufakele okulandelayo:
o izimali ezibekiwe okumele zikhokhelwe isicelo nohlolo o imbewu noma izinqanyana ezisetshenziselwa ukwandisa izitshalo o isigunyaziso esibhaliwe somnikazi, uma ungeyena umtshali owandisayo, inhlobo ekhethiwe noma yathuthukiswa.
Sicela ucacise kwi-questionnaire (kuhla lwemibuzo) ukuthi lenhlobo kumele iqhathaniswe naziphi izinhlobo ezaziwayo eNingizimu Afrika, izinhlobo ezintsha kumele ziqhathaniswe ukubona ukwehluka kwazo.
Ungenza isicelo sovikelo lwesikhashana senhlobo. Lokhu kusho ukuthi inhlobo ivikelwe ngaleso sikhathi sokuhlolwa. Kumele unikeze isibopho esibhaliwe sokuzimisela ukuthi ngeke uthengise (ngaphandle kwenhloso yokwandisa noma ukulinga - testing), noma yiziphi izinqanyana zokwandisa (propagating material) zenhlobo yesitshalo ehlolwayo kuze kufinyelele lapho lonke uhlolo seluphethiwe kanti amalungelo okwandisa isitshalo esinikeziwe. Kunconywa ukuthi kube novikelo lwesikhashana kwizitshalo lapho kwenziwa izinhlobo zohlolo ezithatha isikhathi esingaphezulu konyaka.
Uma uhla lohlobo lukhona kwinhlobo yesitshalo eyenzelwa isicelo samalungelo okwandiswa kwesitshalo, fakela izicelo kanye-kanye ukuze wonke isikhathi nemali.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo

Izimvume namalayisense
Isicelo sobhaliso yokudla kwemfuyo nokudla kwezilw
Isicelo sobhaliso (serejistreshini) yokudla kwemfuyo nokudla kwezilwanyana ezifuyiwe
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Inkampani yaphesheya ngeke yakwazi ukwenza isicelo sokubhalisa (serejistreshini) eNingizimu Afrila. Zinkampani ezibhalise lapha ezweni kuphela, isakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika, noma inhlangano esemthethweni ebhalise eNingizimu Afrika engenza isicelo sokudla kwemfuyo kanye nezinonisi (ama-fertilizer).
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Isicelo sobhaliso lokudla kwemfuyo kumele sibe nalokhu:
Incwadi ehambisana nesicelo ebuya kulowo obhalisile, noma kulowo oqokelwe ukumela obhalisile. Incwadi kumele ibandakanye nokulandelayo:
o isizathu sokuthumela isicelo (ngisho noma ngabe yisicelo esisha, isicelo sesichibiyelo, noma isicelo sobhaliso oluyindodakazi)
o incwadi yokuvuma uma kudingeka imvume kulowo obhalisile (imvume yobhaliso oluyindodakazi noma imvume yokusebenzisa igama le-product yenye inkampani kwilebuli)
Gcwalisa ifomu ngokugcwele, ngaphandle kokuchaza ngezicelo ezedlule, inombolo yekhasi noma amanye ama-product (okungenani ikhophi eyodwa yamafomu esicelo kumele ibe nomsayino wokuqala (original signature). Enye kungaba yikhophi).
Umuntu oqokwe ngokomthetho, okumele kube nguye ozoxhumanisa inkampani no-Registrar, kumele asayine amafomu ezicelo.
Igama kanye nenombolo yobhaliso (yerejistreshini) ye-product kumele isetshenziswe njengoba kuchaziwe kwisitifikedi sobhaliso (serejistreshini). Noma yiluphi ushintsho, ngisho noma luluncane kangakanani, luzothathwa njengesichibiyelo.
Fakela amafomu ezicelo kwihhovisi lezokuphatha. Ukuhambisa amafomu kuma-Technical Adviser, kuzochitha isikhathi ngokubambezela inqubo.
o yonke imininingwane kumalebuli kumele ihambelane ncamashi naleyo efakelwe kwifomu lesicelo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha cishe izinyanga ezintathu ukuya kwezine ukuthi ukudla kwemfuyo nokwezilwanyana ezifuyiwe (pet food) kurejistwe (kubhaliswe).

Izincazelo
Esinye isimo, kungaba lapho umholo olindelwe wanonyaka womqondisi (kadayirektha) ungaphansi kwalowo owatholakala ngonyaka odlule, kanti lokhu kuzobangela ukuthi kube nokukhokhwa okweqile kwentela ye-PAYE, ngendlela engabangela ubunzima.
Lapho inkampani (umqashi) engakahloli inkokhelo yanyakenye (yonyaka odlule), kumele acele ihhovisi legatsha eliseduze labe-SARS ukuhlola inani lomholo okuthathwa ngokuthi umqondisi (udayirektha) walithola.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Kumele unikeze lolu lwazi olungezansi kwifomu lesicelo lapho wenza isicelo:
Unyaka wokuhlolwa kwentela okwamukelwa ngawo lowo mholo.
Inani lezinyanga eziphelile, lapho okwatholwa khona lowo mholo.
Isilinganiso noma inani langampela lomholo walo nyaka. (Lolu lwazi kumele lunikezwe uma isizathu sokucela umyalelo wentela kuwubunzima.)
Isicelo kumele sifakele izizathu zokucela umyalelo wentela. Lokhu kumele kusekelwe ngamadokhumende, isibonelo, izitatimende zezimali, kanye namaminithi emihlangano ebanjwa ngabaqondisi (ngodayirektha), akhombisa ukuthi inani lomholo okumele likhokhelwe abaqondisi, lizoba ngaphansi kwenani abakhokhelwa lona ngonyaka odlule.

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Esinye isimo, kungaba lapho umholo olindelwe wanonyaka womqondisi (kadayirektha) ungaphansi kwalowo owatholakala ngonyaka odlule, kanti lokhu kuzobangela ukuthi kube nokukhokhwa okweqile kwentela ye-PAYE, ngendlela engabangela ubunzima.
Lapho inkampani (umqashi) engakahloli inkokhelo yanyakenye (yonyaka odlule), kumele acele ihhovisi legatsha eliseduze labe-SARS ukuhlola inani lomholo okuthathwa ngokuthi umqondisi (udayirektha) walithola.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Kumele unikeze lolu lwazi olungezansi kwifomu lesicelo lapho wenza isicelo:
Unyaka wokuhlolwa kwentela okwamukelwa ngawo lowo mholo.
Inani lezinyanga eziphelile, lapho okwatholwa khona lowo mholo.
Isilinganiso noma inani langampela lomholo walo nyaka. (Lolu lwazi kumele lunikezwe uma isizathu sokucela umyalelo wentela kuwubunzima.)
Isicelo kumele sifakele izizathu zokucela umyalelo wentela. Lokhu kumele kusekelwe ngamadokhumende, isibonelo, izitatimende zezimali, kanye namaminithi emihlangano ebanjwa ngabaqondisi (ngodayirektha), akhombisa ukuthi inani lomholo okumele likhokhelwe abaqondisi, lizoba ngaphansi kwenani abakhokhelwa lona ngonyaka odlule.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Asikho isikhathi esibekiwe.

Izincazelo
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Nikeza lemininingwane elandelayo ukuze uncedise umnyango wezentela ukuthi wamukele isicelo sakho somyalo wokukhokha intela:
isitatimendi esikhombisa imali engenayo nemali ephumayo esijulile. Kungenzeka ukuthi umnyango wezentela ucele ubuvakazi bezindleko ezithile ngaphambi kokuvumela isicelo sakho somyalo wokukhokha intela indlela oyisebenzisile (noma edingekayo) ukuthi ubale imali yentela okufanele ukuthi uyikhokhe.
Uma isicelo sakho semukelekile uzakwaziswa ezinsukwini ezimbalwa, kodwa umyalo wokukhokha intela osemthethweni uzothatha amaviki amane ukufika kuwena.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kuzothatha amaviki amane ukusebenza isicelo sakho.

Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi

Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Lo myalelo unikezwa lapho kwenziwe isicelo sokuthi kubekwe inani elingaguquki okumele lidonswe lentela kumholo womkhokhi wentela. Umqashi kumele afake lesi sicelo egameni lomsebenzi.
Imiyalelo ayidluliselwa phambili kanti lapho umsebenzi eseshintshe isimo sakhe kwezomsebenzi, kumele kwenziwe isicelo esisha. Uma unabaqashi abangaphezu koyedwa, kumele kwenziwe isicelo somyalelo kumqashi nomqashi ngamunye.
Amakhophi ezincwadi zokuqashwa, izinkontilaki zemisebenzi kanye namanye amadokhumende afanele ayadingeka ukuqinisekisa imibandela nezimo zokuqashwa.
Umyalelo wamaphesente angaguquki azokhishelwa isikhathi esiyizinyanga ezingu-12, kanti futhi kumele uvuselelwe ngemuva kwazo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Qinisekisa ukuthi izindaba zakho zezentela zikwisimo esifanele lapho wenza isicelo somyalelo wentela. Abe-SARS ngeke bakhipha umyalelo wentela uma ukweleda intela.
Kwifomu lesicelo sakho somyalelo wentela, nikeza okulandelayo:
Fakela namadokhumende alandelayo kwisicelo sakho:
o ikhophi yekontraki yomsebenzi (lapho okunemininingwane yokusebenza kwakho kanye nalapho kugqanyiswe khona izimo)
Fakela isicelo sakho samaphesente angaguquki omyalelo ngesandla kwabehhovisi legatsha le-SARS, njengoba umsebenzi kumele afakele isitatimende semininingwane yomholo nezindleko.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Uma isicelo sesamukelwe ngabe-SARS, uzonikezwa isiqiniseko ezinsukwini ezimbalwa. Kodwa, umyalelo osemthethweni wentela ungathatha cishe amaviki amane.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Qinisekisa ukuthi izindaba zakho zezentela zikwisimo esifanele lapho wenza isicelo somyalelo wentela. Abe-SARS ngeke bakhipha umyalelo wentela uma ungafakelanga amafomu entela noma ukweleda intela.
Fakela okulandelayo ukusizana nabe-SARS ekwamukeleni isicelo sakho somyalelo wentela:
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Lapho abe-SARS sebevumele isicelo, uzonikezwa isiqiniseko ezinsukwini ezimbalwa, kodwa umyalelo wentela osemthethweni ungathatha cishe amaviki amane.
Indlela elula yokwenza isicelo somyalelo wentela, wukusenza nge-inthanethi kwi-website ethi: eFiling. Isicelo sakho somyalelo wentela ngale ndlela sivamise ukuvunyelwa kungakapheli amahora angu-48.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Ukungenisa izinto ezweni kanye nokuthumela izinto
Ukungenisa izinto ezweni kanye nokuthumela isicelo sokungenisa impahla ngaphakathi ezweni
Yenza isicelo sokungenisa impahla ngaphakathi ezweni
Izincazelo
Ngokulandela umthetho wokuphathwa kwezohwebo lwamazwe omhlaba we-International Trade Administration Act, 71 of 2002, uNgqongqoshe wezoHwebo neZimboni angathi lapho ebona kufanele noma kuyinto ewusizo (expedient) ngokubonelela isizwe, angathi ngokwenza inothisi kuGazethi kaHulumeni, abeke ukuthi akukho mpahla ngaphandle kwaleyo yeklasi ethize noma yohlobo oluthize engangeniswa ngaphakathi eNingizimu Afrika; noma engangeniswa ngaphakathi eNingizimu Afrika ngaphandle kwegunya le noma ngokulandela izimo ezibekwe kwimvume enikezwe nguye (ungqongqoshe) noma ngumuntu ogunyazwe nguye.
Izicelo zemvume zokungenisa impahla ngaphakathi, impahla engaphansi kolawulo lwezimpahla kumele ifakelwe kwifomu elibekiwe.
Inhloso yenkonzo wukuqinisekisa ukuthi impahla ethengiswa okwesibili engeniswayo, ngeke yacikela phansi imboni yeningizimu ne-Afrika i-Southern African Customs Union (SACU). Le nqubo iqinisekisa nokuthi kuba nolawulo lwempilo yesimo sendalo kanye nangezizathu zezokuvikeleka, ukuphepha kanye nokulandelwa kwamaqophelo, kanye nolawulo ngenhloso yokulandelwa kwezizathu zezendalo.
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Kumele kugcwaliswe amafomu esicelo ebese efakelwa.
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Asikho isikhathi esibekiwe (esinqunyiwe) sale nkonzo (sale sevisi).
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Ukudweba ngenhloso yohwebo
Ukufaka isicelo selungelo lokudoba ukuze uthengise
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ukudobela ukuthengisa kuwukusebenzisa ilungelo lakho lokudoba izinhlanzi. Lelilungelo kumele licelwe umuntu anikezwe lona ukuze alisebenzise, avune izinhlanzi olwandle noma asebenze umsebenzi ophathelene nokudoba ukuze athengise. Lifaka phakathi nokwenza umsebenzi ophathelene nokudoba kodwa ungazidli lezozinhlanzi. Ukuze usebenzise ilungelo lakho lokudoba, faka isicelo sephomedi yokudobela ukuthengisa ekhishwa ngungqongqoshe wegatsha eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha (Department of Environmental Affairs and Tourism) (DEA&T) noma isiphathimandla esikhethiwe. Ilungelo eligcwele noma elinqunyelwe liyanikezwa ezinhlobeni ezithile zezinhlanzi yigatsha lwakwa DEA&T ngokubheka inani elivunyelwe lokudotshwa kwezinhlanzi (TAC/E) enqunywa njalo ngomnyaka ngungqongqoshe wegatsha eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha (DEA&T). akekho umuntu ozovele ayodobela ukuthengisa ngaphandle kwephomedi
Uqala ngokufaka isicelo selungelo lokudobela ukuthengisa ngaphambi kokuthi ufake isicelo sephomedi yokudobela ukuthengisa. Lephomedi iyavuselelwa njalo ngomnyaka, kanti uma uhluleke ukuthobela lesimiso sephomedi, ingamiswa iphomedi yakho noma ihoxiswe noma ilungelo lakho lihoxiswe
Ngaphezu kwephomedi yokudobela ukuthengisa, uzodinga iphomedi yokuthutha izinhlanzi ukuze ukwazi ukuzithutha.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Faka isicelo selungelo lokudoba ngakuthi umenywe ukuthi wenze njalo nguhulumeni abhale encwadini yezaziso zikahulumeni.
Thola ifomu lokufaka isicelo egatsheni eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha (DEA&T) noma hamba uyolilanda lapho uzakutshelwa ukuthi ulilande khona ngabakwa DEA&T.
Fundisisa ifomu uqinisekise ukuthi uphetha zonke izimfanelo zesimiso zesicelo.
Gcwalisa, usayine ulithumele ifomu lakho namaphepha nezitifiketi ezidingekayo kanye nemali engabuyiselwa endaweni ozotshelwa yona noma ahhovisi lwakwa DEA&T eliseduze nawe.
umsebenzi wokuhluza uzoqala emuva kokudlula kosuku elibekiwe elijuba ukungena kwezicelo kanti imiphumela izomenyezelwa ngokubhalelana nabakwa DEA&T.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi isicelo selungelo lokudoba:
Isicelo sakho sizosetshenzwa izinyanga ezingu 1 kuya ku 3 noma ngaphezudlwana kuya ngokuthi umsebenzi ungakanani noma kuya ngokutholakala kwezinhlobo zezinhlanzi.
Ukufaka isicelo sephomedi yokudoba:
Isicelo sakho sizosetshenzwa izinsuku ezingu 7 noma ngaphezudlwana Kuya ngokuthi bangakanani abantu abafake izicelo.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Inhlobo yelungelo lokudoba
Imali ekhokhwayo ukudoba izinhlanzi
Njengokusho kukangqongqoshe noma isiphathimandla esikhethiwe ngokuya ngezinhlobo zezinhlanzi iphinde imenyezelwe uma kumemywa abantu ukuthi bafake izicelo zelungelo lokudoba ephepheni lezaziso zikahulumeni
R100 (lemali ingashintsha)
Ukudoba izimbaza ezimhlophe ukuzenza ibheyiti
R100 (lemali ingashintsha)
R200 (lemali ingashintsha)
Ukudoba iabalone enesisindo esingu 850kg noma ngaphansi
R300 (lemali ingashintsha)
Ukudoba iline fish usebenzisa abantu abane
Inhlabo yephomedi yokudoba izindleko zamanje
Ukwenza umsebenzi wokuhlola ophathelene nokudoba
Ukudoba noma okuphathelene nokudoba okwenziwa ngabantu abangebona base South Africa

Ukuthutha izinhlanzi
ukungenisa izinhlanzi ezivela kwamanye amazwe
Ukuthumela izinhlanzi ziye kwamanye amazwe
R150 kanye nemali yokufaka isicelo engu R75

Uhwebo kwezinhlanzi ukuzithokozisa
Ukufaka isicelo sephomedi yokudoba izinhlanzi kwamanye amazwe njengendlela yokuzilibazisa
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ukudobela ukuzilibazisa wokuthi abantu bahambe bayodoba izinhlanzi emanzini angewona ase South Africa bengaqonde ukuzithengisa. Ukuze ungenise izinhlanzi ezivela kwamanye amazwe uzingenisela ukuzilibazisa kuphela, kumele ufake isicelo sephomedi yokwenze njalo esikhishwa yigatsha eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha (DEA&T) noma isiphathimandla esikhethiwe. Izinhlanzi ezilandwe kwamanye amazwe okufanele zithengiswe kodwa kumele abantu bazidlele bona. Akekho umuntu ovunyelwe ukuthi angenise izinhlanzi zakwamanye amazwe ngaphandle kwephomedi yokwenze njalo. Faka isicelo esisha sephomedi yokudobela ukuzilibazisa emanzini angewona ase South Africa uma lena onayo isiphelelwe yisikhathi. Uma uhluleke ukuthobela lesimiso sephomedi, ingamiswa iphomedi yakho noma ihoxiswe.
Lemithetho elandelayo yokuthibela ukungenisa izinhlanzi ibekiwe:
Abantu abavunyelwe ukuthi beze nezinhlanzi eziphilayo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
o ikhophi enesitembu samaphoyisa yomazisi yowonke-wonke ofaka isicelo o ubufakazi bokuthi ukhokhile imali yokufaka isicelo ebhange (isilibhu sokufaka imali ebhange sika R120 umuntu amunye)
Qaphela:
Faka isicelo sakho kusenamaviki amabili usengakahambi ukuze ubanike isithuba sokulungisa amaphepha akho
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isicelo sakho sizosetshenzwa izinsuku ezingu 7 noma ngaphezudlwana uma amaphepha onke aphelele.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Ukufaka isicelo sephomedi yokudobela ukuzilibazisa emanzini angewona ase South Africa yokuthi abantu bazidlele nje ngu R120.
Imininingwane yase Bhange:
Izindleko zingakhushulwa njalo ngomnyaka noma ngokusho kuka ngqongqoshe wegatsha eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha (DEA&T) noma isiphathimandla esikhethiwe besebenzisana nongqongqoshe wezezimali.

Ukungenisa izinto ezweni kanye nokuthumela izinto
Isicelo seMvume yokuthumela izinto ngaphandle izinhlanzi kwamanye amazwe ngokuhweba
Ukufaka isicelo sokuhweba namanye amazwe ngezinhlanzi
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ukuhweba ngezinhlanzi wukwenza imisebenzi ephathelene nokudoda njengokuthengisela amanye amazwe izinhlanzi ngenhloso yokwenza inzuzo. Ukuhambisa izinhlanzi zasemanzini uzise kwamanye amazwe noma uzise ngaphandle kwemincele yase South Africa, kumele ufake isicelo sephomedi yokuthumela impahla ngaphandle kwaleli ekhishwa ngungqongqoshe wegatsha eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha noma isiphathimandla esikhethiwe. Akekho umuntu ovunyelwe ukuthi athumela izinhlanzi ngaphandle kwaleli ngaphandle kwephomedi yokwenze njalo. Faka isicelo esisha sephomedi uma lena onayo isiphelelwe yisikhathi emuva kwezinyanga ezintathu. Uma uhluleke ukuthobela lesimiso sephomedi, ingamiswa iphomedi yakho noma ihoxiswe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Faka isicelo Iphomedi yokuthumela impahla ngaphandle kwaleli.
Fundisisa ifomu uqinisekise ukuthi uphetha zonke izimfanelo zesimiso zesicelo.
Thumela ulwazi olulandelayo:
Uma lowo ofaka isicelo sokuthumela impahla ngaphandle kwaleli egeyena onelungelo lokudoba, akasinikeze lamaphepha alandelayo:
o ubufakazi bomthwalo kanye nekheli lalabo abazokunikeza izinhlanzi ozozithumela o igama elejwayelekile nelesayensi lenhlanzi o nenani lezinhlanzi ozozithumela.
Thumela amakhophi anesitembu samaphoyisa alamaphepha alandelayo:
Imali yokufaka isicelo ingu R225.
Gcwalisa, usayine uposele ifomu lakho namaphepha adingekayo kwabaphethe ezamanzi nesifunda sasogwini(Imininingwane yabo ibhaliwe ngaphansi) thumela amaphepha okuqala ngaphandle kwaloko iphomedi ingeke ikhishwe.
Amaphepha okuqala yiwona okumele uwathumele, ngaphandle kwaloko iphomedi ungeke uyithole.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isicelo sakho sizosetshenzwa izinsuku ezingu 7 noma ngaphezudlwana uma amaphepha onke aphelele.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Imali yokufaka isicelo ingu R225. Izindleko zingakhushulwa njalo ngomnyaka noma ngokusho kuka ngqongqoshe wegatsha eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha noma isiphathimandla esikhethiwe besebenzisana nongqongqoshe wezezimali.

Izincazelo
Ukudobela ukuzothengisa wokuthi abantu bahambe bayodoba izinhlanzi emanzini angewona ase South Africa beqonde ukuzothengisa. Ukuze ungenise izinhlanzi ezivela kwamanye amazwe uzingenisela ukuzothengisa, kumele ufake isicelo sephomedi yokwenze njalo ekhishwa yigatsha eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha noma isiphathimandla esikhethiwe. Akekho umuntu ovunyelwe ukuthi angenise izinhlanzi zakwamanye amazwe ngaphandle kwephomedi yokwenze njalo. Faka isicelo esisha sephomedi uma lena onayo isiphelelwe yisikhathi. Uma uhluleke ukuthobela lesimiso sephomedi, ingamiswa iphomedi yakho noma ihoxiswe.
Lemithetho elandelayo yokuthibela ukungenisa izinhlanzi ibekiwe:
Kuvumeleke Inkalankala ephelele esemakhazeni enesisindo asingu 600g ngasikhathi sinye noma umthwalo ungedluli kumathani angu 25 ngomnyaka.
Abantu abakavunyelwa ukungenisa inkalankala ephilayo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Fundisisa ifomu uqinisekise ukuthi uphetha zonke izimfanelo zesimiso zesicelo.
Thumela ulwazi olulandelayo:
o ikheli labakuthengiselayo ezweni lapho izinhlanzi ziphuma khona o igama elejwayelekile nelesayensi lenhlanzi o nenani lezinhlanzi ozozingenisa.
Thumela isicelo sakho kanye nalamaphepha alandelayo:
o ikhophi enesitembu samaphoyisa yomazisi wakho nenombholo yokubhalisa yenkampani yakho o ikhophi enesitembu samaphoyisa yephomedi yokungenisa impahla evela kwigatsha lezohwebo kanye nezimboni noma isaziso sephomedi.
Gcwalisa, usayine uposele ifomu lakho namaphepha adingekayo kwabaphethe ezamanzi nesifunda sasogwini.
Imininingwane yabo ibhaliwe ngaphansi thumela amaphepha okuqala ngaphandle kwaloko iphomedi ingeke ikhishwe.
Imali yokufaka isicelo ingu R225.
Phezulu

 (Umthetho kaZwelonke wezimila nezilwane ezehlukahlukene)

Ukuthuthukiswa kwezilwane nemfuyo
Isicelo sokwamukelwa ukuqoqa imbewu yesilisa yenku
Isicelo sokwamukelwa (sokuvunyelwa) ukuqoqa imbewu yesilisa yenkunzi yenkomo, inkunzi yengulube, noma inkunzi yehashi

Izincazelo
Isilwane singavunyelwa ukuthi sibe ngumnikeli (donor) wemetheriyali yegenethiki uma udokotela wezilwane eqinisile ukuthi leso silwane semukelekile maqondana:
nesimo sempilo ngokunabile ukungabibikho kwesifo iqophelo laso lembewu yesilisa ezinye izimpawu ezingachazwa (ezingafakelwa).
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Isicelo kumele senziwe egameni lesikhungo esibhalisiwe (esirejistiwe), lapho okuzobekwa khona isilwane.
Amadokhumende alandelayo kumele ahambisane nesicelo:
o amarekhodi okusebenza kwesilwane o inani lemali okumele ikhokhelwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Uma u-registrar ala (enqaba) ukuvumela isicelo, umenzi wesicelo u zokwaziswa ngkubhala ngesinqumo kanye nezizathu ezisekela leso sinqumo.
Uma u-registrar evumela isicelo ngaleso silwane okukhulunywa ngaso njengesizonikela, uzokhipha isitifikedi sokuvumela isilwane.
Phezulu
Izindleko

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Ezobudlelwane babaqashi nabasebenzi
Isicelo sokusungula ilearnersip uma ungumqashi
Isicelo sokusungula i-learnersip uma ungumqashi
Isimo
I-learnership ngenye indlela ehlukile yoqeqesho egxila kakhulu kwisipiliyoni esibambekayo.
Ama-learnership abandakanya izifundo ezenziwa emaklasini esikhungweni soqeqesho noma ekholishi kanye noqeqesho lwasemsebenzini ngesikhathi wenza umsebenzi.
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi, hamba uye kwaboMnyango wezemiSebenzi
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Thola omunye umuntu kwinhlangano yenu ukunikeza umkhombandlela kubafundi nokubasiza ngezinkinga abangahlangabezana nazo kwinhlangano.
Abaqashi bangakhetha abafundi phakathi kwabasebenzi babo noma bakhethe kubantu abangasebenzi ukuze bathole ama-learnership. Uma befuna abantu abangasebenzi ukwenza ama-learnership, bangaxhumana ne-labour centre (isikhungo sezemisebenzi) esiseduzane nabo ukuthola uhla lwabantu abangasebenzi ababhalise kuso. Asikho isidingo sokuthi baqashe laba bantu kulaba bafundi abangasebenzi ngemuva kwama-learnership.
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha cishe izinsuku ezingu-30 ukusungula i-learnership.
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe

Izincazelo
I-visa yimibhalo egxivizwe kwipasipoti esebenzayo yomuntu wangaphandle enikeza lowo muntu wangaphandle igunya lokungena eNingizimu Afrika.
itholakale ngaphambi kokuba umenzi wesicelo asuke ezweni lakubo ukuza eNingizimu Afrika itholakale ezweni langaphandle nakhona ngeke kwenziwa isicelo kumachweba/izikhumulo zokungena kumele kwenziwe isicelo sayo kwinxusa noma i-mission yeNingizimu Afrika emazweni lapho iNingizimu Afrika emelwe khona kwenziwe isicelo kwinxusa noma i-mission yeNingizimu Afrika eseduzane, uma iNingizimu Afrika ingamelwanga ezweni lakini. Lokhu kusebenza nakulabo abanamapasipoti ezikhulu zobunxuswa (zobudiplomatiki) abadinga izimvume zobudiplomatiki (zobunxuswa).
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo se-visa le-BI-84
ipasipoti yakho kumele isebenze isikhathi esingekho ngaphansi kwezinsuku ezingu-30 ngemuva kokuphela kwesikhathi sokuvakasha esihlongozwayo ipasipoti yakho kumele ibe nekhasi elilodwa elingasetshenziswanga, lapho uyiletha ukuthi izogxivizelwa imvume (for endorsement)
imali ebekiwe okumele ikhokhelwe, uma ikhona isitifikedi sokugonywa, uma lokhu kusebenza fakela isitatimende noma amadokhumende aqinisekisa inhloso yokuvakasha kwakho kanye nesikhathi sokuvakasha kwakho khipha ubufakazi bokuthi unemali eyenele khipha ubufakazi bethikithi elisebenzayo lendiza lokubuyela emuva kungenzeka kucelwe idiphozithi.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Le nkonzo (le sevisi) ithatha ukufinyelela kwizinsuku ezingu-10.

Izincazelo
lapho umthamo wentengiso yakho okumele ikhokhiswe intela ye-VAT wehla uba ngaphansi kuka-R300 000 ngonyaka lapho uyeka ibhizinisi, nakhona uzobe ungenzi ibhizinisi ezinyangeni ezingu-12 ngemuva kosuku lapho wenze isicelo sokubhalisa ukuqala ibhizinisi kodwa ungakaliqali lelo bhizinisi.
Kumele wazise abe-SARS ezinsukwini ezingu-21 zokuvalwa kwebhizinisi lakho.
Abe-SARS ngeke bakhansela ukubhalisa komuntu obebhalisile uma kukhona izizathu ezibambekayo zokukholelwa ukuthi uzoqala elinye ibhizinisi esikhathini sezinyange ezingu-12 ukusukela ngosuku lokuyeka kwakhe elinye ibhizinisi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thola amafomu okubhalisa kwi-inthanethi ngekhompyutha noma uye emahhovisi eSARS lapho obhalise khona ukwenza isicelo.
Thumela amafomu kwihhovisi le-SARS lapho obhalise khona.
Abe-SARS bazokunikeza inothisi ebhaliwe kumuntu owenze isicelo sokukhansela ukubhalisa noma ukumazisa ukuthi bayala (bayenqaba) ukukhansela ubhaliso (irestreshini).
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo

Khetha ulwimi
Ukubhalisa umkhumbi wokudweba kwabemikhumbi yamazwe angaphandle
Ukudweba ngenhloso yohwebo ukuhambisa noma ukuphatha ukudla ukuphatha ubisi kanye nokuluhambisa
Ukufunana nomcebo wamaminerali ibhizinisi lomsebenzi wezimayini
Ukuqhuba ibhizinisi lezimayini

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Izimvume namalayisense
Isicelo semvume noma selayisense ukuhambisa noma ukuphatha ukudla
Isicelo sesitifikedi sokwamukeleka ukuhambisa noma ukuphatha ukudla
Izincazelo
Noma yimuphi umuntu ofisa ukuba nendawo yokudla noma ukuhambisa (ukuthutha) ukudla kumele abe nesitifikedi sokwamukeleka. Lesi sitifikedi sikhishwa ngemuva kokuba izipikitela (abahloli) beziphathimandla zendawo behlole indawo noma imoto.
Ukuze uthole olunye ulwazi, ngena kwaboMnyango wezeMpilo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yiba nendawo efanele ezosetshenziswa kuphela ukuphatha ukudla noma imoto ezosetshenziswa kuphela ukuhambisa (ukuthutha) ukudla.
Gcina indawo noma imoto ihlanzekile ngazo zonke izikhathi, ukuqinisekisa ukuthi ukudla okuhanjiswa noma okuphathwayo kuleyo ndawo akoniwa zimpukane, ukungcola, nodoti, nokuxubana nezinye izinto ezingalungile noma ezinye izinto ezilimazayo.
Imoto noma indawo izohlolwa zipikitela (abahloli) beziphathimandla zendawo ukubona ukuthi ngabe ikufanele yini ukuhambisa noma ukuphatha ukudla
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Amazinga okwenziwa komsebenzi ancike kuzipikitela (kubahloli) beziphathimandla zendawo.
Izindleko
Inkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa, kodwa iziphathimandla zendawo zingashaja imadlana encane.
Amafomu atholakala mahhala kwaboMnyango kaZwelonke wezeMpilo. Ungawathola futhi lamafomu emahhovisi weziphathimandla zendawo.
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Izimvume namalayisense
Isicelo semvume noma selayisense ukuphatha ubisi kanye nokuluhambisa
Isicelo sesitifikedi sokwamukeleka ukuphatha ubisi kanye nokuluhambisa (ukuluthutha)
Izincazelo
Amashede obisi, yindawo lapho kusengwa khona nalapho kuphathwa khona ubisi ngendlela ehlanzekile. Isicelo sesitifikedi sokwamukeleka kweshede lokusengela ubisi, kumele senziwe ngokubhala. Isicelo kumele sibandakanye ulwazi okukhulunywa ngalo ngezansi.
Ukuze uthole olunye ulwazi, ngena kwabo Mnyango wezeMpilo.
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yenza isicelo esibhaliwe kwiziphathimandla zendawo endaweni lapho kuzosengelwa khona ubisi, noma lapho ishede izokwakhiwa khona.
Nikeza ngolwazi olulandelayo.
Igama lakho (wena menzi wesicelo), ikheli kanye nezinombolo zocingo
Isihloko esichaza indawo
Inani labasebenzi abaqashiwe, noma elizoqashwa
Inani lezilwane ezizosengwa
Iplani yendawo ekhombisa inyakatho, kanye nezakhiwo (amabhilidi) esondelene nawo, noma asemnceleni aleyo ndawo nokuthi asetshenziselwa ini
Isikeshi nokwendlaleka kwendawo ngesikali sika-1:100 kwazo zonke izindawo zeshede lokusengela ubisi, kanye neplani yomphansi wendawo, ukubonakala kwengxenye yendawo (sectional view) kanye nobude bokuya phezulu kwendawo (vertical elevation).
Zilungiselele ukunikeza noma ngabe yiluphi olunye ulwazi olungacelwa ziphathimandla zendawo.
Isipikitela (umhloli) weziphathimandla zendawo, uzokwenza lonke uhlolo lwendawo. Isipikitela (umhloli) uthumela umbiko kanye nesincomo kwiziphathimandla zendawo.
Kukhishwa isitifikedi sokwamukeleka uma ngabe umbiko wesipikitela (womhloli) umuhle.
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.
Isicelo sesitifikedi sokwamukeleka kweshede lokusengela. Lelifomu litholakala emahhovisi weziphathimandla zendawo noma kwaboMnyango wezeMpilo.

Isimo
Inhlangano ephethe (governing body) ingadinga ulunye ulwazi lokusekela isicelo, kanti futhi, ngaleyo nhloso, ingacela umenzi wesicelo ukuya kowodwa umhlangnao noma eminingana yenhlangano ephethe.

Izincazelo
Ongenisa impahla udinga isitifikedi sokuthutha utshwala obusemabhodleleni ukusuka echwebeni lokungena (endaweni yokungena) ukuzobuthengisa eNingizimu Afrika. Ongenisa impahla udinga isitifikedi kumthwalo nomthwalo (kwi-consignment) owodwa.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Isicelo kumele sihambisane namadokhumende alandelayo:
Isitifikedi sezwe utshwala obubuya kulo, unyaka wobudala babo, inhlanganisela yabo (composition), nenhlobo yalobo tshwala (Cultivar), okukhishwa yinhlangano esemthethweni yalelo lizwe obubuya kulo.
Phezulu

Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ngisho noma umthetho wezinkampani i-Companies Act, 1973, udinga ukuthi kube nemihlangano ephoqelekile yezinkampani, ikakhulukazi imihlangano kawonkewonke, asikho lesi sidingo kuma-CC. Imihlangano ivame ukubanjwa nje phakathi kwamalunga ngokubona kwabo noma kunini.
Asikho isidingo sokuthi onke amalunga e-CC adlale indima ebonakalayo njalo(active role) ekuqhubeni ibhizinisi. Kuma-CC amaningi, kuvame ukuthi amalunga abuye abengomininjela bebhizinisi.
Okubalulekile maqondana nokugcwaliswa kwefomu kufakelwe ngemuva kwekhasi 1 lefomu ye-CK1.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa kahle ngokucophelele ifomu le-CK1 noma ubhale kulo ngo-inki omnyama ngokusebenzisa amagama amakhulu.
Fakela amakhophi aqinisekisiwe amadokhumende omazisi wamalunga athintekayo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha isikhathi sosuku olulodwa ukuya kwezinsuku ezine ukubhalisa i-CC.

Izincazelo
Lomsebenzi wenzelwe abanikazi bezimoto abanemininingwane yemoto eshintshile. Lolushintsho lungaba yinombholo yenjini, noma inombholo yemoto. Umele ukuthi wazise igatsha lezothutho uma ushintsho olunjengalolu lwenzeka.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Vakashela ehhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Phezulu
Izindleko
Awukhokhelwa lomsebenzi.
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Awekho amafomu agcwaliswayo. Abasehhovisi eliphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni bazothatha imininingwane eshintshile yemoto entsha bayifake emabhukwini olwazi oluphathelene nezimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni kazwelonke i (NATIS).

Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
I-close corporation (CC) kungenzeka isikhule kakhulu kangangokuba ifuna ukuqhubeka nokukhula, kudingeka abathengi bamasheya ukuze bafakele enye imali kwi-CC.
Noma, mhlawumbi i-CC iqhudelana nezinkampani ezinkulu nakhona ifuna ukuba yinkampani ukuze ikwaze ikwazi ukwenza imisebenzi yayo iqhubeke kakhulu nokwazi ukuqhudelana kakhulu.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Uma igama le-CC lizoshintshwa, kudingeka ukuthi kugcwaliswe ifomu le-CM5.
Yenza amakhophi efomu le-CMI ukuya kwi-CM44C
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Uma kungekho madokhumende afakelwe ngokwehlukahlukana kunamadokhumende avela enkampani, kuzothatha izinsuku ezimbili ukuya kwezinhlanu zokusebenza ukushintsha (ukuguqula) i-close corporation ukuthi ibe yinkampani. Uma amadokhumende efakelwe ngokwehlukahlukana, kuzothatha amaviki amathathu ukuphethe ngenqubo.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Ngu-R50 ukubekelwa igama.

Isicelo semvume yabantu abasebenza emikhunjini
Isicelo semvume yabantu abasebenza emikhumbini

Izincazelo
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Fakela ipasipoti esebenzayo.
Khipha incwadi yesicelo ebuya kumnikazi womkhumbi, ebandakanya ukuzimisela kwakhe okwamukela emahlombe akhe lowo muntu ukulandela umthetho wababuya ngaphandle abangenayo i-Immigration Act, 2002.
Fakela ubufakazi obenele ngokuba nemali eyenele komnikazi walowo mkhumbi ukuhlangabezana nezidingo zosuku nosuku kanye nezindleko zokwelapha.
Khokha idiphozithi ebonwa ifanele ngabomnyango wezaseKhaya ukuze uqinisekise ukulandelwa kwemibandela nezimo zemvume yabasebenza emikhumbini. Leyo diphozithi iyobuyiselwa kuwe ngemuva kokuhamba kwabo kokugcina noma ngemuva kokuba imvume ikhishiwe wanikezwa yona. Ukukhokhwa kwediphozithi kungenziwa ngokukhokha ngesheke eligarantwe yibhangi (bank guaranteed cheque) noma nge-credit card ezindaweni lapha okukhona le fasilithi.
Fakela ubufakazi bokukhokha noma yiyiphi ifayini okungenzeka ikhokhwe ngummeli.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi

Ukufaka isicelo sokusula imoto emabhukwini
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Umnikazi wemoto angayesula emabhukwini imoto yakhe uma ingasasebenzisi imigwaqo yomphakathi noma isikhishiwe emgwaqweni.
Imoto engenalayisensi kuze kuphele iminyaka emine izokwesulwa, ihhovisi eliphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni lizokhipha isitifiketi sokuyesula.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu ehhovisi eliphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
Thumela lamaphepha alandelayo:
o ikhophi yomazisi enesitembu samaphoyisa o ikhophi enesitembu samaphoyisa efakaza ukuthi ungumnikazi wemoto o isitifiketi sokubhaliswa kwemoto.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha izinsuku ezimbili ukusebenza isicelo sakho.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Amafomu atholakala ehhovisi eliphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni noma kwabagunyazwe ukubhalisa izimoto.

Isicelo sokukhishwa kuhla lobhaliso noma ukuvala i
Izincazelo
Ukuvalwa (liquidation) kusho ukuthi i-CC
ayikwazi ukukhokhela izikweledu zayo;
ayisakwazi ukuqhubeka nemisebenzi yayo ngokuvamile, ngenxa yezinkinga zezimali; noma amalunga afuna ukwabelana ngempahla ye-CC.
I-CC ingavalwa ngenxa yenqubo yenkantolo (ecelwe ngabakweledwa yinkampani), noma kungenzeka ivalwe ngokuzikhethela (voluntary) lokhu (okusuke kucelwe ngamalunga e-CC). Uma amalunga e-CC enza isicelo sokuvala ngokuzikhethela kwawo, kudingeka kugcwaliswe ifomu lezokuvikeleka (le-JM12) elivela ku-Registrar of Companies (umbhalisi wezinkampani).
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gudluzela ifomu lokufaka isicelo, CK6.
Fakelani amakhophi amadokhumende omazisi awo onke amalunga e-CC.
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha isikhathi sokusukela kusuku olulodwa ukuya kwezimbili ukuphetha ngenqubo ye-deregistration (yokukhishwa kuhla lwamabhizinisi abahlisiwe)
Izindleko
Ngu-R40

Izincazelo
Ilayisensi yokushayela inikeza umuntu igunya lokushayela imoto. Iqukethe imininingwane yomazisi wakho njengomshayeli onelungelo lokushayela. Ukuze ufake isicelo selayisensi umele ukuthi ube neminyaka engu 18 noma ngaphezulu.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
o ilayisensi yokufundela ukushayela o incwadi yakho yomazisi noma ipasipoti o izithombe ezingezona zombala zencwadi yomazisi ezine.
Uzothathwa iminwe uma ufika esenta.
Amehlo akho azohlolwa esenta ngaphambi kokuba usuku lokuvivinywa lubekwe. (Lubhale phansi lolosuku onikezwe lona).
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Phezulu

Ukufaka isicelo sokubhalisa njengomfundisi wokushayela
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe

Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi

Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe

Izincazelo
Izimiselo eziphathelene nezimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni zikazwelonke zithi umuntu ofuna ukuba ngumfundisi wokushayela kumele abhaliswe negatsha lezokuthutha. Ukuze ube ngumfundisi wokushayela umele ukuthi ufundiswe kwawena uphumelele ekuhlolweni kwakho. Umuntu ofuna ukuba ngumfundisi wokushayela uzosebenza ngesigaba sezimoto anelayisensi yokuzishayela. Isitifiketi somfundisi wokushayela siyaphela emuva komnyaka.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu RI, lokufaka isicelo sokuba ngumfundisi wokushayela.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Uzothunyelwa esiteshini samaphoyisa uyothola umbiko wamacala.
Isenta izothumela isicelo sakho kwilungu lesigungu sekhansela ukuze ivunywe noma yenqatshelwe, uma ivunyiwe, isenta izokhipha iphomede.
Phezulu

Izincazelo
Isakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika esifuna ukuba nesimo sokuba yisakhamuzi sakwelinye izwe kumele senze isicelo kwaboMnyango wezaseKhaya ukuze sikwazi ukuqhubeka nokuba yisakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika kodwa sibe sithola isimo sokuba yisakhamuzi sakwelinye izwe. Ukuba nesimo sokuba yisakhamuzi samazwe amabili kusho ukuthi umuntu othathwa njengasakhamuzi sawo womabili amazwe.
Isimo sokuba yasakhamuzi samazwe amabili sinikezwa abantu abangaphezu kweminyaka engu-21.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo le-BI-1664 kanye ne-BI-529 kunoma yiliphi ihhovisi lezaseKhaya eNingizimu Afrika.

Isicelo sokwenzelwa iduplikhethi yedokhumende yela
Ukufaka isicelo samaphepha afana nalawo awokuqala emoto

Izincazelo
Ihhovisi eliphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni lizokhipha amaphepha afana nalawo awokuqala emoto, njengesitifiketi sokubhalisa, isitifiketi esigunyaza ukuthi imoto ihambe amgwaqweni, iphepha eliyindingilizi lelayisensi, noma ngayimaphi amanye amaphepha alahlekileyo, awonakele, noma okubhaliwe kungasabonakali.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thintana nehhovisi eliphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Phezulu
Izindleko
Izindleko zibekwe ngamalungu esigungu sekhansela kanti ziyashiyana ngezifundazwe.
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Amafomu okufaka isicelo samaphepha afana nalawo awokuqala emoto (CDV), atholakala ehhovisi eliphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni.

Izincazelo
Le nkonzo (le sevisi) yenza ukuthi umuntu siqu, isikhungo noma inhlangano zikwazi ukusebenzisana nabophiki lwezimali ezingenayo abe-South African Revenue Service (SARS) ngokusebenzisa itheknoloji echaziwe. Inqubo entsha yeNtela yeNgeniso, yenzelwe ukuphungula ukusetshenziswa kwamaphepha lapho kukhonakala khona, kanye nokwenza ukuthi izinto eziningi ezenziwa ngendlela ye-manual zihambe ngendlela yemishini (authomate), kodwa kube kuqinisekiswa ukuthi bonke abakhokhe bentela baphathwa ngendlela elinganayo. Kusungulwe izindlela eziningi zokusebenzisa inqubo ye-elektroniki.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Bhalisa imininingwane elandelayo enikezwa ngabe-SARS ngemuva kobhaliso (kwerejistreshini):

Izincazelo
Okwamanje kukhona inqubo yokulandelayo yokufakela amafomu entela:
Abakhokhi bentela banikezwa ithuba lokwamukela, ukugcwalisa, ukufakela kanye nokukhokha intela ngendlela evikelekile ye-gateway kumahora angu-24 nsuku zonke.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Inqubo ye-e-filing izokhipha izikhumbuzo kumkhokhi wentela, ngokuthumela i-SMS noma nge-email, ukumazisa ukuthi ifomu lakhe selilungile ukuligcwalisa noma ukulibuyisela.
Umkhokhi wentela uzosebenzisa ikhodi yakhe yokungena eyimfihlo kanye ne-password ukuthola amafomu entela.
Ukwenza isicelo nge-website kuzovele kukhombise lonke ulwazi maqondana namafomu njengokuvamile nakwifomu eliprintiwe. Umkhokhi wentela uzobe esegcwalisa ifomu kwi-website.
Uma umkhokhi wentela esegcwalise ifomu, ulwazi olufakela kwifomu luzoqinisekiswa ngokugcwele. Konke ukubalwa kwenziwa yinqubo ukuze kungabi namaphutha kulwazi olufakelwayo.
Umkhokhi wentela angakhetha ukukhokha ngesikhathi egcwalisa ifomu noma angakhokha ngaphambili kosuku olufanele.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Ezobudlelwane babaqashi nabasebenzi
Ezobudlelwane babaqashi nabasebenzi
Cela ikomiti ebalulekile ukubona inkonzo ebalulek
Ukucela ikomiti lezinkonzo ezibalulekile (lamasevisi) abalulekile ukwenza uphenyisiso ukubona ukuthi yonke inkonzo noma ingxenye yayo iyinkozo ebalulekile
Isimo
Inkonzo (isevisi) ebalulekile, yinkonzo okuthi uma iphazamiseka, lokhu kufakele impilo engozini noma kuphazamise impilo yabantu.
Ngesikhathi sesiteleka, noma yiluphi uhlangothi oluyingxenye yempikiswano ngokunye kwezinto ezilandelayo, lungathi ngokubhala ludlulisele impikiswano kukomidi lezinkonzo ezibalulekile:
ngisho noma inkonzo ingabalulekile noma cha;
ngisho noma umsebenzi noma umqashi ukwinkonzo ebekwe njengebalulekile.
Uhlangothi oluhambisa impikiswano kwikomidi lezinkonzo ezibalulekile, kumele lenze isiqiniseko sokuthi ikhophi yesicelo esihanjisiwe inikezwa nezinye izinhlangothi ezithintekayo kwimpikiswano. Ikomidi lezinkonzo ezibalulekile (la masevisi) abalulekile kumele libheke impikiswano masishane ngendlela okungakhonakala ngayo.
Izinkonzo (amasevisi) ePhalamende, Inkonzo yamaPhoyisa aseNingizimu Afrika kanye nawezeMpilo ngabalulekile.
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi, hamba uye kwaboMnyango wezemiSebenzi.
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Fakela ikhophi yesitifikedi sesigunyaziso esikhona kwifomu lesicelo.
Nikeza uhlangothi oluthintekayo ikhophi yaleli fomu.
Nikeza ubufakazi bokuthi ikhophi yaleli fomu inikezelwe kolunye uhlangothi, ngokubufakela:
Ikhophi lesilipi sokurejista ePosini; noma
Ikhophi lesiliphi esisayiniwe uma ifomu lihanjiswe ngesandla; noma o isitatimende esisayinelwe esivela kumuntu odilive ifomu ukuqinisekisa ukuthi lokhu kwenziwe; noma

Imvume yokusebenza kumakhono angavamile
Isicelo semvume yokusebenza kumakhono angavamile
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Imvume yokusebenza kumakhono angavamile inikezwa kumuntu wangaphandle onamakhono angavamile noma amakhwalifikheshini (amakhono afundelwe). Inganikezswa nakumalunga omndeni walowo muntu. Izicelo zingenziwa naku-embassy (indlu yenxusa) noma i-mission yeNingizimu Afrika.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu le-BI-1738.
Fakela ipasipoti yakho okumele isebenze isikhathi esingekho ngaphansi kwezinsuku ezingu-30 ngemuva kokuphela kwesikhathi sokuvakasha.
Ubufakazi bokuba nemali eyenele ngokufakela izitatimende zebhangi, iziliphi zemiholo, kanye nesiqinisekiso kwabazokwamukela eNingizimu Afrika, amabhasari, imali yezokwelaphiwa, noma imali engukheshi uma ikhona, okubandakanya ama-credit card noma amasheke asetshenziswa ekuhambeni (traveller's cheques) ukubhekana nezindleko zempilo ngesikhathi uhlala eNingizimu Afrika.
Isitifikedi sokugonywa, uma sidingeka.
Khipha incwadi ebuya kumkhakha kahulumeni wangaphandle, noma umkhakha kahulumeni waseNingizimu Afrika, noma obuya kwisikhungo sezemfundo eNingizimu Afrika noma kwabezamasiko noma inhlangano yezebhizinisi, eqinisa uuthi amakhono akho noma amakhwalifikheshini akho angavamile.
Nikeza noma yibuphi ubufakazi obubambekayo obukhombisa ukuthi amakhono noma amakhwalifikheshini angavamile, afana nezincwadi ezike zashicilelwa noma ama-testimonial.
Cela umqashi ofuna ukukuqasha ukuthi agcwalise isigaba sika-13 , no- kwifomu le-BI-1738.
Phezulu

Izinkonzo Kubantu
Ezokuthutha nokuhamba
Ukushayela nokufunda ukushayela
Isicelo sokushintsha ilayisense yokushayela yangap
Ukufaka isicelo sokushintsha ilayisensi etholakale  kwamanye amazwe
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Amalayisensi okushayela akhishwe ngamanye amazwe avunywa eMzansi azoshintshwa abe ngamakhadi uma abawaphethe befaka isicelo selayisensi yaseMzansi ngokulandela umthetho wezinqola ezihamba emgwaqweni ka 1996.
Isicelo sokushintsha iphomede yakwamanye amazwe kumele iphelezelwe yilayisensi yokushayela ekhishwe yilelozwe.
Ilayisensi yakwamanye amazwe noma iphomede yakwamanye amazwe ingashintshwa ukuze ibe ngeyaseMzansi uma ofaka isicelo eyisakhamuzi sakuleli noma enephomede yokuhlala njalo (loku akubafaki abanamaphomede obunxusa noma ezivumelwano ezithile).
Ilayisensi yankulelinye izwe kumele ibe ngesebenzayo ngosuku ishintshwa kanti futhi kumele ibe ibhalwe ngolunye lwamalimi asemthethweni akuleli. Uma kungenjalo isicelo kumele size nehumusho oluvela kumuntu owaziyo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
o umazisi omukelekile eMzansi, njengomazisi, umazisi owokwesikhashana, ipasipoti yaseMzansi, noma ipasipoti yakwelinye izwe ihamba nephomede yokuhlala eMzansi o incwadi evela kusiphathimandla esaziwayo ifakazela ukuthi lelayisensi yakwelinye izwe iyasebenza, lencwadi kumele isho isigaba selayisensi (inhlobo yemoto lowomuntu angayishayela)
o ilayisensi yakwelinye izwe o izithombe ezingezona zombala zencwadi yomazisi ezine khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Uzohlolwa amehlo ngaphambi kokuba ilayisensi yakho ishintshwe.
Isaziso: ungeke udinge ukuthi ubhale uhlolo lwelayisensi yokushayela. Uzonikezwa ilayisensi yokushayela okwesikhashana ezophela emuva kwezinyanga eziyisithupha ngaphandle kokukhokha.

Ukuthuthela eNingizimu Afrika noma ukuvakashela
Ilungelo lokuhlala isikhashana
Isicelo semvume yohlelo lokushintshisana
Isicelo semvume yohlelo lokushintshisana kwezamasiko/ezomnotho/kanye nezenhlalisano yabantu
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Imvume yokushintshisana ingakhishwa kumuntu wangaphandle obamba iqhaza kwezokushintshisana kuhlelo lwezamasiko, ezonomnotho nezenhlalisano yabantu, olugqugquzelelwa noma oluphethwe yinhlangano yangaphandle noma umkhakha wumbuso, noma umkhakha wesikhungo semfundo ephakeme ngokuhambisana nomkhakha kahulumeni wangaphandle. Yenza isicelo semvume yokushintshisana kwinxusa (embassy) yaseNingizimu Afrika noma kwi-mission eseduzane nawe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu le-BI-1738.
Fakela ipasipoti yakho kanye nesicelo.
Khipha incwadi ye-entity, yenhlangano, noma umndeni lapho ofuna ukuyokwenza khona uhlelo eNingizimu Afrika, eqinisa isimo noma ubukhona balolo hlelo lokushintshisana. Incwadi kumele ibe wubufakazi bokuthi ukufanele ukubhalisela (ukungenela) noma ukubamba iqhaza kuhlelo, kanti futhi kumele kukhombise ukuthi i-entity, inhlangano noma umndeni uzobhekana nawe ngesikhathi uhlezi eNingizimu Afrika.
Ukuthola incwadi evela kumkhakha kahulumeni ofanele noma ovela kwinhlangano esekelayo (sponsoring body) ezweni lakini, eqinisa isimo noma ubukhona bohlelo lokushintshisana neNingizimu Afrika kanye neqiniso lokuthi wamukelwe ukubhalisa kuhlelo.
Phezulu

Imfundo
Imvume yohlelo lokushintshisana kwabafundi
Isicelo semvume yohlelo lokushintshisana kwabafundi (kwezitshudeni)
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Imvume yohlelo lokushintshisana kwezitshudeni (kwabafundi), yimvume ekhishwa kumfundi (kwisitshudeni) sangaphandle kwezwe okumele sizongenela kwiprojekthi ethile noma uhlelo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Fakela incwadi evela kuMnyango evela kuMnyango wezeMfundo noma kwisikhungo semfundo ephakeme eNingizimu Afrika esenza okulandelayo:
o esiqinisekisa ukuthi isikhungo yisona esibhekene nokugqugquzela noma ukuhambisa uhlelo o esichaza imisebenzi kanye nobude besikhathi sohlelo o esiqinisekisa ukuthi isikhungo sizobhekana nakho konke okuqondene nalowo mfundi (naleso sitshudeni) ngesikhathi siseRiphabhliki nokuthi isitshudeni samukelwe ukuze sibhaliswe (sirejistwe).
Fakela incwadi evela evela kwinhlangano yezwe langaphandle ezokwenza okulandelayo:
o ezoqinisa imininingwane yomfundi, ebandakanya ubhaliso (irejistreshini) yesitshudeni kanye nesikhungo sezemfundo ephakeme ngaphandle o esiqinisa usuku okuyosungulwa ngalo uhlelo eNingizimu Afrika.

Ukuthuthukiswa kwezilwane nemfuyo

Isicelo sokuthumela ngaphandle kwezwe izilwane ama-embryo (imibungwane ekhula ibe zilwane) ama-ova (amaqanda ensikazi) kanye ne-semen (imbewu yesilisa)

Izincazelo
Imvume yokuthumela ngaphandle kwezwe evela ku-Registrar of Animals Improvement (umbhalisi wokuthuthukiswa kwezilwane) iyadingeka ngaphambi kokuba uthumele izilwane ngaphandle kwezwe noma imetheriyali yezofuzo (yejenethiki) efana nama-embryo, ama-ova, noma i-semen ethunyelwa ngaphandle isuka ngaphakathi eNingizimu Afrika. Amasosayathi okuzalisa izilwane afanele, noma iziphathimandla zobhaliso, kumele banikeze isincomo sokuthumela ngaphandle kwezwe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Izicelo ezingaphelele zizobuyiselwa kumenzi wesicelo.
Fakela uhla olukhombisa ulwazi maqondana nabanikezeli bama-embryo, ama-ova, noma ama-semen ezilwane ezizongeniswa ngaphakathi maqondana nesigaba C kwifomu lesicelo.

Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Abe-EIMA bahlinzeka abathumela izimpahla ngaphandle kwezwe ngosizo lwezimali ukuze bakwazi ukuyobonisa ngemikhiqizo kanye nezinkonzo (namasevisi) abo kumazwe angaphandle kanye nokubasiza ngenqubo yokubameshanisa ukuthola ukuthunyelwa kwezimpahla ngaphandle kanye nokutshalwa kwezimali zangaphandle ngokuqonde ngqo ezweni. Abe-EIMA babuye basize ngokunciphisa izindleko zokuthumela impahla ngaphandle.
Le nkonzo (le sevisi) iqondiswe kumaklayenti alandelayo:
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Inyathelo lokuqala: Sebenzisa ibhukwana lemikhombandlela
Amafomu esicelo kanye namabhukwana emikhombandlela yakhona kumele kufundwe ngokuhambisana kwako. Isicelo siyophumelela kuphela uma sihambisana nezidingo ezibekwe kumaBhukwana emiKhombandlela ("Guidelines Booklets").
Funda la madokhumende ngokucophelela ukuze uqondisise imitheshwana yolawulo (rules) kanye nezinqubo.
Inyathelo lesibili: Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo
Uhla oluphelele lwamadokhumende okumele afakelwe kanye nesicelo kunikeziwe. Amabhukwana emikhombandlela azokhombisa, uma kukhona amanye amadokhumende adingekayo.
Isinyathelo sesithathu: Fakela isicelo
Amabhizinisi avunyelwe ukufakela izicelo ezifinyelela kweziyisithupha ngonyaka, kodwa kufakelwa kuphela ezine kwisikimu esisodwa. Noma yini okunye okuhlukile kulokhu, kumele kugunyaziswe nguMininjela ophezulu (Senior Manager) we-EMIA.
Amafomu esicelo kumele agcwaliswe kahle nakhona afakelwe ngesikhathi esibekiwe kwisevisi nesevisi ye-EMIA.
Izicelo ezifakelwa ngemuva kwesikhathi, ngeke zabonelelwa.
Inyathelo lesine: Ukubonelelwa kwesicelo
Uma isicelo sakho siphelele futhi sihambisana nawo onke amadokhumende asekelayo kanyenokulandela imibandela yokwamukelwa, singabonelelwa ukuze sivunyelwe.
Isinyathelo sesihlanu: Ukuvunyelwa noma ukwalelwa kwesicelo
Abe-EMIA bazokwazisa ngokuthi ngabe isicelo sako sivunyelwe yini noma salelwe kumahora angu-48 ngemuva kokwenziwa kwesinqumo sokugcina.
Izicelo ngeke zavunyelwa ngomlomo. Kumele uqinisekise ukuthi uthola ukuvunywa kwesicelo sakho okubhalwe phansi ngaphambi kokuba uhambe.
Ikhophi yesazizo sokuvunyelwa kwesicelo kumele kuhambisane nakho konke ukuxhumana kanye/noma ama-submission amanye amadokhumende kwabe-EMIA. Uma abe-EMIA bethola ezinye izincwadi zokuxhumana nawe noma amadokhumende, angabandakanyi incwadi yokuvunyelwa kwesicelo, abe-EMIA, ngeke babekwa icala uma la madokhumende elahleka noma efakelwa kwenye indawo engafanele. Uma la madokhumende elahleka noma ebekwa kwenye indawo engafanele, lokhu kungaphazamisa noma ukulibazisa inqubo yezamahhovisi.
Uma isicelo sosizo sesivunyelwe, kumele kugcwaliswe ifomu lokukleyima le-EMIA, kanye namadokhumende asekelayo kanye nohla lwemibuzo (questionnaire) okumele kugcwaliswe ebese kufakelwa kwabe-EMIA kungakapheli izinyanga ezintathu ukusukela ngosuku okumele kubuyiswe ngalo ukusukela kwindawo yokwenziwa kwebhizinisi elivunyelwe.
Uma ama-ayithemu noma amadokhumende maqondana nekleyimi engekho ngemuva kwezinyanga ezintathu, ikleyimu ngeke isavunyelwa.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo

Ukungenisa izinto ezweni kanye nokuthumela izinto
Isicelo seMvume yokuthumela izinto ngaphandle
Isicelo seMvume yokuthumela izinto ngaphandle
Isicelo seMvume yokuthumela izinto ngaphandle
Izincazelo
Imvume yokuthumela impahla ngaphandle idingeka ukuqinisekisa ukuthi umuntu siqu noma inhlangano enenhloso yokuthumela izimpahla ngaphandle ilandela imibandela ethize ebekiwe kanye nokulandela imibandela yezivumelwano zamazwe omhlaba. Izivume zokuthumela impahla ngaphandle zisiza ukulawula ukuthi impahla ephumayo ingeyohlobo olubalulekile nakhona ukuthi kungashushumbisiwa impahla kanye nempahla eyebiwe.
Akuyizo zonke izimpahla (goods) noma imikhiqizo (ama-product) angeniswayo noma athunyelwa ngaphandle adinga inqubo yolawulo. Kodwa-ke zonke izinto ezisetshenziselwa impahla noma udoti, kanye nezikrebhe kungaphansi kwenqubo yolawulo longenisa impahla (import control measures). Uhla lwempahla engaphansi kolawulo lwezimpahla ezingeniswayo kanye nezithunyelwayo luyatholakala. Uma ufakele imininingwane yokuxhumana nawe, ungathunyelelwa uhla ngeposi, ngefekisi noma nge-email.

 (Umthetho wokuphathwa kwezohwebo namazwe omhlaba)

Ukuthuthela eNingizimu Afrika noma ukuvakashela
Isicelo sokunweba noma ukushintsha imvume yokusebe
Isicelo sokunweba noma ukushintsha imvume yokusebenza yomsebenzi oqashiwe

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Le nkonzo (le sevisi) ngeyabantu abanemvume abafuna ukunweba isikhathi noma ukushintsha imvume yabo yokusebenza. Isicelo sokunweba noma ukushintsha kumele senziwe emavikini ayisishagalombili ngaphambi kokuba kuphele isikhathi semvume. Izimvume eseziphelelwe yisikhathi ngeke zanwetshelwa isikhathi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe gcwalisa ifomu le-BI-159. Hamba uye kunoma yiliphi ihhovisi lezaseKhaya (leZangaphakathi). Yenza isicelo kwinxusa (embassy) noma i-mission uma ungaphandle kwezwe abaqashi kumele bagcwalise kuma-ayithemu ka-6 no-6.1 wefomu lesicelo abaqashi kumele basayine isifakazelo (declaration) kanye nokufakela isitembu senkampani uma kungenasitembu, ukuqashwa komuntu kumele kuqiniswe ngokubhala incwadi eceleni kwi-letterhead yenkampani fakela isitifikedi samaphoyisa sokuba msulwa (uma sasingafakelwanga lapho kwenziwa isicelo sokuqala)
fakela ikhophi yekontraki yomsebenzi esemthethweni uma kusebenza, fakela ubufakazi bobhaliso (berejistreshini) nenhlangano yezeprofeshini efanele.
fakela into ekhombisa amaplani angekusasa omenzi wesicelo fakela isikhangiso (i-advethi) yesikhundla uma kuzongezelwa isikhathi sekontraki yakuqala yomsebenzi fakela umbiko wenqubekela phambili ngokuthi ngabe izakhamizi zaseNingizimu Afrika/ abanelungelo lokuhlala unomphela, baqeqeshiwe yini noma bayaqeqeshwa.
Phezulu

Isicelo selayisense entsha yesibhamu
Isicelo sokuvuselela ilayisense yokuba nesibhamu
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe

Izincazelo
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Ungathi ngesikhathi esifanayo wenze isicelo sokuvuselela ilayisense (amalayisense) ekhona (akhona) esibhamu (ezibhamu). Kusebenza izikhathi ezilandelayo zokuvuselela ngokulandela izinsuku zokuzalwa:
Usuku lokuzalwa
Isikhathi sokuvuselela
o ilayisense (amalayisense) esibhamu, imvume noma isigunyaziso sesibhamu (sezibhamu) ofisa ukuzivuselela o isitifikedi sakuqala soqeqesho owasithola kumqeqeshi ogunyaziwe o isitifikedi sakuqala sekhona (uma sesakhishwa)
o izithombe zosayizi wepasipoti ezicace kahle nezikhombisa ubuwena zombala (unobscured passport size photographs), ezingezindala ngokweqe ngezinyanga ezinthathu (kudingeka izithombe ezimbili kuwo wonke amalayisense esibhamu ofisa ukuwavuselela kanye nezimbili zesitifikedi sekhono).
Uzothola irisidi yokwamukela isicelo sakho ye-SAPS 523 njengobufakazi bokuthi ufakele isicelo sokuvuselela ilayisense (amalayisense) yesibhamu (yezibhamu).
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
U-DFO uzokwenza uhlolo lophenyisiso ngawe, ukuthola ukuthi ngabe ungumuntu okufanele nokulungele yini ukuba nesibhamu. U-DFO uzozohlola indawo yakho, ukuhlola ukuthi ngabe uthathe izinyathelo ezifanele kwezokuphepha endaweni noma eduzane nendawo yakho kanye nesisefo sesibhamu, ukuqinisekisa ukuthi ngabe uyawalandela amazinga abakwa-South African Bureau of Standards (SABS). Uma wathola ilayisense yesibhamu ngaphambi komhlaka 1 Julayi 2004, nakhona kufanele uvuselele ilayisense, asikho isidingo sokuthenga isisefo esisha. Izidingo ezikhona manje ngokulandela umtheshwana wenqubo we-Arms and Ammunition Regulations, 1994, zithathwa ngokuthi zamukelekile. Uma isisefo singahlangabezani nezidingo, kumele uthenge isisefo esisha, esilandela amazinga abekwe abakwa-SABS.
Uma isicelo sakho (izicelo zakho) zivunyiwe, uDFO uzoxhumana nawe siqu ukuzolanda isitifikedi sekhona (uma singakakhishwa) kanye nelayisense (amalayisense) esibhamu (ezibhamu).
Phezulu
Izindleko
Isitifikedi seKhono:R70
Kwilayisense ngayinye:R70

Izincazelo
Abenzi bezicelo abafisa ukubhalisa kungenzeka kudingeke ukuthi bafezekise imibandela ethize okungenzeka ibe ngaphambi kobhaliso noma ngemuva kobhaliso. Lokhu kubandakanya nemibandela ekhonjiswe kwisichibiyelo somthethosisekelo wama-Asosiyesheni.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu ebese ulifakela ehhovisi labe-DWAF eliseduzane nawe.
Isicelo sakho sizohlolwa kanti uma samukelwe, abomnyango bazacela ukuthi ugcwalise i-Form 2: Isicelo sobhaliso ye-asosiyesheni yokuvimbela imililo.
Gcwalisa i-Form 2 ebese ulifakela kwaboMnyango wezaManzi namaHlathi eliseduzane nawe
Isicelo sakho, sizohlolwa futhi, kanti uma samukelwe, abomnyango bazokhipha isitifikedi sobhaliso.
Isicelo sakho sobhaliso kumele sibe nolwazi olulandelayo:
o imininingwane ye-asosiyesheni yokuvimbela imililo eyenza isicelo o imininingwane yomuntu oqala inqubo yobhaliso lwe-asosiyesheni yokuvimbela imililo eyenza isicelo sokubhaliswa o isiqinisekiso sokuthi akukho mnikazi oye akangabandakanywa ngamabomu emihlanganweni noma ezingxoxweni maqondana nolwazi lwe-asosiyesheni yezivimbela mililo o imininingwane ngendawo i-asosiyesheni yezivimbela mililo eyenza isicelo enegunya kuyo o isiqinisekiso sokuthi ayikho enye i-asosiyesheni yezivimbela mililo enenhloso yokusungula lapho noma ekhona kuleyo ndawo lapho le asosiyesheni eyenza isicelo sakhona o isiqinisekiso somuntu osungula ukubunjwa kwe-asosiyesheni yokuvimbela imililo o isincomo ngofanele omele uhulumeni wendawo o isincomo ngomele ihhovisi lesifunda lobomnyango wezamanzi namahlathi o isiqinisekiso ngomqondisi ophezulu wesifunda waboMnyango wezaManzi namaHlathi.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Izimvume namalayisense
Isicelo semvume noma selayisense kwabemikhumbi yamazwe angaphandle
Ukufaka isicelo selayisensi yomkhumbi wangaphandle wokudoba
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Awukho umkhumbi wangaphandle ozonikezwa ilayisensi yokudoba yase South Africa ngamphandle uma usebenzisana ngokubambisana nomuntu onelungelo wase South Africa bedoba. Kumele bathintane negatsha eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha bengakaqalisi ukudoba ukuze kutholisiswe ukuthi ukuqasha umkhumbi wangaphandle kuvumelekile yini enhlobeni ethize yezinhlanzi nokuthi maphepha mani adingekayo ukuze imvume inikezwe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Uma ngokomgomo kwemukeleka ukuthi kusetshenziswe umkhumbi wangaphandle, ofaka isicelo kumele athole ifomu lokucela ukusebenzisa umkhumbi wangaphandle ehhovisi eliseduze naye lakwa DEA&T.
o amandla omkhumbi o ubude nokuthi uthwala kangakanani o inombholo obhaliswe ngayo umkhumbi o izwe obhaliswe kulona umkhumbi (uma uqashwe noma uhlanganyelwe nomuntu wase South Africa)
o isitifiketi sokuphepha esivela kulelozwe.
Yonke imikhumbi yangaphandle kumele ibe nomshini osebenzayo okuthiwa yi INMARSAT CVMS othintana nabaphethe ezamanzi olwandle. Kumele ufakazelwe yilabo abaphethe ezamanzi olwandle.
Esicelweni sakho, yisho ukuthi nguyiphi isekisheni yokudoba ozosebenza kuyona nokuthi uzodoba Isikhathi esingakanani.
kuzodingeka incwadi esemthethweni evela kwabaphethe ezokudoba kulelizwe umkhumbi umbethe ifulegi yakhona ivuma ukuthi:
Uma umkhumbi ungewona okhethiweyo ngokuya ngomthetho welungelo, ofaka isicelo kumele agcwalise ifomu lokugudluzela lomkhumbi kwenye indawo.
Ukuze umkhumbi unikezwe ilungelo lokudoba, ofaka isicelo kumele agcwalise ifomu lephomedi ehambelana nesekisheni yokudoba lomkhumbi ozodoba kuyo. Okokugcina, uma ofaka isicelo acabanga ukuthi lomkhumbi uzodoba olwandle olunamagagasi aphezulu, kumele agcwalise ifomu lokudoba endaweni enjalo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isicelo sokusebenzisa umkhumbi wangaphandle (kanye nezicelo zokugudluzela umkhumbi, ilayisensi yokudoba olwandle olunamagagasi aphezulu) kungathatha izinsuku ezingu 14 uma onke amaphepha ephelele.
Phezulu
Izindleko
R21 200- yelayisensi yangaphandle
R1 695 - yelayisensi yolwandle olunamagagasi aphezulu
R790 - yephomedi yokudoba

Izincazelo
Inothisi yokuzalwa kumele inikezwe abazali noma umnakekekeli osemthethweni, kanti uma umzali noma abazali bengakwazi ukwenza lokho, umuntu ocelwe ngomunye wabazali noma umnakekeli osemthethweni anganikeza inothisi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Fakela inothisi yokuzalwa kwifomu le-BI-24 kunoma yiliphi ihhovisi lezaseKhaya.
Umuntu ocelwe ukuth anikeze inothisi kumele abe nelungelo lokuthunywa elibhaliwe, okumele libandakanye izizathu zokuthi kungani abazali bengakwazi ukunikeza inothisi ngokwabo.
Uma ingane izalwe ngaphandle kweNingizimu Afrika kanti omunye wabazali eyisakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika ngesikhathi sokuzalwa kwengane, ukuzalwa kungafakelwa inothisi yakho kwiNxusa, iConsulate noma kunoma yiliphi ihhovisi lezaseKhaya eNingizimu Afrika.
Kwinothisi yokuzalwa enikezwa ngemuva kwezinsuku ezingu-30, kodwa ngaphambi kokuba ingane iqede unyaka, abazali noma umnakekeli osemthethweni kumele banikeze izizathu zokuthi kungani bengabhalisanga ezinsukwini ezingu-30 njengoba kudingwe wumthetho.
Uma abazali bengashadile nakhona befisa ukubhalisa ingane ngaphansi kwesibongo sikababa, ubaba kumele amukele ukuthi nguyena ubaba wayo (paternity), kwisikhala esibekelwe loko kwiFomu lokubhalisa ukuzalwa. Ubaba kumele abekhona lapho kubhaliswa ukuzalwa.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izicelo zingathatha isikhathi sokufinyelela kumaviki ayisishagalombili ukuya kwangu-12.

Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ukuze ufaneleke kulolu hlelo, kumele ube yilunga lemiphakathi eyayicikelwe phansi ngesikhathi esedlule (okusho ama-Afrika, amaKhaladi noma amaNdiya)
ubeneminyaka angu-18 noma engaphezulu kwayo ube nenhloso yokusebenzisa umhlaba ngenhloso yezolimo kuphela ube nenhloso yokulima ngokuphelele ungabe sesikhundleni sikahulumeni uzimisele ukungenela uhlelo loqeqesho ngemuva kokuthola umhlaba ube kwisimo sokufaka esivivaneni nawe nibe nihleleke kahle, uma nenza isicelo njengeqembu nokuba ne-akhawunti yebhangi.
Abalimi abaphumelelayo, abafisa ukukhulisa imisebenzi yabo yolimo, bangenza izicelo zemixhaso (zosiso lwamagranti), kodwa inani eliphelele lwemali yosizo ngeke yaba ngaphezulu kwenani lika-R100 000. Ezinye izimali zokuqala noma zokukhulisa umsebenzi wolimo, kumele zicelwe kumabhenki.
Umxhaso (usizo lwegranti) yohlelo lwe-LRAD, alubuyiselwa (alukhokhelwa) njengemali-mboleko. Kusetshenziswa ifomyula (indlela ethize) ukubala ukuthi umuntu oyedwa uthola malini. Ukuze ufaneleke ukuthola umxhaso (imali yosizo lwegranti) ka-R20 000, kumele ube nemali ozoyifakela nawe engu-R5 000. Lokhu okufakelayo akudingekile ukuthi kuze kube yimali engukheshi, kungaba yimishini yokulima, imfuyo noma enye impahla  (asset) yezolimo. Ngisho nabasebenzi imbala, bangafakelwa kuprojekthi njengokuthela kwakho esivivaneni somxhaso wohlelo lwezolimo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Khomba umhlaba ofisa ukuwuthenga. Kokunye, ungaba usunawo umhlaba, ofana nomhlaba ohlanganyelwe womphakathi.
Thintana noMnyango wezoLimo noma isikhulu somlimisi endaweni yangakini ukwenza isicelo somxhaso (sosizo lwegranti).
Fakela okulandelayo kanye nesicelo:
o isiphakamiso sokuthi umhlaba uzosetshenziswa kanjani noma iplani yokulima (i-proposal yeprojekthi)
o ukukhetha ukuthenga nesivumelwano senani lentengo, uma niqasha ngenhloso yokuthenga o uhla lwamalunga omuzi noma amalunge eqembu, uma nihlongoza ukuthola umhlaba njengeqembu o isiqinisekiso sokuthi udaba lwetayitela lucacile, akukho abenze isicelo sokukhalazela ukubuyiselwa lowo mhlaba, umhlaba ubhaliswe ngegama lomthengisi, kanye nokuthi inani lentengo yomhlaba alikho ngaphezulu kwenani lentengo ezimakethe (market price)
o ubufakazi bokuthi insalela yemali edingekayo ikhona (okungaba yimali yenu, noma isivumelwano semali-mboleko)
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Inqubo izothatha isikhathi esingangezinyanga ezine, kodwa uma iprojekethi yenu inesixaka-xaka, kungathatha isikhathi eside kunalesi.

Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Idokhumende kamazisi (i-ID), yidokhumende efakazela ubuyena bomuntu. Amadokhumende kamazisi anikezwa kwizakhamizi zaseNingizimu Afrika noma kubantu abanezimvume zokuhlala ezweni unomphela, abaneminyaka engu-16 nangaphezulu. La madokhumende anganikezwa nakunoma ngubani omunye umuntu osebenzela uhulumeni noma inhlangano esungulwe ngokulandela umthetho ngaphandle kweNingizimu Afrika, noma umuntu ocelwe ukusebenza egameni likahulumeni kwelinye izwe. Lokhu kunikezwa nakowakwakhe noma ezinganeni.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yenza isicelo kwihhovisi eliseduzane leZangaphakathi noma imission uma ungaphandle kwezwe.
Gcwalisa ifomu le-BI-9.
Uma ukuzalwa komuntu kungabhaliswanga (kungarejistwanga), kanti futhi singekho isitifikedi sokuzalwa, gcwalisa ifomu le BI-24/15 ebese uhambisa nenye yamadokhumende asekelayo, isibonelo, isitifikedi sokubhabhadiswa, incwadi yesikole, noma isitifikedi sokukhulelwa.
Fakela izithombe ezimbili zosayizi kamazisi (we-ID).
Abenzi bezicelo abashadile kumele bakhiphe namakhophi ezitifikedi zomshado.
Owesimame owenza isicelo sikamazisi (se-ID) nakhona ofisa ukusebenzisa esinye sezibongo ayaziwa ngaso, kumele anikeze nobufakazi bokuthi unelungelo lokusebenzisa leso sibongo.
Umuntu obuya ngaphandle kwezwe, owenza isicelo sikamazisi (se-ID), kumele afakele nemvume yokuhlala ezweni unomphela, kanye nesitifikedi sokuba nelungelo lokuba yisakhamuzi sezwe (naturalisation) noma isitifikedi sokukhishwa phansi kwezibopho (exemption certificate), isitifikedi somshado kwifomu le-BI-529.

Izincazelo
Kukhona inqubo yemithetho okumele ilandelwe maqondana nezilwane kanye nemikhiqizo (ama-product) ezilwane. Ngenxa yokuthi izidingo kwezempilo zishintsha njalo, sicela nixhumane nabophiko lobuqondisi bempilo yezilwane iDirectorate Animal Health ukuthola izidingo zakamuva.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Fakela imali yediphozithi kwi-akhawunti yoMnyango yebhangi:
Inombolo yegatsha: 010845
Inothisi ebalulekile: I referense kumele ibe yigama nesibongo somngenisi
Sicela Uqaphele: Umngenisi womkhiqizo, unomsebenzi emahlombe akhe wokukhokhela amashaji ebhangi, kanye namashaji eBhangi langaPhandle uMnyango ngeke wakhipha imvume uma inani eligcwele elingu-ZAR110.00 lingakakhokhelwa.
Imali ingakhokhelwa nasemahhovisi e-Department of Agriculture (oMnyango wezoLimo), uhla lawo olufakelwe ngezansi esigabeni esithi Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Kwisimo lapho wenza isicelo egameni lomunye umuntu, sicela uqinisekise ukuthi ukhokha egameni lalowo muntu noma leyo nkampani.
Sicela uchaze kwifomu ukuthi ngabe imvume izolandwa noma kumele uposelwe yona.
Isiteshi sokuhlukaniswa kwezilwane zingahlaliswa nezinye i-quarantine station, eThekwini, okwamanje asisebenzi, kodwa ulwazi lungatholakala ehhovisi lenombolo ethi: +27 31 368 6011 noma ngefekisi kule nombolo: +27 31 337 7469.
Lapho kungeniswa izikhumba ezimanzi noma ezomile kanye nezindondo (trophies), ifomu lesicelo kumele liqinisekiswe ngudokotela wezilwane kahulumeni endaweni lapho ifasilithi etholakala khona. Ifomu lesicelo kumele libuye lisayinwe ngomunye kanti futhi lifakelwe isitembu sikadokotela wezilwane wombuso, ozobuye athumele ifomu ngefekisi.
Lapho kungeniswa imfuyo yepulazi ephilayo efana nezinkomo, izimvu, izimbuzi, amahhashi (amakati nezinja akuvunyelwe) kumele uthole imvume yokuthuthukiswa kwezilwane (animal improvement permit) ngaphambi kokuba kukhishwe imvume yempilo yokungeniswa kwezilwane.

Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isicelo, sivamise ukuthatha izinsuku ezine zokusebenza ukuphetha ngodaba lwemvume, uma udokotela wezilwane esevumele isicelo.

Ukungenisa izinto ezweni kanye nokuthumela yokungenisa imithi yogonyo lwezilwane ngaphakathi
Isicelo semvume yokungenisa imithi yogonyo lwezilwane ngaphakathi eNingizimu Afrika
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Udinga nemvume yokungenisa impahla ebuya kwabe-Directorate Animal Health yemithi yogonya ukuze ingene ezweni. Banikeza lemvume kanye kuphela, lapho usuthole isigunyaziso esivela kwabomkhandlu wolawulo lwemithi abe-Medicines Control Council, kanti futhi ngokuveza ubufakazi balokho kubo. Kulesi simo esinjena, umuthi wogonyo ongarejistiwe ungathengiswa isikhathi esithize kuphela, kuze kufinyelele isikhathi lapho usurejistiwe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
o ubungakanani (bamanani)
o umxubo noma isithako (izithako), ezibandakanya ukujula nomkhawulo walezo zithako (concentration)
o izwe umuthi obuya kulo o abenzi bomuthi (manufacturer)
o ukuthi ngabe leyo-product idingeka nini o incazelo yokuthi lo muthi uzosetshenziselwa ukulinga ukuze urejistwe njengesitokwe semithi o isimo sobhaliso lwawo (serejistreshini yawo) kwezinye izindawo (uma ngabe kukhona lapho orejistwe khona)
o imininingwane ye-Trial Protocol (ngokulandela izidingo ze-VICH GCP), okubandakanya ukuthi ngabe kulindeleke ukuthi uzoqala ngaluphi usuku, isibonelelo esiyisipesheli sokuthi ngabe kuzokwenzekani ngokudla okuvela kuzilwane ezenza ukudla (isibonelo, ngaba kuzolahlwa) kanye nokuthi abanikazi bomuthi bachaza ngokufanele ngomuthi.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Sicela wenze isicelo se-veterinary import permit okungenani amaviki ayisithupha ngaphambi kwesikhathi sokungenisa lokho ohlongoza ukukungenisa. Uma abe-state veterinarian sebevumele isicelo, kuthatha izinsuku ezimbili ukuya kwezintathu zokusebenza ukuthi ihhovisi lezimvume libhekane nesicelo nokunikeza imvume.
