Ukungenisa izinto ezweni kanye nokuthumela
Isicelo semvume yokungenisa izimoto ezithengiswa o
Isicelo semvume yokungenisa izimoto ezithengiswa okwesibili noma esezike zasetshenziswa
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Uma ufuna ukungenisa izimoto ezithengiswa okwesibili noma esezike zasetshenziswa eNingizimu Afrika, udinga imvume yesipesheli ebuya kuNgqongqoshe waboMnyango wezoHwebo neZimboni noma ama-agent akhe. Ihhovisi likaNgqongqoshe lizothatha isinqumo ngokubonelela isizwe ngezimoto ezithengwa okwesibili noma esezike zasetshenziswa ukuthi zingeniswe ngaphakathi ezweni.
Kumele ufakele ifomu elichaziwe ukwenza isicelo sezimvume zokungenisa ngaphakathi ezweni. Izimvume zokungenisa impahla ngaphakathi zidingeka kuzo zonke izimpahla ezingaphansi kolawulo lwezimpahla ezingeniswayo.
Izimvume ziyadingeka ukuqinisekisa ukuthi impahla ethengiswa okwesibili engeniswayo, ngeke yacikela phansi imboni ye-South African Customs Union (SACU). Le nkonzo (le sevisi) ithuthukisa ukulandelwa kwenqubo kwezokuphepha kanye neqophelo.

Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Nikeza aboMnyango ikhophi eqinisekisiwe yesitifikedi sobhaliso (serejistreshini) yemoto.

Indawo yokuhlala
Isicelo somxhaso sosizo lwendlu kumuntu oyedwa
Isicelo somxhaso (sosizo) lwendlu kumuntu oyedwa
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Umxhaso (usizo) lwendlu kumuntu oyedwa, yigranti kahulumeni enikezwa umuntu siqu ofuna ukuba nobunikazi besitende esinamasevisi noma izinsiza (esinezinkonzo). Umxhaso (usizo) lwenza ukuthi labo abazuzayo bakwazi ukungenela ikontraki nomakhi, noma ukuthenga indawo yokuhlala evele ikhona, engeyona ingxenye yeprojekthi yomxhaso wendlu osuvele wamukelwe.
Lo mxhaso ubuye usize nalabo okumele bawuthole obafisa ukukhuphula izinga lemixhaso yabo ngokuthola imali-mboleko, kanye nalabo abangakwazi ukuthola imali-mboleko.
Abantu abahlangabezana nalezi zidingo ezingezansi kumele benze isicelo:
izakhamuzi zaseNingizimu Afrika noma umuntu onemvume yokuhlala unomphela oshadile noma ohlalisana nomunye umuntu isikhathi eside umuntu oneminyaka engaphezulu kuka-21 ubudala onabancike kuye abathola umholo womndeni uhlangene ongu-R3 500 noma ongaphansi kwaleli nani umuntu ongakaze abe nendlu yokuhlala ngaphambilini (kodwa kukhona abangabophezelekile kulo mbandela)
abenzi-zicelo abakhubazekile abenza izicelo sokushintshelwa okuthile (kudingeka isitifikedi sikadokotela).
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo elitholakala kuphela kwaboMnyango wezeZindlu wesiFundazwe (weProvinsi), emabhangi kanye nakomasipala.
Thatha ifomu uhambe nalo kwabomthetho ababhekana nokubhalisa ubunikazi bempahla ukusuka komunye ukuya komunye (conveyance) ukuyogcwalisa iSigaba (i-section) G.
Thatha ifomu uhambe nalo kuMboleki noma kwisikhungo sezezimali ukugcwalisa iSigaba (isection) H umangabe leso sicelo sihambisana nokuthola imali-mboleko.
Hambisa ifomu kuSokontilaki ukugcwalisa iSigaba (isection) I, uma kuyindlu esanda kwakhiwa.
Hambisa nawo wonke amadokhumende esiqinisekiso, isibonelo, incwadi kamazisi, isitifikedi somshado, isitifikedi sokuzalwa, ubufakazi bomholo kanye netayitela lokuthenga.

Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu le-BI-1738.
Khipha incwadi evela kwinkampani yaseNingizimu Afrika eqinisa ukuthi udluliselwa kuyo yinkampani engumzali wayo noma inkampani eyi-affiliate yayo ephesheya, kanye nokuchaza inhlobo yomsebenzi owenzayo kanye nezinga noma isikhundla sakho ozobe usebenza kuso.
Cela umqashi ofuna ukukuqasha ukuthi agcwalise isigaba sika-13 , no- kwifomu le-BI-1738.
Fakela isiqiniseko esivela ku-chartered accountant esebenza egameni lomqashi esichaza isidingo sokukuqasha kanye nokuchaza inhlobo yomsebenzi owenzayo.

Izincazelo
Isitifikedi esi-Green (Green Certificate) sinikezwa lapho isampuli ithathwa phakathi kwezinye ngaphansi kwegunya lelabhorethri egunyaziwe, kodwa uhlolo lwenziwa kwenye ilabhorethri nayo egunyaziwe kodwa kwelinye izwe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Amasampuli anikezwa abe-OSTS abese ehlolelwa iqophelo (ikhwalithi) ebese kukhishwa isitifikedi.
Khokha imali yokuhlolwa kwezimbewu. Lapho sekwamukelwe amasampuli, kuzokhishwa i-invoice (isigqebhezana sephepha esibonisa imali okumele ikhokhelwe). Imininingwane yeBhangi yilena engezansi:
Ikhodi leGatsha: 01-08-45
Inombolo ye-akhawunti: 011244887
Kungakhokhwa ngendlela ye-elektroniki (ngokusebenzisa i-inthanethi) noma ngokukhokha kukhawuntala, kodwa kubalulekile ukuthi usho inombolo ye-invoice ukuze bazokwazi ukuqhathanisa imali ekhishiwe kanye ne-invoice eyenziwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Awukho umthetho osetshenziswayo.
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Abesiteshi esigunyaziwe sokuhlolwa kwezimbewu iOfficial Seed Tasting Station, babonelela kuqala ukuhlolwa kwamasampuli ezimbewu ezithunyelwa ngaphandle. Izinhlobo ezehlukene zezitshalo zinezimo ezehlukene zokuhlolwa kanti isikhathi sokuhlolwa siyehlukahluke ukusukela ezinsukwini ezimbalwa ukuya kumaviki ambalwa.
Abe-OSTS bazimisele ukuqhumisa amasampuli ezimbewu kwikhonsayinimente noma inqwaba eyodwa ngaphansi kwezimo ezifanayo zohlolo. Kanti futhi iklayenti ingacela uhlolo olunhlobo-nhlobo. Ngeke kwanikezwa ulwazi maqondana namazinga okuhlola. Buza kuleyo ndawo ngokwayo (bheka imininingwane yokuxhumana).
Phezulu
Izindleko
Ukwenza amasampuli kanye nokuvala imiphongolo ngci angangeni umoya uR230 ngehora
Izigxivizo zokuvala imiphongolo inganeni umoya ngu-R0,10 ukuvala ngci (per seal)

Izinkonzo Kubantu
Isicelo sokujoyina ilearnership uma ungasebenzi
Isicelo sokujoyina i-learnership uma ungasebenzi
Isimo
Ubani ongenza isicelo?
Noma ngubani oqede isikole, ikholishi noma iyunivesithi nakhona ongasebenzi. Abantu abangasebenzi kumele babhalise njengabantu abafuna umsebenzi kuMnyango wezeMisebenzi.
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi, hamba uye kwaboMnyango wezemiSebenzi.
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Cabanga ngohlelo loqeqesho kanye nomsebenzi owufunayo. Ngamanye amagama, uma uthanda ukuba ngaphandle, ungenzi isicelo somsebenzi osetshenzwa ngaphakathi ehhovisi.
Yenza i-CV enikeza imininingwane ngawe siqu efana nokulandelayo:
Igama lakho kanye nosuku lokuzalwa
Imfundo, isikole owafunda kuso kanye namakhwalifikheshini akho
Isipiliyoni somsebenzi, igama labaqashi oke wasebenza kubo nomsebenzi owawuwenza.
Amanye amakhono onawo, afana nekhono lekhompyutha, ukuthi ngabe unelayisense yokushayela, nokunye.
Orefri (referees) (amagama nemininingwane yabantu abakwazi kahle emsebenzini noma ezifundweni zakho)
Bhalisa njengomuntu ofuna umsebenzi esikhungweni sezemisebenzi (labour centre) esiseduzane nawe. Lokhu kuzosiza abaqashi ukuthi bakuthole uma befuna ukusungula i-learnership.
Xhumana nama-SETA ukuthola ukuthi ngabe yibaphi abaqashi abanama-learnership.
Cela abangani nomndeni ukuthi bakubhekise lapho kunezithuba zemisebenzi kumanyuziphepha.
Xhumana nesikhungo sezemisebenzi i-labour centre ukuze ufakele imininingwane yakho emisha uma ishintshile.
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ubude bama-learnership buyahlukahlukana, kodwa kuvamise ukuthi kungabi ngaphansi konyaka.
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Izinkonzo Kubantu
Ukwenza amaphepha kahulumeni nezitifiketi ezisetshenziswa ngabantu zibe ngezisemthethweni
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Isigaba sokwenza into ibe semthethweni:
Senza amaphepha kahulumeni adabuka kuleli lase Iningizimu Africa abe semthethweni ukusetshenziswa ngaphandle kwaleli ngokukhipha isitifiketi okuthiwa yi Apostille noma ngesitifiketi eziveza ubufakazi bokuthi angawo qobo
Inikeza abakhonzi ulwazi ngocingo, ngencwadi noma nge email.
Amanxusa ase Iningizimu Africa kwamanye amazwe angwenza amaphepha ukuthi abe semthethweni uma aqale enziwa ukuba semthethweni yisiphathimandla saleyo ndawo, noma enziwe ukuba semthethweni yisigaba salowomsebenzi segatsha lezangaphandle. Amanxusa akuleli awanalo ilungelo lokukhipha isitifiketi okuthiwa yi Apostille kodwa banalo ilungelo lokukhipha isitifiketi eziveza ubufakazi bokuthi angawo qobo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Qala uthole ukuthi yiliphi iphepha ofuna ukuthi lenziwe libe senthethweni.
Isigaba sokwenza into ibe semthethweni ingakhipha isitifiketi eziveza ubufakazi bokuthi angawo qobo noma isitifiketi okuthiwa yi Apostille gokulandela lemithetho elandelayo:
Umele ukuthi utshele abesigaba sokwenza into ibe semthethweni ngelizwe lapho ufuna ukuthi iphepha lisebenze khona ukuze bakwazi ukufunisisa ukuthi kudingeka isitifiketi eziveza ubufakazi bokuthi angawo qobo noma isitifiketi okuthiwa yi Apostille.
Ngaphambi kokuba abesigaba sokwenza into ibe semthethweni bangakhipha isitifiketi eziveza ubufakazi bokuthi angawo qobo noma isitifiketi okuthiwa yi Apostille iphepha kumele lenziwe libe semthethweni ngu:
Yimantshi, noma yimantshi eyongeziwe, noma yisekela likemantshi
Ngumbhali noma isekela lakhe noma inkantolo ephakeme yase Iningizimu Africa.
Thunmela amaphepha akho kwabesigaba sokwenza into ibe semthethweni ngayinye yalezindlela ezilandelayo:
Wena ngesiqu sakho - amaphepha athunyelwe ngalendlela ungawalanda uma uveza ubufakazi bokuthi wawaletha. Uma uthumela amaphepha akho ngokuwamikisa wena mathupha unikezwa irisidi yokuvuma ukuthi bawatholile amaphepha akho.
Ngesithunywa - isithunywa sizonikezwa iphephe lokulanda amaphepha elizodingeka mzukwana ulanda amaphepha. Isithunywa sikhokhelwa nguwena.
Ngeposi eliphuthumayo - emaphepheni akho, faka imvilopi elingana nephepha elejwayelekile enekheli lakho eseyikhokhelwe nemali yokuposa (imali ayivunywa) ukuze abesigaba sokwenza into ibe semthethweni bakwazi ukubuyisela amaphepha akho emuva. Bhala nencwadi esho ukuthi uthumele amaphepha angakanani ukuze enziwe abe semthethweni, nezwe ofuna ukuthi asebenze kulona, kanye nemininingwane yakho ephelele yokukuthinta.
Hambisa amaphepha akho adlule enxuseni lase Iningizimu Africa kulelozwe (umkhomishane omkhulu, ehhovisi lenxusa).
Amaphepha afingqiwe noma abhalwe ngecomputha
Amaphepha omshado, afakazela ukuzalwa, afakazela ukufa noma isitifiketi semvume yamaphoyisa afakazelwe ngesitembu samaphoyisa
Izitifiketi ezichaza isimo somuntu mayelana nomshado noma ezifakazela umuntu ukuthi uyisakhamuzi sakuliphi izwe ezifakazelwe ngesitembu samaphoyisa
Amakhophi epasipoti noma encwadi yomazisi afakazelwe ngesitembu samaphoyisa
Amaphepha afakazelwe ukhomishane ofungisayo ukuthi angawo qobo. Ummeli wokulungisa izivumelwano nguye omele ukuthi awafakazele.
Lamaphepha alandelayo awadingi ukwenziwa abesemthethweni kuqala yinkantolo njengoba kushiwo ngaphezulu uma ngabe asayiniwe yisiphathimandla esiwakhiphile:
Izitifiketi ezingafinciwe zokuzalwa, zomshado, zokufa nencwadi efakazela umuntu ukuthi uyisakhamuzi sakuliphi izwe, ezisayiniwe zaphinde zagqotshwa isitembu yisisebenzi segatsha lezasekhaya elinemvume yokwenze njalo.
Amaphepha okuthola intandane asayiniwe, agqotshwa isitembu ngumbhali walelohhovisi lokuthola izintandane egatsheni lezomthetho.
Amaphepha okuthengisa impahla yalapha kwamanye amazwe asayiniwe, agqotshwa isitembu yigatsha lezohwebo.
Izitifiketi zemfundo ezisayiniwe zaphinde zagqotshwa isitembu yisisebenzi segatsha lezemfundo.
Izitifiketi zemvume yamaphoyisa ezisayiniwe zaphinde zagqotshwa isitembu ngabaphenti bamacala bamaphoyisa ase Iningizimu Africa. (isitifiketi semvume yamaphoyisa siyaphela emuva kwezinyanga ezintathu).
Incwadi esho ukuthi azikho izigibe mayelana nomshado esayiniwe yaphinde yagqotshwa isitembu yisisebenzi segatsha lezasekhaya elinemvume yokwenze njalo.
Amaphepha achaza isimo sempilo asayiniwe, agqotshwa isitembu yisisebenzi sekhansela yabantu abafundele ezempilo esinemvume yokwenze njalo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Amahora omsebenzi omphakathi msombuluko - lwesihlanu, 08:30 - 12:30 (ngaphandle kwamaholide).
Abesigaba sokwenza into ibesemthethweni bazokhipha isimemezelo sezinsuku ezingu 7 uma bezovala, loku akuwafaki amaholide.

Ukubhalisa umkhumbi wokudweba kwabemikhumbi yamazwe angaphandle

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Izimvume namalayisense
Isicelo semvume noma selayisense
Isicelo selayisense yokudoba kwabemikhumbi yangaphakathi kwezwe
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Umkhumbi wokudweba uvamise ukuba ufakelwe izinto ezizosetshenziselwa ukudweba noma okunye okuhambelana nalokhu. Umkhumbi ubandakanya onke amagiya (gear), imishini esetshenziswayo (equipment), indawo yokugcina, impahla, kanye namafutha okuqhuba umkhumbi (fuel) ngaphakthi komkhumbi. Umkhumbi wangaphakathi ungabandakanya noma yimuphi omunye umkhumbi owodwa noma ngaphezulu eselwandle ekwenzeni imisebenzi ehambelana nokwedweba. Lokhu kungabandakanya, kanti futhi hhayi lokhu okulandelayo kuphela:
amalungiselelo ukusaplaya (ukuphakela)
Akukho umuntu ongavunyelwa ukusebenzisa umkhumbi wokudweba, noma yimuphi omunye umkhumbi ukusebenzisa ilungelo lokufinyelela (access) ngaphandle kokuba kukhishwe ilayisense, ehambelana nemvume uma umnikazi womkhumbi enelayisene yelungelo lokudweba. Uma umnikazi womkhumbi engenalo ilungelo lokudweba, kumele kube nekontraki yokusebenzisa lowo mkhumbi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thola ifomu lesicelo kwaboMnyango wesimo seNdalo nezokuVakasha.
Kumele ube ngonelungelo lokudweba (wenze isicelo kanti futhi wanikezwa imvume yokudweba). Uma ungeyena umnikazi welungelo lokudweba onomkhumbi, kumele ufakele, noma ubandakanye ikontraki, oyiphethe nomnikazi womkhumbi ukusebenzisa lowo mkhumbi lapho ufakele isicelo selayisense yomkhumbi.
Gcwalisa ifomu ngokuprinta ngokusebenzisa amagama amakhulu kuphela, kanye nokubhala incazelo yesidingo (motivation) lapho kufanele.
Khombisa ubude besikhathi sokusebenza kwawo kanye nesicelo somkhakha wokudweba lapho lowo mkhakha ohlose ukusebenza khona.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Iisicelo singathatha izinsuku ezintathu zokusebenza noma ngaphezulu kwazo, kuye ngokuthi isicelo sefakwe ngokulandela inqubo efanele.

Ukubhekana nomthetho
Izikhalazo
Isicelo sokufakela isikhalazo
Isimo
Ukubopha ummangalelwa;
Ukuhluleka ukusiza umenzi wesikhalazo ukuvula icala, ukumtholela indawo efanele yokufihla ikhanda (shelter), ukuhluleka ukuhambisa umenzi wesicelo ukuyokwelashwa, ukuhluleka ukuhamba nomenzi wesikhalazo ukuyolanda impahla, kanye nokuhluleka ukuthatha noma yisiphi isikhali esiyingozi kumhlukumezi;
Ukuhluleka ukubonisa umenzi wesicelo izinto angakhetha kuzo, ezifana nokuhluleka ukweluleka umenzi wesicelo ukuvula icala lobugebengu noma ukwenza isicelo somyalelo wokuvikelwa yinkantolo, noma kokubile; kanye
Nokuhluleka ukuhambisa umyalelo obhaliwe kummangalelwa wokuthi avele enkantolo.
Udlame lwasekhaya lwenziwa ngumuntu onobudlelwane naye enza okulandelayo:
Ekuhlukumeza ngokomzimba noma ngokocansi, isibonelo, ekushaya noma ekudlwengula.
Ekuhlukumeza ngamazwi, ngokomoya nemizwa (emotional) noma ngokwengqondo (psychologically), isibonelo, ekwethuka, ekuphatha ngendlela yomona noma ekhweleza ngokweqile njalo.
Ekukhathaza (harass you), isibonelo, ehlala ekushayela izingcingo ezicasulayo, ekuthumela izincwadi zochuku, noma ekunqabela ekwenzeni izinto, noma egadane nawe ekulandelela lapha oya khona.
Engena endaweni yakho ngaphandle kwemvume yakho.
Ekuhlukumeza ngokwezimali, isibonelo lapho umyeni noma uphathina efuna ukwenza zonke izinqumo ngemali, futhi engafuni ukukhokhela ukudla noma imali yesikole, nokunye.
Ekwesabisa, isibonelo, ekusabisa ngokukubulala uma ufuna ukuya emaphoyiseni.
Elimaza impahla yakho, isibonelo, eshisa izingubo zakho zokugqoka, ephihliza ifenisha yakho, noma edabula amathayela emoto yakho.
Ephula umyalelo okhona wenkantolo wokukuvikela.
Eziphatha nanganoma yiyiphi indlela yokukuhlukumeza, esabisa ukuphepha kwakho, impilo yakho noma indlela yakho yokuphila kahle.
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Fakela isikhalazo ngokwako uqobo, ngokushayela ucingo, ngencwadi, noma nge-email kunoma yiliphi ihhovisi eliseduzane nawe labe-ICD.
Nikeza abe-ICD ulwazi olulandelayo:
Isitatimende ngalokho okwenzekile
Igama nemininingwane yokuxhumana nohlukunyeziwe kanye/noma umfaki wesikhalazo
Igama nesikhundla sephoyisa (samaphoyisa) elingumsolwa (angabasolwa)
Igama lesiteshi samaphoyisa
Inombolo yekheyisi (yecala) yamaphoyisa, uma ikhona
Imininingwane yofakazi, uma bekhona
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Imiyalelo kaZwelonke maqondana noDlame lwaseKhaya: 7/1999
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isikhathi sokulindela ukukhulumisana nawe - imizuzu (amaminithi) angu-30
Ukwamukela isikhalazo ngokubhala kungathatha cishe izinsuku ezingu-30.
Umbiko ngokuqhubekayo - ungathatha izinsuku ezingu-30
Umbiko wokugcina - ungathatha izinsuku ezingu-90
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Izinkonzo Kubantu
Ukubhekana nomthetho
Izikhalazo
Isicelo sokufakela isikhalazo ngokungaziphathi kah
Isimo
Inhloso yale nkonzo ukusiza amalunga omphakathi ukuthi akwazi ukubika izikhalazo kwabophiki oluzimele lwezikhalazo abe-Independent Complaints Directorate (ICD). Uma ufakela isikhalazo nabehhovisi likazwelonke le-ICD, udaba luzothunyelwa kwihhovisi lesifundazwe (leprovinsi) (kuye ngokuthi indawo ikuphi lapho okwenzeke khona lesosenzakalo) ukuze kuphenyisiswe.
Izinhlobo zezikhalazo ezingafakelwa kwabe-Phiko oluziMele lweziKhalazo (ICD):
Isikhalazo se-Class III (sesigaba sesithathu) Isikhalazo esibeka ukuthi iphoyisa noma amaphoyisa enze icala elibangele ukulimala kakhulu komzimba okuze kwabangela ukuthi umuntu ayokwelashwa esibhedlela. Lokhu kungaziphathi kahle noma icala kumele lenzeke ngesikhathi umuntu engaphansi kogcini lwamaphoyisa noma eboshwe ngamaphoyisa. Okulandelayo wuhla lwezikhalazo eziwela ngaphansi kwe-Class III (izikhalazo zesigaba III)
Ukusetshenziswa kabi kwamandla okunomphumela emphakathini ngokulandela ukulimala okubangekile; inani labantu abathintekile; inani lemali ethintekile; noma isikhathi lokhu okwenzeke ngaso.
Ukushaya ngenhloso yokulimaza umzimba kabi, noma ukuzama ukubulala
I-forgery (umgunyathi), ukwamukela idokhumende efojiwe (yomgunyathi) futhi ebe azi ukuthi ifojiwe, ukufinyelela umkhawulo ongu-R50 000 noma ngaphezulu
Ukuqamba amanga enkantolo ngaphansi kwesifungo (perjury)
Ukuthwala ngenkani ngenhloso yokukhokhelwa (kidnapping)
Ukulimaza kabi impahla ngamabomu
Ukubamba iqhaza kumaqembu obugebengu ahlelwe ngenhloso yobugebengu (criminal syndicate)
Ukutholakala unempahla eyebiwe (entshontshiwe)
Ukudlwengula
Ukwamukela impahla eyebiwe (entshontshiwe)
Ukurobha noma ukuphanga impahla (robbery)
Ukuhlasela umuntu ngokumlala ngemuva (Sodomy)
Ukweba (ukuntshontsha)
Ukuhlukumeza kabi ngenhloso yokuzwisa ubuhlungu (Torture)
Ukuthola inzuzo ngempoqo noma ngokwesabisa (Extortion)
Ukugqugquzelela abanye ukushisa imimoya nokwenza okubi okungekho emthethweni (incitement) ukubopha uzungu lwento embi (conspiracy) noma ukuzama ukwenza amanye amacala abekwe kuhla ngenhla
Izikhalazo ezingeke zafakelwa kwabe-ICD:
Izikhalazo zezenzakalo ezenzeka kungakasungulwa i-ICD, isibonelo, zonke izinto ezenzeka ngaphambi kuka-Epreli 1997. Kanti futhi nezenzakalo ezenzeka esikhathini esingaphezulu konyaka ngaphambi kokuba zibikwe kwabe-ICD, ngaphandle kokuba kunezimo ezingavamile.
Ukuze uthole olunye ulwazi olunabile, ngena kwi-website yabe-Phiko oluziMele lweziKhalazo.
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Fakela isikhalazo ngokwako uqobo, ngokushayela ucingo, ngencwadi, noma nge-email ehhovisi eliseduzane nawe le-ICD. Zonke izikhalazo kumele zifakelwe ngesikhathi samahhora okusebenza, esikhathini esiphakathi kuka-8h00 (ekuseni) kanye no-16h30 (ngo-4:30 ntambama).
Abe-ICD bangakucela ukuthi unikeze ngolunye ulwazi ngaphambi kokuba kuphenyisiswe ngesikhalazo.
Uma uzizela wena mathupha, uzokhulumisana nobhekene namakheyisi noma umhlaziyi (analyst).
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ukubonelela izikhalazo:
Ukwamukela isivakashi okwenziwa yiklayenti kungathatha cishe imizuzu emihlanu noma amaminithi amahlanu.
Ukwamukela isikhalazo ngokubhala kungathatha cishe izinsuku ezingu-30.
Umbiko ngokuqhubekayo kungathatha cishe izinsuku ezingu-30
Umbiko wokugcina ungathatha cishe izinsuku ezingu-90
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Ukubika ubugebengu izenzakalo

Isimo
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Nikeza abe-ICD ulwazi olulandelayo:
Igama nemininingwane yokuxhumana nohlukunyeziwe kanye/noma umfaki wesikhalazo
Igama nesikhundla sephoyisa (samaphoyisa) elingumsolwa (angabasolwa).
Igama lesiteshi samaphoyisa noma sikamasipala lapho okusebenza khona iphoyisa elingumsolwa.
Inombolo yekheyisi yamaphoyisa, uma ikhona
Usuku, isikhathi kanye nohlobo lokuhluleka ukusiza ohlukumeziwe.
Imininingwane yofakazi, uma bekhona.
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Abe-ICD bazophenyisisa ikheyisi (icala) ngokuya lapho okwenzeke khona ubugebengu, kanye nokuthola izitatimende kufakazi(kofakazi).
Uphenyisiso lungathatha cishe izinsuku ezingu-30 noma ngaphezulu, kuncike kubunzima becala.
Abe-ICD bazothumela Umbiko wokubonwa nokutholakala unecala/ womlayelo wokukhululwa wokutholakala ungenacala kwiklayenti.
Ukwenziwa kombiko wokugcina ungathatha izinsuku ezingu-180.
Yonke imibiko efakelwa kwi-database ingathatha cishe amahora angu-48.
Ukwenziwa kwesincomo semibiko yokugcina, kungathatha cishe izinsuku ezingu-14 ngemuva kokwamukela imibiko etheknikhali.
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Izincazelo
ukunweba 'ukufinyeleleka' kwelungelo lokuthola ulwazi ukubandakanya ukufinyelela kweningi kumkhakha wokwenza nokukhiqiza imisebenzi yobusakazi.
ilungelo lenkululeko yezezindaba zamaphepha nababhali bezindaba (press) nezinye izinhlobo zezezindaba (ze-media), inkululeko yokuthola nokuhambisa ulwazi nezindaba nemibono, inkululeko kwezobuciko nobuqambi; inkululeko kwezomkhakha wezemfundo ophezulu kanye nenkululeko yezocwaningo.
ukusukela ekuthuthukisweni kombono, udaba lwenqubo yokulingana lubalulekile ekwenzeni ukuthi zonke izinto zihambe ngokulingana ngendlela efanele nelungile kwimboni yezokusakaza ngokukhuthaza ukungena nokusungulwa kwamabhizinisi amasha kule makethe yezokusakaza.
Izinkonzo (amasevisi) ezobusakazi bamaredioyo bomthamo ophansi - obusebenza ezindawo ezihlala abantu abadala, ukuxhumanisa izindawo zamasonto, ukuxhumanisa izindawo zezinkonzo zenkolo kanye namakhaya, noma ezinye izindawo ezinjengalezi, iziphathimandla ezingabubona bufanele.
Ubusakazi bamarediyo bemiphakathi kanye nobentengiso bomthamo ophansi buyosebenza iminyaka emithathu, kanti ilayisense yobusakazi bomthamo ophansi buyosebenza izinsuku ezingamashumi amathathu.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Ukubheka ukuthi ngabe yimaphi amafrikhwensi afanele angasetshenziswa ngumthamo ophansi.
Ukubheka indawo isiteshi sokusakaza esizosakazela kuyo.
Sungula iqembu lokusebenzisana (working group) elizobhekana nenqubo yesicelo kanye nokuba ngumenzi wesicelo (applicant) egameni labagqugquzeli bomcimbi ozokwenziwa.
Qinisekisani ukuthi iqembu lokusebenzisana, lenza okulandelayo:
o ukuqoqa amasignesha emphakathini o ukubhala iplani yebhizinisi o ukugqugquzelela ukukhethwa kwamalunga eBhodi ngenqubo yedimokhrasi (yentando yeningi)
Fakelani ifomu lesicelo lokuqala (original) kanye namakhophi amafomu esicelo aqinisikisiwe angu-16 kwabe-(ICASA) nemali yesicelo.
Izicelo ezingagcwalisiwe ngokuphelele, zizobuyiselwa kumenzi wesicelo kanti ngeke samukela emahlombe ethu ukuphazamiseka okungabakhona.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo

Izincazelo
Wonkemuntu owakha izimoto, nozivuselelayo, noma ozithenga ngaphandle kwaleli ezozithengisa, noma ozithengisayo nje, kumele abhalise negatsha lezokuthutha.
Lizothi lingathola isicelo, igatsha lesifundazwe lizothumela umhloli ayohlolisisa othumele isicelo ukuthi uyazithobela yini izimiselo ezibekiwe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thintana nagatsha lezokuthutha lesifundazwe.
o ikhophi yomazisi enesitembu samaphoyisa o ikhophi yesitifiketi sebhizinisi esinesitembu samaphoyisa o incwadi yokumela uma umele inkampani o inombholo yentela evela kumnyango wezentela waseMzansi (uma uthenga izimoto ngaphandle kwaleli)
o isitifiketi semvume esivela emaphoyiseni aseMzansi.

Izincazelo
Isicelo kumele sifakwe ngosuku athatha amalanga akhe okuphumula noma-ke zingakapheli izinyanga eziyisithupha ingane ibelethiwe.
Imibandela ebekiwe:
Onikela esikhwameni somshuwarensi wokungasebenzi kumele ukuthi uma aselivini yokukhulelwa, uthola iholo elingaphansi kwaleli avamise ukulithola.
Imali yenzuzo ingakhokhwa kuze kufike ezinsukwini ezingu 121 uma ngabe ofake isicelo unazo lezonsuku.
Uma kungenzeka isisu sonakale noma ingane ibelethwe isibhubhile, imali yenzuzo ingakhokhwa kuze kufike emavikini ayisithupha.
Incwadi efakazela okhulelwe isho nesikhathi asithethe aze abelethe imele ukuthi nayo ithunyelwe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thintana nehhovisi labasebenzi eliseduzane nawe ukuze uthole amafomu afanele.
Gcwalisa uphinde uthumele amafomu afanele kanye nencwadi ekufakazela ukuthi ukhulelwe evela kadokodela noma esibhedlela ehhovisi labasebenzi.
Khumbula ukunikeza imininingwane yasebhange eyiyona evunyelwe yibhange.

Izincazelo
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo BI-1738.
Fakela ipasipoti yakho okumele isebenze isikhathi esingekho ngaphansi kwezinsuku ezingu-30 ngemuva kokuphela kwesikhathi sokuvakasha.
Fakela ubufakazi bokuba nemali elingene ukubhekana nezidingo zosuku nosuku zabo bonke abantu abahamba nalowo onemvume yokuzokwelashwa (uma bekhona) ngokufakela isitatimende, isiliphi somholo (uma sikhona), noma amasheke asetshenziswa ekuvakasheni.
Fakela incwadi ebuya kudokotela noma isikhungo sezokwelapha, ekhombisa izizathu noma isidingo sokwelashwa, isikhathi okulindeleke ukuthi sizothathwa kulokhu kwelashwa kanye nemininingwane yohlelo lokwelashwa eNingizimu Afrika.
Fakela imininingwane yomuntu noma isikhungo esibhekene nezindleko zokwelashwa kanye nezimali zesibhedlela zalowo onemvume uma kukhona. Fakela ubufakazi bokuba nezimali zokukhokhela ukwelashwa noma i-medical aid yakho noma umqashi wakho bengeke bakhokha.
Fakela enye noma amanye alamadokhumende alandelayo ukuhambisana nesicelo sakho semvume:
o isitifikedi esiphelele sokuzalwa o isitifikedi semininingwane ephelele yomendo (uma lokhu kusebenza)
o isitatimende esifungelwe kwabezomthetho (i-afidavithi) engu-BI-1712A, uma kungaganenwe ngendlela evamile, kanye nobufakazi bokuhlalisana o ikontraki yesivumelwano eyenziwe ngabomthetho (notarial contract), uma kunokuhlalisana o ubufakazi bomendo wosiko (customary union), uma lokhu kusebenza o incwadi yomthetho yedivosi (divorce decree), uma lokhu kusebenza o ubufakazi bomyalelo wenkantolo onikeza igunya lokunakekela (custody), uma lokhu kusebenza o isitifikedi sokushona, uma owakwakho eshonile, uma lokhu kusebenza o imvume ebhaliwe evela kubo bobabili abazali, noma lowo mzali ongumnakekeli oyedwa, uma lokhu kusebenza, kanye nobufakazi bokuthi nguye yedwa ongumnakekeli o ubufakazi obusemthethweni bokuba ngumnakekeli wengane, uma lokhu kusebenza o umyalelo wesehlukaniso (separation order), uma lokhu kusebenza o isitifikedi samaphoyisa sokukhombisa ukuba msulwa (police clearance certificate), uma umenzi wesicelo eneminyaka engu-21 noma ngaphezulu kwayo, maqondana namazwe owake wahlala kuwo isikhathi esingangonyaka noma ngaphezulu kwawo o isitifikedi sokugonywa, uma lokhu kusebenza.
Phezulu

Ukuqhuba ibhizinisi lezimayini
Ukwenza isicelo selungelo lokuqhuba ibhizinisi lezimayini
Izincazelo
Ilungelo lokuqhuba ibhizinisi lezimayini yimvume enikezwa wumbuso ekhishwa wuMnyango wezemaMinerali ne-Eneji enikeza wena igunya lokwenza umsebenzi wokumba amaminerali endaweni ethize.
Ilungelo lokumba amaminerali linikezwa uma: 
Iminerali ingambiwa ukufinyelela ezingeni eliphezulu
Uma umenzi wesicelo enezimali ezenele kanye nekhono lokuqhuba umsebenzi wokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali ukufinyelela kwizinga eliphezulu
Iplani yezezimali (financial plan) ihambisana nomsebenzi wokufunana nomcebo wezimali waso sonke leso sikhathi.
Uma kungeke kwaba nokungcoliswa komoya nendalo noma ukulinyazwa kwesimo sendalo okuzokwenzeka ngomsebenzi wokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali.
Umenzi wesicelo enze amalungiselelo ezimali kanye nokunye ngohlelo olubekiwe (olunqunyiwe) ngezenhlalakahle yabantu kanye nezemisebenzi
Ukuze uthola olunye ulwazi, hamba uye kwaboMnyango wezamaMinerali
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Ifomu kumele lihambisane nemali ekhokhelwa isicelo njengoba ibekwe ngu-Regional Manager.
Ukuze ufakele uhlelo lonakekelo lwesimo sendalo ukuze lwamukelwe
Ukwazisa kanye nokuthintana nabo bonke abathintekayo ezinsukwini ezingu-180 ukusukela ngosuku lwenothisi.
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
U-Regional Manager (uMininjela weRijini) uzokwazisa ngokubhala uma isicelo sakho sivunyiwe, lokhu kusho ukuthi isicelo sakho sihambisana nazo zonke izidingo, lokhu uzokwenza ezinsukwini ezingu-14 ngemuva kokuthola isicelo kanye nokukulayela ukuthi uxhumane nomnikazi womhlaba, ohlezi kulowo mhlaba ngokomthetho, kanye nabanye abathintekayo.
Uma ungqongqoshe enqabela ilungelo lokumba amaminerali, kumele akwazise ngokubhala, ezinsukwini ezingu-30, achaze izizathu zokwala.
Izindleko
Umnyango uzobeka izindleko, kanti le mali ayibuyiselwa kuwe.
Inani lezindleko liyahluka kuye ngeminerali ofuna ukusebenzana nayo.

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Imvume yokuqhuba ibhizinisi lomsebenzi wezimayini yidokhumende ekhishwa ngaboMnyango wezamaMinerali ne-Eneji okuyiwona okuvumelayo ukuthi uqhube umsebenzi wezimayini. Akukho muntu ovunyelwe ukuthi aqhube ibhizinisi lezimayini ngaphandle kokuthola imvume yokuqhuba ibhizinisi lezimayini.
Imvume yokuqhuba ibhizinisi lezimayini ngeke yadluliselwa komunye. Imvume inenhloso yokulawula ukufunana namaminerali kanye nokuqhuba ibhizinisi lezimayini ngokubonelela ezempilo nokuphepha, ukulawulwa kwesimo sendalo kanye nokumbiwa kwamaminerali ngendlela efanele.
Imvume yokuqhuba ibhizinisi lezimayini kumele isebenze ngokulandela leso sikhathi esichazwe kwimvume, kodwa esingeqi iminyaka emibili. Ingavuselelwa ezinye izikhathi zezihlandla ezintathu, kodwa isihlandla nesihlandla esisodwa ngeke saba ngaphezulu konyaka.
Imvume yokuqhuba ibhizinisi lemayini ingakhishwa kuphela uma:
leyo minerali okukhulunywa ngayo, ingambiwa isikhathi seminyaka emibili indawo yokwenza ibhizinisi lemayini ingadluli kumamitha angu-1.5 mÂ².
kungekho omunye umuntu onelungelo lokufunana namaminerali, ilungelo lokuqhuba ibhizinisi lemayini, imvume yokuqhuba umsebenzi wemayini, noma imvume yokugodla iminerali noma umhlaba.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yenza isicelo kuMininjela weRijini (Regional Manager) woMnyango wezamaMinerali ne-Eneji kwirijini lapho kutholakala khona lowo mhlaba.
Umininjela weRijini uzokwamukela isicelo uma zonke izidingo kuhlangatshezwane nazo.
Uma isicelo sakho sesamukelwe, uMininjela weRijini uzokucela ukuthi ufakele uhlelo iplani yokunakekelwa kwesimo sendalo, kanye nokuxhumana nomnikazi womhlaba kanye nabakuwo ngokomthetho legal occupier, kanye nabanye abathintekayo.
Fakela umbiko wokukhulumisana kuMininjela ezinsukwini ezingu-30.
Ungqongqoshe uzokhipha imvume uma zonke izidingo kuhlangatshezwane nazo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
UMininjela weRijini uzokwazisa ezinsukwini ezingu-14 ukuthi isicelo sakho siphumelele, okusho ukuthi uhlangabezane nazo zonke izidingo.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Umnyango uzobeka izindleko, kanti le mali ayibuyiselwa kuwe.
Inani lezindleko liyahluka kuye ngeminerali ofuna ukusebenzana nayo.

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Le nkonzo isiza amalunga emiphakathi ukubika ngomuntu onyamalele. Ungabika umuntu onyamalele ngokushesha uma usola ukuthi kukhona okungalungile okwenzeke kuye.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Uma usola ukuthi umuntu unyamalele, zama ukuxhumana naye kuqala.
Uma ungakwazi ukuxhumana naye, hamba uye kunoma yisiphi isiteshi samaphoyisa , noma kwisikhungo senkonzo yamaklayenti (client service centre) ngokushesha.
Nikeza ulwazi ngomuntu, kanti nikeza ngesithombe sakhe samaduzane.
Uma umuntu onyamalele etholakala, hamba uye esiteshini samaphoyisa lapho owabika khona ukuthi umuntu unyamalele ukwazisa iphoyisa noma isikhulu esiphenyisisa icala.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ungabika ngomuntu onyamalele kunoma yisiphi isiteshi samaphoyisa, hhayi nje kuphela endaweni lapho umuntu okusolwa ukuthi unyamalele khona.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe

Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Lomsebenzi wenzelwe abantu abakha izimoto, nabathenga izimoto kwamanye amazwe nabathengisa izimoto kuleli. Inombholo yokuhweba ngezimoto ibavumela ukuthi bashayele imoto engabhaliswanga emgwaqweni womphakathi.
Inombholo yokuhweba ngezimoto idingekela loku okulandelayo:
abakhi bezimoto bakwazi ukuzidiliva , nokuzithengisa, nokuzishintsha noma ukuzilungisa abantu abakhontileka ngezimoto bakwazi ukuzidiliva abantu abathengisa izimoto bakwazi ukuzidiliva abantu abathenga izimoto kwamanye amazwe bakwazi ukuzidiliva.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu MTN 1 ehhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
Chaza ukuthi uzifunelani izinombholo zokuhweba kanti futhi udinga ezingakanani.
Thumela lamaphepha alandelayo:
o ikhopi yencwadi yomazisi wakho noma incwadi yokumela uma kuyinkampani o isitifiketi sokubhaliswa kwebhizinisi.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Phezulu
Izindleko 
Imali yokufaka isicelo
Mpumalanga
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Lokufaka isicelo senombholo yokuhweba ngezimoto, MTN1, litholakala emahhovisi abaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni.

Izincazelo
Loku kwenzelwe ukwazisa igatsha lezokuthutho ngokushintsha kwekheli lomuntu onelungelo emotweni noma umnikazi wemoto. Bonke abanamalungelo ezimotweni noma abanikazi bezimoto kumele baqinisekise ukuthi amakheli abo abhliswe encwadini yezimoto ayiwo uqobo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Vakashela ehhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
Gcwalisa ifomu lesaziso ubanikeze.
o ikhophi yomazisi wakho o ubufakazi bekheli lakho elisha.

Izincazelo
I-Nedlac kumele yaziswe lapho kuzoba nenyathelo lokubhikisha, lokhu kwenziwe kwifomu le-LRA 4.5. Kuleli fomu, izizathu kanye nohlobo lombhikisho, kumele kuchazwe. Lokhu akubandakanyi usuku lwenyathelo elihloswe ukuthathwa, ngoba lokhu kungacikela phansi inqubo yokuthola indlela yokuvumelana.
Uma imizamo yokuxazulula udaba ihluleka, inyunyane noma ifedereshini yezinyunyane kumele yethule inothisi yesibili kwabe-Nedlac ukubazisa ngenhloso yabo yokuqhubeka nenyathelo lokubhikisha. Le nothisi yesibili kumele ilethwe kwabe-Nedlac okungenani izinsuku ezingu-14 ngaphambi kokuba kuqalwe inyathelo lombhikisho.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Unobhala wenyunyane noma ifedereshini yezinyunyane kumele agcwalise ifomu lesicelo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
I-Nedlac kumele ithole ifomu okungenani ezinsukwini ezingu-14 ngaphambi kokuba kuqalwe inyathelo lombhikisho.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Izincazelo
Loku kwenzelwa ukwazisa iziphathimandla ezibhalisa izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni ukuthi ubuniyo bemoto bushintsile. Isaziso kumele sithunyelwe kuziphathimandla ezibhalisa izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni kungakapheli izinsuku ezingu 21 ushintsho lwenzekile.
Onelungelo manje (imvamisa ngamabhange noma izinhlangano zezimali) noma umnikazi wamanje, kumele asayine isitifiketi sokubhaliswa kwemoto asinikeze onelungelo omusha noma umnikazi omusha.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umnikazimoto manje umele athintane nehhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze naye.
o isitifiketi sokubhaliswa semoto o ilayisensi esebenzayo yendingilizi yemoto o nomazisi wakhe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Phezulu
Izindleko
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Isaziso sokushintsha komnikazi noma ukuthengiswa kwemoto sitholakala ehhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni.

Izimvume namalayisense
Isicelo semvume noma selayisense
Isicelo semvume yokwenza umsebenzi wokuprosesa izinhlanzi
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
I-fish processing establishment (FPE) kungaba yimoto, umkhumbi, isakhiwo noma indawo lapho okukhiqizwa khona i-substance noma i-article yezinhlanzi. I-substance ingakhiqizwa nganoma yiyiphi indlela efana nokusika, ukuhlinza, ukuhlukanisa izingxenye, ukuhlanza, ukuqoqa nokuhlela (sorting) ukusika uqweqwe (lining) kanye nokwenza okuvimbela ukuvunda kwezinhlanzi (preserving fish). Ukwenza lomsebenzi kungabandakanya indawo lapho izinhlanzi ezifakelwa khona emathinini, ezipakelwa khona, ezomiswa khona, ezikhishwa amathumbu nokwangaphakathi, ezifakelwa khona usawoti (itswayi), eziqandiswa khona ngo-ayisi (iced), ezibandiswa khona, ezomiswa khona ngokuqandiswa (frozen) noma eziproseswa khona ukuze zithengiswe eNingizimu Afrika noma ngaphandle kwayo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yenza kuphela isicelo selungelo lokudweba lapho kunesimemo esishichilelwa kuGazethi kaHulumeni.
Funda ngokucophelela, uqondisise kanye nokuqinisekisa ukuthi wenelisa izidingo nemibandela yesicelo.
Gcwalisa, usayine kanye nokufakele ifomu lesicelo elihambisana namadokhumende asekelayo aqinisekisiwe, kanye nemali yesicelo engabuyiselwa emuva.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isicelo selungelo le-FPE singathatha inyanga eyodwa ukuya kuzinyanga ezintathu  noma ngaphezulu ngenxa yobuningi bezicelo. Iisicelo semvume ye-FPE singathatha usuku noma ngaphezulu , kuye ngokuthi isicelo sifakwe ngokulandela inqubo efanele.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Imali ekhokhwayo ibuyekezwa ngonyaka/ibekwa ngonyaka nguNgqongqoshe noma ubuthunywa bokugunyaza, obuhlanganyelwe noNgqongqoshe wezeziMali.
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe

Izincazelo
Lapho umuntu ethola ingeniso okumele likhokhele intela kodwa elingadonselwa intela ye-SITE noma ye-PAYE (isibonelo, inzalo, irente, noma ingeniso lebhizinisi), kumele akhokhele intela yesikhashana ngaleli ngeniso. Intela yesikhashana (provisional tax) yenziwa njalo ezinyangeni eziyisithupha.
Intela yesikhashana (provisional tax) inenhloso yokusiza abakhokhi bentela ukuhlangabezana nezibopho zabo zentela ngokuqhubekela phambili kunokuthi bakhokhe isamba esikhulu kanye ngonyaka lapho kuhlolwa inani lentela okumele ikhokhekhelwe. Intela yesikhashana izokhokhelwa ukwehlisa inani okumele likhokhelwe kunani lilonke lengeniso (intela evamile) leyo okumele ikhokhelwe ngumuntu siqu kulowo nyaka wezimali ofanele wokukhokhwa kwentela.
Umuntu okumele akhokhele intela yesikhashana (provisional tax), kumele kuthi kungakapheli izinsuku ezingu-30 ngemuva kokuba efanele ayikhokhe, enze isicelo ngokubhala, isicelo sobhaliso (serejistreshini) ehhovisi legatsha labe-South African Revenue Service (SARS). Ukuhluleka ukwenza njalo, kuyoba nomphumela wokukhokhiswa inzalo (i-interest) kanye nama-penalty ngokukhokha sekwedlule isikhathi, kanye nenye futhi intela engezelelwe ehambisana namafomu okukhokhelwa kwentela ngokukhokha kamuva sekwedlule isikhathi.
Usuku okumele kukhokhwe ngalo intela yesikhashana kwisikhathi sokuqala kwenziwa kungakapheli izinyanga eziyisithupha ukusukela onyakeni wokuhlolwa kwezimali zentela. Intela yesikhashana yokuqala kumele imele inani elinguhafu wentela okumele ikhokhelwe kunyaka wonke.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Balela intela yesikhashana kwisikhathi sokuqala ngokulandelayo:
o uhafu wentela iyonke kunyaka ophelele o ususe intela yomsebenzi edonsiwe kulesi sikhathi (izinyanga eziyisithupha - 6)
o ususe noma yini enye imali yentela yangaphandle evunyelwe kulesi sikhathi (izinyanga eziyisithupha - 6).
Khokha lemali kwifomu lokukhokhela intela le-IRP6, ngosuku noma ngaphambi kosuku olubekelwe ukukhokha. Abe-SARS bazothumela abakhokhe bentela yesikhashana ababhalisile amafomu afanele.
Imali yentela, abantu bangayikhokha ngalezindlela ezilandelayo:
o imali yentela yokwesikhashana ingakhokhwa ehhovisi lomnyango wezentela eliseduzane, kusukela ngoMsombuluko kuya kuLwesihlanu phakathi kwehora lesishiyagalombili ekuseni, nehora lesithathu maseligamenxe entambama ngaphandle kwezinsuku ezingamaholide omphakathi.
o uma ngabe abantu bakhokhe ngokuyiposa imali noma bayifake ebhange, kufanele bakhumbule ukuthi bazinikeze isikhathi sokuhamba kweposi noma ukusetshenzwa kwezimali ezifakwe ebhange.
o umangabe imali ithunyelwe nge Computer, kufanele abantu bakhumbule ukuthi ibhange linesikathi elijuba usuku ngalo kanti futhi lidinga isikhathi sokuhlola ukuthi ngabe lomuntu othumela imali unayo yini lemali ebhange, loko kungathatha izinsuku ezimbili kuya kwezinhlanu.
Imininingwane yaseBhange:
inombolo yomthelo ethi uma iphelele ibe nezinombolwana eziyishumi neshiyagalolunye inombolo okuzokuthi uma lemali ingena iye kuyona, loku kusho iakhawunti ezokwemukela lemali engenayo.
Yonke imali ekhokhwayo ingavumelani noma ingahambelani naloku okulotshiwe ngaphezulu, ngokwenombolo yomthelo kanye nenombolo yeakhawunti emukelayo, ayingeke imukeleke.
Uma usuku lokugcina lokukhokha lufika kuyiholide noma kuyimpela-sonto, kufanele abantu bakhokhe ngosuku lokugcina lokusebenza olungaphambi kwe holide noma impela-sonto. Uma ufuna ulwazi olunzulu yiya kuwebsite yakwa SARS ethi www.sars.gov.za
Qaphela:
Amafomu e-IRP 6 kwisikhathi sokuqala kumele afakelwe, ngisho noma ngokubona kwakho kungekho intela yesikhashana okumele ikhokhelwe.
Uma ukukhokhelwa kwentela yesikhashana kwenziwa ngokubonelela isilinganiso sengeniso okumele likhokhelwe intela, elingaphansi kwenani lokuqala (basic amount), kumele kwenziwe isicelo kanye nencazelo. Ifomu lesicelo sentela le-IRP 6 kwisikhathi sokuqala, libandakanya incazelo yaleso sicelo. Uma abe-SARS benganelisekanga, kungecelwa isilingeniso esibuyekeziwe.
Ukugwema ukukhokhiswa ama-penalty kanye nenzalo, qinisekisa ukuthi amafomu e-IRP 6 ayafakelwa kwihhovisi elifanele legatsha le-SARS kungakafiki isikhathi sokukhokhwa kwentela. Lokhu kuzoqinisekisa ukuthi isicelo sakho samukelwe nokubonelelwa kusenesikhathi.
Manje isigaba semali okumele ikhokhwe kwifomu le-IRP 6 sithokalala kwelinye ikhasi eliseceleni. Ifomu lakho lokukhokha lingafakelwa lodwa kunemali ekhokhwayo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Qaphela
Uma ucele omunye umuntu ukuthi akwelekelele ukufaka isaziso sakho sentela, kumqoka ukuthi wazise ihhovisi lomnyango wezentela kahulumeni. Loku kwenzalwa ukuvikela wean ukuze kuqinisekiswe ukuthi umnyango wezentela awukhiphi izimfihlo zakho eziqondene nawe kuphela.

Izincazelo
Lapho umuntu ohola imali okumele idonswe intela kodwa ongaweli ngaphansi kwemithetho yokudonswa kwentela ye-SITE ne-PAYE. (isibonelo, inzalo yemali, irente, noma ingeniso lebhizinisi), kumele akhokhele intela yesikhashana yaleli ngeniso. Ukukhokhela intela yesikhashana (provisional tax), kwenziwa njalo ezinyangeni eziyisithupha.
Intela yesikhashana (provisional tax), inenhloso yokusiza abakhokhi-ntela ukuhlangabezana nezibopho zabo zentela, njalo kunokuthi balindele ukukhokha kanye ngonyaka lapho sekuhlolwe isimo sabo sezentela. Intela yesikhashana, ekhokhwayo isiza ukuphungula isamba sentela okumele sikhokhelwe ekupheleni konyaka wentela lapho sekuhlolwe ingeniso (ngesikhathi esivamile sentela), okumele ikhokhwe ngumuntu kulowo nyaka.
Umuntu okumele akhokhele intela yesikhashana, kumele kuthi kungapheli izinsuku ezingu-30 ngemuva kokufaneleka ukukhokha, enze isicelo ngokubhala, ukuthi abhaliswe egatsheni eliseduzane naye lophiko lwabezentela iSouth African Revenue Services (SARS). Ukuhluleka ukubhalisa, kuzobangela ukuthi ukhokhiswe inzalo yentela kanye nama-penalty (ukujeziswa), lapho ukhokhe ngemuva kwesikhathi, kanye nenye intela ngenxa yokukhokha ngemuva kwesikhathi.
Usuku lokukhokha intela yesikhashana yesibili ngeke lwaba semuva kakhulu nosuku lokugcina lokwenzwa uhlolo lwezimali noma usuku oluvunyelwe lokuphela konyaka wezimali.
Qaphela: (kusebenza kubantu ziqu kanye nakuma-trust kuphela)
Lapho ukuphela konyaka wezimali ngoFebruwari kubanga ubunzima kwezezimali, nakhona kutholwe imvume evela kwa-SARS, ukufakela izitatimende zezimali ngosuku olwehlukile nolukaFebruwari, abantu abanjalo bangacela imvume yokufakela imibiko yentela yesikhashana ngokuhambisana nokuphela konyaka wezimali okwamukelekile. Zonke ezinye izimali ezingenayo kusafanele zibe sesikhathini sokuphela konyaka wohlolo lwezimali oluphela ngomhlaka-28/29 Februwari.
Balela ukukhokhelwa kwentela yesikhashana kwesibili ngendlela elandelayo:
inani eliphelele lentela kunyaka ogcwele ususe intela ekhokhelwa abasebenzi ekhokhelwe unyaka ogcwele ususe umkhawulo ovunyelwe wezimali zentela yangaphandle yonyaka ogcwele kususwe inani elikhokhiwe lesikhathi sokuqala sonyaka.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Khokha ngokugcwalisa ifomu le-IRP 6 lenothisi yokukhokha, ngaphambi kosuku noma ngalolo suku okumele ukhokhe ngalo.
E-SARS kukhona izindlela ezilandelayo zokukhokha:
Khokha noma kuliphi ihhovisi legatsha le-SARS, NgoMsombuluko ukufikela kuLwesihlanu, phakathi kwesikhathi sika-08h00 ekuseni ukufikela ku-15h30 ntambama, ngaphandle kwezinsuku zamaholidi.
Lapho ukhokha ngeposi, noma ngokusebenzisa i-ATM, kumele ukhokhe kuseyisikhathi ukuze ifomu lakho lifike kusenesikhathi emahhovisi e-SARS.
Uma ukhokha ngendlela ye-elektroniki (ngamakhompyutha nezingcingo), kumele lokhu ukwenze ngesikhathi ukuze unike ibhangi lakho ithuba lokudlisela lemali kuseyisikhathi, kanye nesikhathi sokubheka ukuthi ngabe kunemali eyanele kwi-akhawunti yakho, ngoba lokhu kungathatha cishe izinsuku ezimbili ukuya kwezinhlanu.
Imininingwane yaseBhange
inombolo yomthelo ethi uma iphelele ibe nezinombolwana eziyishumi neshiyagalolunye inombolo okuzokuthi uma lemali ingena iye kuyona, loku kusho iakhawunti ezokwemukela lemali engenayo.
Yonke imali ekhokhwayo ingavumelani noma ingahambelani naloku okulotshiwe ngaphezulu, ngokwenombolo yomthelo kanye nenombolo yeakhawunti emukelayo, ayingeke imukeleke.
Uma usuku lokugcina lokukhokha lufika kuyiholide noma kuyimpela-sonto, kufanele abantu bakhokhe ngosuku lokugcina lokusebenza olungaphambi kwe holide noma impela-sonto.
Qaphela:
Qinisekisa ukuthi amafomu e-IRP6 afakelwa ehhovisi legatsha elifanele le-SARS ngaphambi kokuba ukhokhe. Lokhu kuzoqinisekisa ukuthi isicelo sakho samukelwe kuseyisikhathi ukuze sibonelelwe, nakhona kungabi bikho isidingo sokuthi ujeziswe (ukhokhe ama-penalty) kanye nenzalo.
Iseluleko sokukhokhela ingxenye yentela kwifomu le-IRP6 manje isitholakala ekhasini elehlukile. Ngakho-ke, ungafakela umbiko wakho wentela eceleni hhayi lapho ukhokha.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu

Izincazelo
Inqubo yezezindlu zabantu (People's Housing Process), yindlela kahulumeni yokusiza ngezindlu esekela amakhaya afisa ukuthuthukisa imixhaso yawo yamasabsidi ngokuzakhela izindlu noma ngokugqugquzelana ngokwabo ukuzakhela amakhaya abo.
Inqubo isiza ukuthi abafuna ukuzuza basungule inhlangano yokusekela kwezezindlu ezobasiza ngokuba nosizo kwinhlangano, ezobuthekniki kanye nezokuphatha. Usizo lubandakanya uqeqesho kanye nokwelulekwa kwabazuzayo ukuzakhela amakhaya abo.
Le ndlela itholakala njengeprojekthi kubazuzi, ngokuhlanganisa, ukwenza izikhungo kanye namasabsidi amaprojekthi. Nangaphezu kwalokho, ibuye itholakale ezindaweni zasemakhaya lapho khona abantu abanelungelo lokusebenzisa nokuhlala kumhlaba (functional tenure rights) abakuwo.
Le nqubo yenza ukuthi usebenzise usizo lwesabsidi ukuzakhela indlu yakho. Ngenxa yokuthi usizo kwezobutheknikhali (ubucwepheshe) bubaluleke kakhulu kule nqubo, wonke umuntu obamba iqhaza kwinqubo, kumele abe nenhlangano esekelanayo. Le nhlangano isiza ngosekelo lobutheknikhali (lobuchwepheshe) ukuqinisekisa ukuthi indlu yakhiwe ngendlela efanele.
Abantu abazakhela izindlu ngokwabo noma abagqugquzelela ukwakhiwa kwezindlu zabo, bangakha izindlu ezinkulu ngemali encane.
ukonga ngezindleko zabasebenzi ngokuthi abantu bayazakhela okunye ngokwabo noma ngokuthola usizo emindenini yabo, komakhelwane, kubangani kanye nabanye abangasizana nani ukugwema ukukhokha inzalo (iprofit) kwabamakontilaki okwakha, kanye nokuthuthukisa izinqumo.
Ukuze ukwazi ukungena kule nqubo, kumele ube:
Ngoshadile noma onohlalisana naye, noma ube nabancikile ngokwezimali.
Ube ngumuntu omdala ngokwanele okwaziyo ukungenela ikontraki. Lokhu kusho ukuthi kumele ube ngumuntu oneminyaka engaphezu kwengu-21 noma ushadile, nakhona ube ngophile kahle engqondweni
Ube nomholo womndeni ongaphansi kuka-R3 500.
Ningabi ngabathole usizo lwesabsidi yokuthenga indlu kuHulumeni ngesikhathi esedlule.
Ube ngumuntu ofuna ubunikazi bendlu okokuqala ngqa.
Qaphela ukuthi kumele ungenele ikontraki ngokusekelwa yinhlangano esemthethweni (legal entity) Inhlangano esekelayo izosiza ngolwazi lobuchwepheshe (lobutheknikhali) kanye nakwezokuphatha.Â 
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe xhumana noMnyango wezeZindlu weprovinsi (wesifundazwe)
nikeza ngomazizi wakho (i-ID yakho), yowakwakho kanye nezingane nikeza ngekhophi yesiliphi somholo njengobufakazi bokuthi uyahola.Â

Izincazelo
Ungaba yiphoyisa lerizevu ngaphansi kwemikhakha elandelayo:
o ukwenza imisebenzi yokusizana namaphoyisa engenziwa esiteshini samaphoyisa, kwi-area noma emkhakheni wesifundazwe (weprovinsi), kodwa kungeyona imisebenzi eyisipesheli o lingagqoka iyunifomu
o ukwenza imisebenzi yokusekela emahhovisi emkhakheni kazwelonke, wesifundazwe (weprovinsi), kwi-area, noma esiteshini, kanti ngeke yenza imisebenzi yobuphoyisa ngokugcwele o ayigqoki iyunifomu o lapho kufanele khona, ingaqeqeshwa emikhakheni yezomthetho, imigomo nemiyalelo esebenza emkhakheni ekuwo noma eqondene nomsebenzi awenzayo.
o kumele ibe namakhono athize noma ulwazi lobuchwepheshe (expertise) olungasetshenziswa ngabenkonzo yamaPhoyisa, isibonelo, ama-pilot, odokotela, abatshwizi emanzini (divers), osonhlalonhle, noma ama-psychologist o izokwenza imisebenzi ethize kumkhakha wolwazi lwakhe lobuchwepheshe o angathi ngokuvunyelwa ngukhomanda wakhe, agqoke iyunifomu o lapho kufanele khona, ingaqeqeshwa emikhakheni yezomthetho, imigomo nemiyalelo esebenza emkhakheni ekuwo noma eqondene nomsebenzi ewenzayo.
o ukwenza imisebenzi yokusebenza kwamaphoyisa ehambelana nobuphoyisa emikhakheni yezobuphoyisa emadolobheni nasemakhaya emikhakheni ethize esiteshini, noma ukwenza kuphela imisebenzi echazwe kumkhakha wayo emkhakheni othize njengoba kubekwe ngukhomanda o ingagqoka iyunifomu kuye ngokusetshenziswa kwayo o kumele iqeqeshwe emkhakheni ofanele wobuphoyisa kanye/noma imisebenzi yobuphoyisa ehambelana nemisebenzi yakhe. Kungadingeka olunye uqeqesho uma yenza umsebenzi wobuphoyisa emikhakheni ehlukene yobuphoyisa.
Iphoyisa lerizevu liqokwa kumkhakha othize, kanti lingadluliselwa (transferred) kunoma yimuphi omunye umkhakha ohambelana nalo, ngokuhambelana nazo zonke izidingo zomkhakha othize, nakhona yenze iqeqesho noma izimisele ukuthola uqeqesho odingekayo kulowo mkhakha.
lingabi nakuphazamiseka ngokwengqondo, izifo, noma ubuntekenteke (infirmity), obungaphazamisa ukwenza kwakhe imisebenzi yakhe libe ngomuntu wesimilo esihle, kanti kulokho kuzophenyisiswa ngemibuzo nokuthola umlando wakhe;
kumele lenze uvivinyo noma uhlolo, olubekwa nguKhomishana kaZwelonke wamaPhoyisa okungenani abe nesitifikedi sebanga eliphezulu (senior certificate), noma esilingana naso, kanye nokunikeza ubufakazi balokhu akwazi ukukhuluma, ukubhala kanye nokufunda i-English (isiNgisi), njengolunye lwezilimi ezigunyazwe wumthetho avume ukuthi kugxivizwe iminwe yakhe ukuze iyohlolwa nakhona angatholakali enecala noma engumuntu oshishiselwa icala azimisele ukuqeqeshwa azimisele ukuthatha isifungo sehhovisi angabi nawo ama-tattoo abonakalayo; kanti abe nelayisense yokushayela, noma-ke lokhu kungesona isidingo sokuqala sokuba yirizevu.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe yiya esiteshini samaphoyisa esiseduzane nawe gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo le-SAPS 93
ifomu lemininingwane yakho siqu (Annexure B)
Hamba nala madokhumende alandelayo:
o ikhophi eqinisekisiwe yelayisense yakho yokushayela, uma unayo o amakhophi aqinisekisiwe ezifundo zakho.
o umuntu oseke wakhishwa emsebenzini ngenxa yokugula ngengqondo o ilunga labophiko lwe-South Africa Police Service isicelo sakhe sokubuyela ebuphoyiseni esanqatshelwa o umuntu okunoma yisiphi isikhundla kwiqembu lezopolitiki o umuntu osebenza ngokugcwele kwezezindaba noma omele abezindaba o umfundi o isikhulu sezokuphepha o ilunga lenkonzo yobuphoyisa koMasipala.

Isicelo semvume noma selayisense
Isicelo selungelo lokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali
Izincazelo
Ilungelo lokufunana nomcebo wamamaminerali, yivume evumela inkampani noma umuntu siqu ukwenza i-survey noma ukuphenyisisa ngendawo yomhlaba ngenhloso yokubona indawo ethize enamaminerali noma engaba namaminerali. Noma ngubani omunye umuntu ofuna ukwenza isicelo selungelo lokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali, kumele afakele isicelo ehhovisi lika-Regional Manager lapho umhlaba okhona.
Ilungelo lokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali lisebenza iminyaka emihlanu, kodwa ngemuva kokuphela kweminyaka emihlanu, umenzi wesicelo angacela imvuselelo yelungelo lokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali yesikhathi esingekho ngaphezulu kweminyaka emithathu.
Ilungelo lokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali lizonikezwa uma uhlangabezane nezidingo ezilandelayo:
Umenzi wesicelo unezimali ezenele kanye nekhono lokuqhuba umsebenzi wokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali ukufinyelela kwizinga eliphezulu
Iplani yezezimali ihambisana nomsebenzi wokufunana nomcebo wezimali waso sonke leso sikhathi.
Uma kungeke kwaba nokungcoliswa komoya nendalo noma ukulinyazwa kwesimo sendalo okuzokwenzeka ngomsebenzi wokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali.
Uma kungekho omunye umuntu onelungelo lokufunana nomcebo wamaminerali, ilungelo lokuqhuba ibhizinisi lezemayini, imvume yokuqhuba ibhizinisi lezezimayini, noma imvume yokugodla leyo minerali noma umhlaba.
Ukuze uthole olunye ulwazi, ngena kwaboMnyango&#160;wezamaMinerali ne-Eneji
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yenza isicelo kuMininjela weRijini (Regional Manager) woMnyango wezamaMinerali ne-Eneji kwirijini lapho kutholakala khona lowo mhlaba.
Ifomu kumele lihambisane nemali edingekayo okumele ikhokhelwe isicelo
Fakela umbiko wokukhulumisana kuMininjela ezinsukwini ezingu-30.
Uma zonke lezi zidingo kuhlangatshezwane nazo, uRegional Manager uzofakela isicelo kuNgqongqoshe ukuze asibonelele.
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Uma uRegional Manager engasivumeli isicelo, uzokwazisa ngokubhala ezinsukwini ezingu-14 kanye nokubuyisela isicelo kuwe.
Uma u-Regional Manager evumela isicelo sakho, uzokwazisa ezinsukwini ezingu-14,ukuthi ufakele iplani yokubhekana nesimo sendalo, kanye nokukhulumisana nomnikazi womhlaba, ohlezi kulowo mhlaba ngelungelo lomthetho kanye nabanye abathintekayo.
Fakela umbiko wokukhulumisana kuMininjela weRijini ezinsukwini ezingu-30.
Uma zonke lezi zidingo kuhlangatshezwane nazo, uRegional Manager uzofakela isicelo kuNgqongqoshe ukuze asibonelele.

Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ukulalelwa kodaba lokubuyisana kwingxabano, yinqubo lapho khona ukhomishana (noma ithimba, kusimo somkhandlu wengxoxo phakathi kwabaqashi nabasebenzi noma i-agency) ehlangana nazo zonke izinhlangothi ezithintekayo kwingxabano ukubheka izindlela zokuxazulula ingxabano ngesivumelwano.
Inqubo ye-conciliation-arbitration (con-arb) ixazulula ingxabano masishane phakathi komqashi nomsebenzi lapho khona kube nokuxoshwa okungalandelanga inqubo yomthetho, ngokubuyisana nokuthatha isinqumo sokulamula. Le nqubo isiza ngokubuyisana (conciliation) nokuthathwa kwesinqumo sokulamula ukuthi kwenzeke inqubo ngendlela eqhubekela phambili ngalo lolo suku, uma kunesidingo.
Ukubuyisana (conciliation) kuxazulula ingxabano masishane futhi ngendlela elungile. Inqubo yokubuyisana (conciliation) ayinasixaka-xaka, ayibizi ngezimali, nakhona ayidingi ukuthi kube nabameli bomthetho. Isinqumo sokuxazulula inkinga sisezandleni zezinhlangothi ezithintekayo.
Kumhlangano wokubuyisana, umqashi angavela yena siqu noma angamelwa ngu-director noma omunye umsebenzi noma inhlangano yomqashi. Umsebenzi angamelwa ngonesikhundla kwinyunyane (office-bearer) noma isikhulu senyunyane ebhalisiwe.
Uma umenzi wesicelo ehluleka ukuvela kwinkundla yokulalelwa kodaba lokubuyisana ngesikhathi esibekiwe, udaba luzovele luhlakazwe. Uma umenzi wesicelo efisa ukuqhubeka, kumele enze isicelo sokukhanselwa kokuhlakazwa kodaba (rescission of the dismissal).
Phezulu

Ezokuthutha nokuhamba
Isicelo sokubuyiselwa imali yelayisense
Isicelo sokubuyiselwa imali yelayisense
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Umnikazi wemoto entshontshiwe, noma engasakwazi ukusetshenziswa unomphela, noma ekhishwe kuhla lokubhaliswa, angenza isicelo sokubuyiselwa imali yelayisense ayikhokha maqondana nelayisense yaleyo moto.
Isicelo sokubuyisela iamli kumele senziwe ezinyangeni ezintathu ukusukela ngosuku lapho okwaba nenothisi yokuntshontshwa noma ukulimala unomphela kwemoto, noma ukusukela ngosuku eyakhishwa ngalo kuhla lobhaliso.
Qaphela: Inani lemali engaphansi kuka-R30 ngeke yabuyiselwa.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo le-RLF.
Khombisa uMazisi wakho (i-ID yakho).
Nikeza ngobufakazi obuqinisekisa ukuthi imoto intshontshiwe noma ayisakwazi ukusebenza unomphela, noma unikeza isitifikedi sokukhishwa kuhla lobhaliso (deregistration certificate).

Izincazelo
Awukho umkhumbi ozobhaliswa ngaphandle kwelungelo lokudoba kanye nephomedi yokwenza imisebenzi ephathelene nokudoba.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yenza amalungiselo okuthi umkhumbi uyohlolwa ukuthi ulungele yini ukuthi uhambe emanzini, emva kwaloko abakwa SAMSA bazokhipha isitifiketi sokuphepha. Lesitifiketi sizohambisana namaphepha akho akubhalisa.
Gcwalisa ifomu lakho ngesandla usebenzise amagama amakhulu ufake nezizathu lapho kudingekile.
Nikeza nalolwazi olulandelayo:
o igama lomkhumbi o igama nemininingwane yomnikazi (ufake nokuthi amasheya eme kanjani)
o ubude bomkhumbi o amandla nokuthi uthwala kangakanani o ifulegi lezwe umkhumbi obhaliswe ngaphansi kwalo (noma uqashwe noma abanikazi bahlanganyele nabantu abangasibo base South Africa)
Thumela isicelo sakho kanye nelungelo lakho lokudoba noma ikhontilaka yakho nomnikazi womkhumbi yokusebenzisa umkhumbi wakhe uma ungenawo amalungelo okudoba.

 (Umthetho kaZwelonke wokuphathwa kwesimo seNdalo)

Isicelo sakho singathatha izinsuku ezintathu noma ngaphezulu kuya ngokuthi amaphepha aphelele yini.

Izincazelo
Ngisho noma umthetho wezinkampani i-Companies Act, 1973, udinga ukuthi kube nemihlangano ephoqelekile yezinkampani, ikakhulukazi imihlangano kawonkewonke, asikho lesi sidingo kuma-CC. Imihlangano ivame ukubanjwa nje phakathi kwamalunga ngokubona kwabo noma kunini.
Asikho isidingo sokuthi onke amalunga e-CC adlale indima ebonakalayo njalo(active role) ekuqhubeni ibhizinisi. Kuma-CC amaningi, kuvame ukuthi amalunga abuye abengomininjela bebhizinisi.
Okubalulekile maqondana nokugcwaliswa kwefomu kufakelwe ngemuva kwekhasi 1 lefomu ye-CK1.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa kahle ngokucophelele ifomu le-CK1 noma ubhale kulo ngo-inki omnyama ngokusebenzisa amagama amakhulu.
Fakela amakhophi aqinisekisiwe amadokhumende omazisi wamalunga athintekayo.

Isicelo sokubhalisa ilungelo lobunikazi
Isicelo sokubhalisa ilungelo lobunikazi (copyright)
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ilungelo lobunikazi, yilungelo lokuvikela umsebenzi wakho okungenzeka kube yimibhalo nezincwadi, izinhlelo zamakhompyutha (computer programmes), imisebenzi yobuciko, imetheriyali yokusakaza, umculo, amafilimu noma amavidiyo.
Ukuvikela" kusho ukuvimbela abanye ekukopeni, noma ekukhiqizeni futhi lowo msebenzi noma imetheriyali, kanye nokuyisebenzisa (ngaphandle kwemvume yakho) ukuqhubela phambili inzuzo yabo kwezezimali. Umthetho wezamalungelo obunikazi i-Copyright Act, 1978, ubeka ilungelo lobunikazi.
Uma unombono owuqalile original idea, ngeke wakwazi ukuba nelungelo kuwo Kumele uqale ufake lowo mbono ngendlela ebambekayo physical form efana nebhuku incwadi, ikhadi, i-CD, isithombe esipendiwe isithombe esithathiwe picture, umdlalo odlala kwisikrini screenplay noma ifilimu.
Ivamisa yabantu ababhalile, abaprintile, abashicilele, abadlale oshashalazini (performed) abenze umfanekiso obaziwe noma obunjiwe (sculpured), abapendile, abenze ifilimu noma abarekhode umsebenzi, yibona abavele babenelungelo lobunikazi kulowo msebenzi. Kodwa-ke ngezinye izikhathi, lapho umuntu ebenikezwe ubuthunywa balowo msebenzi (commissioned) nakhona bekhokhelwe ukwenza leyo ngxenye yomsebenzi, ilungelo ngelomqashi obathumele ukwenza lowo msebenzi.
Kwimisebenzi eminingi (ngaphandle kwamafilimu) asikho isidingo sokwenza isicelo selungelo lobunikazi.
Amalungelo obunikazi bamafilimu, noma amavidiyo enzelwe inzuzo kwezezimali, kumele kwenziwe isicelo ngokubhala, ngokufakela amadokhumende alandelayo, atholakala mahhala ehhovisi lamalungelo obunikazi e-Copyright IHhovisi:
Ukuba ne-copyright kunikeza ilungelo "lobunikazi" kuleyo metheriyali kanye nolawulo lokusetshenziswa kwayo ngenhloso yokuzuza izimali. Uphawu lwe-copyright oluvamise ukubonwa kakhulu yilolu olunjena: Â©.

Izincazelo
Empeleni i-"design" imaqondana nomumo (isheyiphu) kanye nendlela into ebonakala iyiyo (features) okukhanga iso. Amanye amadizayini abangelwa ngumsebenzi wento kanye amanye ngawobuhle bokuhlobisa.
Kukhona izinhlobo ezimbili zamadizayini ezingabhaliswa (ezingarejistwa), kodwa uma zihlangabezane nezidingo ezifakelwe lapha ngezansi:
kumele ibe ngentsha nengavamile;
Ngisho noma kungaphoqelekile ukuthi kwenziwe ucwaningo lokuhlola ubusha nokuba ngeyokuqala idizayini, noma ukuqinisekisa ukuthi akukabi nenye idizayini efanayo noma ecishe ifane, ebhalisiwe ngaphambili, kungcono ukwenza kanjalo ngaphambi kokwenza isiceo sobhaliso (serejistreshini). Ukufunisisa ngedizayini kungenziwa ehhovisi le-Design Registry ePitoli. Kumele kwenziwe ngumuntu qobo noma i-agent, efane nommeli wama-patent.
Uma idizayini isiyamukelwe njengentsha yabhaliswa (yarejistwa), uzonikezwa isitifikedi sedizayini.
Amadizayini avuselelwa njalo eminyakeni emithathu.

Isicelo sokubhalisa sokurejista inkampani yangapha
Isicelo sokubhalisa (sokurejista) inkampani yangaphandle
Izincazelo
Inhlangano yangaphandle kweNingizimu Afrika efisa ukuqhuba ibhizinisi eNingizimu Afrika ingenza isicelo semvume yenkampani yangaphandle. Inkampani yangaphandle, yinkampani ebhalise (erejiste) kwizwe langaphandle nakhona efisa ukusungula inkampani eNingizimu Afrika.
Sicela uqaphele ukuthi onke amadokhumende kumele agcwaliswe ngo-inki ojulile omnyama.
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa amafomu akuhla olufakelwe kwisihloko esithi: "Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe" lapha ngezansi.
Fakela amakhophi ezitifikedi eziqinisekisiwe zamadokhumende omazisi noma amapasipoti abo bonke abantu abathintekayo kwinkampani ezobhaliswa (ezorejistiwa).
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ubhaliso (irejistreshini) ingathatha okungenani izinsuku ezinhlanu.

Izincazelo
Abantu nje kanye nezinhlangano bangasifaka isicelo senombholo yokubhaliswa kwemoto ezibiza bona uqobo uma befuna ukusebenzisa amagama abo noma amagama ezinhlangano ukuthi zibe yinombholo yokubhaliswa kwemoto inombholo ebiza wena uqobo iboniswa njengezinombholo ezejwayelekile. Inombholo iba ngeyomuntu ogama lakhe libhalisiwe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thintana nehhovisi eliphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
Thumela ikhophi yomazisi wakho enesitembu samaphoyisa kanya nenombholo yokubhaliswa yemoto.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.

Izincazelo
Ukwenza umsebenzi weprofeshini
Ukuba nesimo seprofeshini kanye nokuthola inzuzo enikezwa zikhungo zezezimali
Ukuthola ukusekelwa ngabe-SANASP kanye negaranti (isiqinisekiso) sekhono lakho
Ukuvikela kanye nokuqinisa iprofeshini
Ngabe yini izidingo zerejistreshini (zobhaliso)?
Ubhaliso (irejistreshini) njengososayense weprofeshini yesayense yemvelo - kumele ube nedigri (iziqu)/ idiploma eyenziwa iminyaka emine kanye nesipiliyoni seminyaka emithathu
Ubhaliso (irejistreshini) njengososayense osethwasile wathola isitifikedi (certified) wesayense yemvelo - kumele ube nedigri (iziqu)/ idiploma eyenziwa iminyaka emithathu kanye nesipiliyoni sonyaka owodwa
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo elitholakala kwaboMkhandlu
Fakela imali ebekiwe okumele ikhokhelwe ihambisane nesicelo sakho. Umkhandlu uzokurejista (uzokubhalisa) ebese ukhipha isitifikedi serejistreshini, uma weneliseka ukuthi unemfundo edingekayo nefanele kanye nesipiliyoni njengoba sibekwe ngoboMkhandlu.
Umphumela wohlolo ngeke waziswa kuze kube ngemuva komhlangano woMkhandlu.
Umkhandlu ungala ukurejista umenzi wesicelo ngaphansi kwezimo ezilandelayo:
Uma, ngemuva kokusungula koMthethosisekelo weRiphabhuliki yeNingizimu Afrika, 1996, umenzi wesicelo watholakala enecala eRiphabhuliki noma kwenye indawo nakhona agwetshwa isikhathi esingaphezulu kwezinyanga ezintathu noma ifayini njengendlela yokugwetshwa.
Uma iNkantolo ePhakeme inqume ukuthi umenzi wesicelo unengqondo engathathi kahle noma unezifo zokwengqondo, noma uma efakwe esikhungweni sokusangana kwengqondo ngaphansi komthetho we-Mental Health Act, 1973 (Act 17 of 1973).
Uma irejistreshini (ubhaliso) lomuntu lumisiwe ngenxa yesijeziso asinikeziwe ngaphansi kwalo Mthetho.
Uma umuntu ekhishwa esikhundleni sokwethembeka ngenxa yokungaziphathi kahle.
Uma umuntu etholwe yinkantolo njengohluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu nakhona engakabuyiselwa esimeni esihle ngezikweledu (non-rehabilitated insolvent), kanti ukuhluleka kwakhe ukukhokha izikweledu (insolvency) kwabangelwa wubudedengu bakhe ekwenzeni umsebenzi owela ngaphansi komkhakha enza isicelo seresjistreshini (sobhaliso) kuwo.
Umkhandlu uzokwazisa ngokubhala uma isicelo sakho sobhaliso (serejistreshini) sinqatshelwe.
Ungenza i-aphili ngesinqumo soMkhandlu kungakapheli izinsuku ezingu-30.
Kumele wenze isicelo kuMkhandlu, sokuvuselelwa kwerejistreshini (kobhaliso) okungenani, kusenezinyanga ezintathu ngaphambi kokuphela kwesikhathi sobhaliso (kwerejistreshini) yakho.

Izincazelo
noma yimuphi umuntu (ngaphandle kwenkampani, noma umuntu okhishiwe phansi kwezibopho zokukhokha intela yesikhashana) othola ingeniso (income) kodwa elingewona umholo (remuneration)
Lapho inkampani ingakadonsi intela yabasebenzi kudayirektha, ngeke akwazi ukubonelela leyo ntela yomsebenzi lapho ebalela imali yentela okumele ikhokhelwe.
Umuntu okumele akhokhele intela yesikhashana, kumele kuthi kungakapheli izinsuku ezingu-30 ngemuva kokuba efanele ayikhokhe, enze isicelo ngokubhala, isicelo sobhaliso (serejistreshini) ehhovisi legatsha le-SARS eliseduzane. Ukuhluleka ukwenza njalo, kuyoba nomphumela wokukhokhiswa inzalo (i-interest) kanye nama-penalty ngokukhokha sekwedlule isikhathi, kanye nenye futhi intela ehambisana namafomu okukhokhelwa kwentela ngokukhokha kamuva sekwedlule isikhathi.
Abantu abalandelayo asikho isidingo sokuthi bakhokhe intela yesikhashana (provisional tax):
noma yimuphi umuntu ingeniso (income) lakhe elitholakala ngomholo kuphela noma yimuphi umuntu ingeniso lakhe elingeqi ngaphezu kwenani lomkhawulo wokukhokhwa kwentela elibekiwe (tax threshold), okuyinani elingu-R40 000 kubantu abangaphansi kweminyaka engu-65 ubudala, kanye nenani lika-R65 000 kubantu abaneminyaka engaphezulu kuka-65 ubudala noma yimuphi umuntu othola ingeniso lezimali ezitshaliwe elingeqi ku-R16 500, ngaphandle kwengeniso elitholakaka kumholo, kubantu abaneminyaka engaphansi kuka-65 ubudala noma u-R24 500 kubantu abaneminyaka engaphezulu kuka-65 ubudala. Inzalo ebuya kwizimali ezitshalwa ngaphandle (interest) kanye nezimali zamasheya (dividends), bakhishwa ngaphansi kwesibopho ukufikela kumkhawulo ka-R2 500 kuzo zonke izibopho noma yimuphi umuntu oneminyaka engu-65 ubudala noma ngaphezulu kwayo, uyakhishwa kwisibopho sokukhokhela intela yesikhashana, uma:
o ingeniso lemali ekhokhelwa intela kunyaka wentela lingeqi kwinani lika-R80 000, nakhona libandakanya umholo, impesheni, inzalo (interest) kanye nezimali zamasheya (dividends) noma ingeniso elibuya kwizimali zerente kanti futhi o yena engeyena udayirektha wenkampani yangasese noma ilunga le-close corporation kanti futhi o yena engaqhubi noma yiliphi ibhizinisi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Fakela isicelo sokubhaliswa (sokurejistwa) njengomkhokhi wentela wesikhashana (provisional taxpayer) ehhovisi legatsha le-SARS eliseduzane nawe ngencwadi. Incwadi kumele ichaze usuku owafanela ngalo ukukhokhela intela yesikhashana.

Isicelo sokubhalisa (sokurejista) ubunikazi bophawu lwenkampani
Izincazelo
Uphawu lwenkampani yi-brand name, isilogeni (isiqubulo) noma ilogo. Luphawula izinkonzo (amasevisi) izimpahla zomuntu oyedwa kanye nokwenza ukuthi impahla nezinkonzo (amasevisi) ahluke kwamanye. Isibonelo sibandakanya:
Ilogo: Uphawu lwe-Nike olunjena: "v" noma uphawu lwakwa-MacDonalds olunjena: "M"
Isheyiphu ethize: Yebhodlela le-Coca Cola
Ngakho-ke i-brand name yigama noma inhlanganisela yamagama (isibonelo: Kentucky Fried Chicken). Isilogeni (isiqubulo) wumusho omfishane noma isenthense kanye nelogo eyisithombe esithize esehlukile kwezinye noma uphawu. Kukhombisa ubunjalo obehlukile nobuqondana naleyo nkampani kwezezimakethe kanti futhi kusebenza kwimikhiqizo kanye nezinkonzo (amasevisi).
Uma uphawu lobunikazi (i-brand name, isilogeni noma ilogo) sekubhalisiwe (kurejistiwe), akekho omunye umuntu ongasebenzisa lolu phawu lobunikazi, noma olunye olufana nalo. Uma lokhu, kwenzeka, kungathathwa izinyathelo zomthetho. Kuke kwaba namacala adumile ezinkantolo zeNingizimu Afrika, afana nalapho inkampani yakwa-MacDonalds e-USA ike yamangalela inkampani yaseNingizimu Afrika eyayisebenzisa uphawu olufana nolwabo lwe-"M".
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thola ifomu le-TM1 ebese uligcwalisa ngamathathu. Amakhophi amabili azosetshenziswa yihhovisi kanti elesithathu kumele uzigcinele lona njengobufakazi besicelo sakho.
Khokhela imali yobhaliso (yerejistreshini) ngaphambili, noma lapho ufakele isicelo.
Bhala phansi izimpahla noma izinkonzo (amasevisi) ofuna ukusebenzisa uphawu lobunikazi kuzo, ku-field 57 wefomu le-TM1.
Cophelela kakhulu ukuze ungafakeli izimpahla noma amasevisi (izinkonzo) zakwamanye amaklasi kuphawu lobunikazi olulodwa kwisicelo. Uma wenza lokho, uzokwelulekwa ukuthi uchibiyele incazelo uukbandakanya lezo zimpahla kuphela noma lawo masevisi (lezo zinkonzo) kuphela achazwe kuleyo klasi kuphela. Kokunye, kuzomele ufakele isicelo esiseceleni kabusha.
Uma isicelo sakho sesihambisane nazo zonke izidingo zokosiko, uzonikezwa usuku isicelo sakho esibekelwe lona kanye nenombolo. Konke ukuxhumana nehhovisi kuzoba nenombolo yaleso sicelo. Ngisho noma kuthatha izinyanga ezingu-18 ukubhalisa (ukurejista) ubunikazi bophawu, ungaqala ukulusebenzisa maduzane nje lapho usuthole khona inombolo yomenzi wesicelo (applicant number).
Uma ubunikazi bophawu lwakho lwamukelwa ngaphandle kwemibandlela, ungakhangisa ngalo kwi-Patent Journal, ekhishwa nyanga zonke ngabaPrinti bakaHulumeni.
Abantu emiphakathini banikezwa izinyanga ezintathu zokuthi baphikisane nesicelo (lodge objections).
Ungashayela isikhungo sezokuxhumana ne-dti kunombolo ethi: (0861 84 33 84).
Khokha ebhangi ngokusebenzisa ikhodi yekhastama.
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha iminyaka emibili ukuze ubhaliso (irejistreshini) yobunikazi bophawu kuphethwe ngabo.
Izindleko
Ngu-R85 ukufunsisa ngobunikazi bezimpawu obukhona

Isicelo sobhaliso lwesiteshi sokuhlola izimoto
Ukufaka isicelo sokubhalisa isiteshi sokuhlola izimoto
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Isiteshi sokuhlola izimoto kuyisenta ehlola izimoto ibesikhipha isitifiketi sobufakazi bokuthi leyomoto inelungelo lokusebenzisa imigwaqo yomphakathi. Kuneziteshi zangasese abantu abazakhele zona, kanti futhi kuneziteshi ezilawulwa nguhulumeni. Zonke Iziteshi sokuhlola izimoto kumele ukuthi zibhaliswe zinikezwe izigaba ngaphambi kokuthi zikhipha izitifiketi zobufakazi bokuthi izimoto zinelungelo lokusebenzisa imigwaqo yomphakathi. Kuyothi ngenkathi bathola incwadi yokufaka isicelo sokubhalisa, abegatsha lezothutho besifundazwe, bazothumela abahloli ukuyohlola leyondawo efuna ukubhaliswa ukuthi ngabe isezingeni elifanele yini ukuthi ibhaliswe ngokuya ngomthetho wakwa SABS.
Izinga elifunekayo yimishini eyiyona yokuhlola izimoto, kanye nemfundo abafunde bafika kuyo labo abahlola izimoto.
Iziteshi zokuhlola izimoto zigunyazwe ukuhlola izimoto ezisesigabeni abanikezwe sona kuphela.
Ukuze ubhalise isiteshi sokuhlola izimoto sangasese, thintana nehhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe, bakunikeze ulwazi olunzulu.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu TS1, elokufaka isicelo sokubhalisa isiteshi sokuhlola izimoto ehhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
Thumela incwadi yomazisi wakho kanye nomazisi walabo abaphethe leyondawo oyibhalisayo.
Kungenzeka ucelwe ukuthi uthumele amanye amaphepha njengokusho kwelungu lesigungu sekhansela (MEC).
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Ezobudlelwane babaqashi nabasebenzi zinyunyane nezinhlangano zabaqashi
Isicelo sokubhalisa sokurejista njengomkhandlu wez
Isimo
Imikhandlu yezingxoxo phakathi kwabasebenzi nabaqashi (barganing councils) ibhekana nezivumelwano ezihlanganyelwe, ukuxazulula izingxabano zasemisebenzini phakathi kwabaqashi nabasebenzi, ukusungula izikimu ezehlukene, kanye nokuphawula ngemigomo kanye nemithetho yezemisebenzi.
Izinyunyane zabasebenzi nezinhlangano zabaqashi zingenza isicelo sokusungula imikhandlu yezingxoxo phakathi kwabaqashi nabasebenzi.
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi, hamba uye kwaboMnyango wezemiSebenzi.
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Amaminithi omhlangano lapho izinyunyane nezinhlangano zabaqashi abavumelane khona ukusungula umkhandlu wezingxoxo phakathi kwabaqashi nabasebenzi
Noma yiluphi olunye ulwazi olufanele
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha izinsuku ezingu-60 ukubhekana nesicelo senu.

Izincazelo
Isicelo sokubhalisa isenta yokuvivinya abafuna ilayisensi yokushayela kumele ibhalwe kwifomu elibekiwe. Kuzodingeka ukuthi usho ukuthi lomsebenzi wokuvivinya abanye uzowenziwa ngubani, nokuthi ngobani abazomela abaphathi bendawo

Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thumela lamaphepha alandelayo:
o omazisi babomenijeni besenta kanye nalabo abazabe benza umsebenzi wokuvivinya o isitifiketi sokubhaliswa kwebhizinisi.

Izimvume namalayisense
Ukuthuthukiswa kwezilwane nemfuyo ngenisa ngaphakathi ezweni imetheriyali yejenethik
Isicelo sobhaliso (serejistreshini) njenge-agent yokungenisa ngaphakathi ezweni imetheriyali yejenethiki yezilwane
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
I-agent engenisa ngaphakathi ezweni kumele ilandele lokhu okungezansi:
ifasilithi kumele ibe nayo yonke imishini esetshenziswa ukuphatha imetheriyali yejenethiki yezilwane ilabhorethri kanye nefasilithi ezobhekana nezentela okumele ilandele ubuncane bamazinga abekiwe abaqashi kumele babenekhono ngokuphatha imbewu yesilisa (semen).

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Izinhlangano ezingasebenzeli inzuzo (non-profit organisations) zinkampani ezibhalisele (ezirejistele) ukuhlinzeka ngezinkonzo (ngamasevisi) noma ukuqhubela phambili izidingo zemiphakathi kanti azihlosile ukwenza, noma ukuhlolwa ngenzuzo (ngeprofiti) eziyenzayo. Izinhlangano eziningi ezingekho ngaphansi kukahulumeni zama-NGO, zinhlangano zezinkonzo, kanye nezinhlangano zomhawu noma zozwelo (charity) ziwela kulo mkhakha.
Ngokuvamile, izinhlangano ezingasebenzeli inzuzo zinikeza izinkonzo (amasevisi) emiphakathini ehlukene, njengezikimu zokudla kwezingane, izinhlangano ezinakekela izintandane ngenxa ye-AIDS, izinhlangano zezinkolo, nezinye. Ngisho noma yiyiphi imali eziyenzayo nezibanayo ngemuva kokukhokhela izindleko zokusebenza kwazo, zisetshenziselwa ukunweba nokungezela izinkonzo (amasevisi) azo.

Izincazelo
Noma ngubani ofuna ukugcina inyathi, kumele aqale abhalise umhlaba noMnyango weZoLimo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thola ifomu lesicelo kwi-website, ebese uligcwalisa ngokuprinta. Lingatholakala nakwihhovisi lendawo yempilo (kadokotela) wezilwane kahulumeni.
Cela udokotela wezilwane kahulumeni oseduzane nawe ukuthi ahlole umhlaba kanye nothango (nomfense), kanye nokuqinisekisa isimo sezifo senyathi.
Inhloko yezinkonzo zempilo yezilwane kwisifundazwe (kwiprovinsi) kumele ufakezele ukuthi kukhona imithombo efanele yokuqapha isimo epulazini njalo-njalo, nokuthi ukuhamba-hamba kwenyathi epulazini kanye nangaphandle kwepulazi kungaphathwa kahle ngendlela efanele nokuthi kuzokwazeka ukuthi kulandelelwe ngokushesha yonke into okungenzeka ihambe ngendlela engavamile.
Isicelo kumele sihambisane nokulandelayo: 
o isitifikedi esigunyazisiwe sokubiyelwa kahle ngendlela efanele, esikhishwa ziphathimandla zokugcinwa kwemvelo/kwendalo o ikhophi yemephu (yebalazwe) ekhombisa umkhawulo wawo wonke umhlaba okwenziwa isicelo sobhaliso lwawo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isikhathi esithathwayo ukubonelela isicelo nokwamukelwa kwaso kuyahluka kusifundazwe ngesifundazwe (kuprovinsi ngeprovinsi). Uma isicelo sesamukelwe, sithunyelwa kuMnyango kazwelonke wezoLimo.
Izikhulu kumnyango kazwelonke, zinikeza ngenombolo yobhaliso (yerejistreshini), zingezela ulwazi kwirejista yezinyathi kuzwelonke kanye nokuprinta isitifikedi serejistreshini esinikezwa umnikazi womhlaba. Le nqubo ingathatha izinsuku ezilishumi zokusebenza.

Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
