Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ukuze ukwazi ukuvota okhethweni lwezwe lonkana, noma lwesifundazwe, noma lukahulumeni wasekhaya, umele ukuthi ubhalise endaweni ozovotela kuyona lapho uhlala khona. Lokhu kubaluleke kakhulu okhethweni lukahulumeni wasekhaya ngoba umele ukuthi uvote endaweni lapho ubhalise khona. Uma usubhalisile nemininingwane yakho isihloliwe ebhukwini okubhalwa kulona bonke abantu baseNingizimu Afrika, igama lakho lizokubhalwa ohlwini lwabavoti bezwe lonke.
Ukuze ubenelungelo lokubhalisa kumele ukuthi:
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Bhalisa wena ngesiqu esiteshini sokuvota endaweni lapho uhlala khona ngesikhathi wonke-wonke abhalisela ukuvota.
Bhalisa noma nini ngesikhathi somsebenzi kukhomishane ezimele yokhetho eseduze nawe.
Ubophatha incwadi yakho yomazisi.
Gcwalisa ifomu elifanele.
Umsebenzi kahulumeni ophathelene nokubhalisa uzakunamathisela ipheshana elinenombholo yesigodi sokhetho saleyo ndawo encwadini yakho yomazisi.

Isimo
Inyunyane yinhlangano yabasebenzi eqhubela phambili nokuvikela izidingo zamalunga ayo kwizinto eziphathelene nemiholo kanye nezimo okusetshenzwa ngaphansi kwazo, ikakhulukazi ngezingxoxo nabaqashi.
Uhla olunemininingwane yezikhulu zenyunyane, oluchaza izikhundla zabo
Ikheli lamahhovisi abhalisiwe enyunyane
Ikhophi nekhophi yomthethosisekelo wenyunyane kumele isayinwe nguNobhala kanye noSihlalo ukuqinisekisa ukuthi amakhophi angempela omthethosisekelo.
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi, hamba uye kwaboMnyango wezemiSebenzi
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Gcwalisa amakhophi amabili efomu lesicelo.

Isicelo sokubhalisela ukusetshenziswa kwamanzi
Isicelo sokubhalisela ukusetshenziswa kwamanzi
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Bonke abasebenzisi bamanzi, amanzi abo angaveli kumnikezi wenkonzo (service provider), uhulumeni wendawo, ibhodi yamanzi, ibhodi yamanzi emisele yezokunisela (irrigation scheme), isikimu samanzi kahulumeni, noma abanye abasaplaya ngomthamo omkhulu wamanzi (bulk supplier), kumele babhalise ukusebenzisa kwabo amanzi noMnyango wezaManzi namaHlathi (DWAF). Laba basebenzisi kumele babengabasebenzisa amanzi ukwenza okulandelayo:
ukunisela (ngemisele)
izinhloso zomsebenzi wemayini ukusebenzisela izimboni
Okunye ukusetshenziswa kwamanzi okumele kubhaliswe, kubandakanya okulandelayo:
o ukuchithwa kodoti noma amanzi anodoti o ukuphambukisa imifula kanye nemisele, noma ukwenza umehluko ngendlela umfula ogeleza ngayo nokungenela kwenjula yomfula (alterations to river beds) noma kumazibuko emifula noma indlela umfula obukeka ngayo.
Ukugcina amanzi - Noma yimuphi umuntu ogcina amanzi nganoma yiyiphi inhloso (okubandakanya ukunisela ngemisele, ukuwasebenzisela ekhaya, ukuwasebenzisela imboni, ukuwasebenzisela imayini, izinto ezitshalwa noma ezifuywa emanzini, izinhlanzi, imidlalo yamanzi, ukuhlobisa ngamanzi, izingadi, ukuhlobisa ingadi noma ipaki (landscaping), ukwenza igalofu endaweni, nokunye okunjalo), ukusukela kunqenqema lwendawo, amanzi angaphansi komhlaba, noma umthombo oyisisusa (fountain), ogeleza ngomthamo ongaphezulu kuka-10 000 cubic meters, noma lpaho khona amanzi asaplaywa endaweni engahezulu kukahekthere eyodwa yomnikazi noma umuntu ohlala khona, noma inhlangano, iphemithi noma imvume (permit or permission).
Imisebenzi ephungula ukugeleza kwemisele (Afforestation) - Konke ukungezelwa kwehlathi (ukutshala izihlahla) ngenhloso yentengiso, okubandakanya izihlahla zemiphakathi (communal forestry) ngenhloso yentengiso, okwenzeka ngaphambi kukanyaka ka-1972, kumele kubhaliswe.
Ukusetshenziswa ngohulumeni bezindawo kanye nabasaplayi bomthamo omkhulu ngemithombo yabo yamanzi, kanye nemisebenzi yokuhlanzekisa (purification works).
Imisebenzi elawulwayo (controlled activities) efana nokunisela ngamanzi kadoti, ukwenza amandla kagesi ngamanzi, ukuguqula isimo somoya (atmospheric modification), noma ukushaja futhi unqenqema ledwala noma unqenqema lomhlabathi elimunca amanzi (aquifer).
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Ukuqapha (ukubeka) ukusetshenziswa kwamanzi okuqondene kanye.
Ukuxhumana nehhovisi lerijini laboMnyango wezaManzi namaHlathi. 
Thumela amafomu ehhovisi eliseduzane lerijini le-DWAF kanye namadokhumende alandelayo: 
o imininingwane yomenzi wesicelo o indawo lapho okuzosetshenziswa khona amanzi o imininingwane yomnikazi wendawo (weprophathi)
o nikeza ngekhophi yesitifikedi esiqinisekisiwe sikaMazisi wakho (se-ID) yakho o ikhophi eqinisekisiwe yetayitela leprophathi (lempahla)
Phezulu

Imfundo noqeqesho
Izikhungo zemfundo ephakeme
Isicelo sokubhalisa njengososayense weprofesheni yesayense yemvelo kumkhakha weSayense ye-Forensic
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
ukusebenza emkhakheni we-consultancy ukuba nesimo seprofeshini kanye nokuthola inzuzo enikezwa zikhungo zezezimali ukuthola ukusekelwa wuMkhandlu kanye negaranti (isiqinisekiso) esingavuni sekhono lakho ukuvikela kanye nokuqinisa iprofeshini.
Ukuze ukhwalifaye (ukufanele) ukubhalisa (ukurejista) ukusebenza emkhakheni weSayense ye-Forensic, kudingeka uhlangabezane nokulandelayo:
Okungenani u-50% wezifundo kuhlelo kumele kube ngezibonwa njengezifundo zesayense yezemvelo.
Olunye uqeqesho:
Kumele unikeze ubufakazi boqeqesho kwiSayense ye-Forensic noma yophenyisiso (yama-Investigation), isibonelo, ukuhlola amadokhumende, ukuphenyisisa ngomlilo, ukuphenyisisa izimpawu zamathuluzi, ukuphenyisisa kobushushisi, inqubo yomthetho nezinkantolo, ufakazi wobuchwepheshe, noma ezinye izifundo ezifishane ezigunyaziwe.
Kumele ube nomlando (nerekhodi) lomsebenzi oqondene nesayense ye-forensic kanye nocwaningo.
Izibhamu:
o ukuhlola kanye nokubeka ngezinhlozo zazo izikhali/ izinhlam o ukuxhumanisa isikhali nobugebengu, ukunaka indawo okwenzeke kuyo ubugebengu kanye nokuhlola ubufakazi be-forensic.
Ukuphenyisisa ingozi:
Usekelo lobuchwepheshe ekuphenyisisweni kwezingozi zasemgwaqeni, ukuhlanganisa indlela izinto ezenzeke ngayo okuholele ekungqubuzaneni, ukucwaninga okunemininingane kwezingxenye zemoto, ulwazi oluthile maqondana netheknoloji yezimoto. (Amakhwalifikheshini kwi-physics noma isayense yezinto ezibambekayo (materials science) kungayinto engaba wusizo.)
Ukufundisisa amadokhumende emibuzo - ukwazi ukubona imibhalo yesandla
Ukukhulisa indawo okwenzeke kuyo isenzo kanye nokuqoqa ubufakazi
Imibhalo yezesayense
Isipiliyoni ngesimo sangaphakathi kwelabhorethri
Ulwazi lokubheka indawo okwenzeke kuyo ubugebengu ngokweluleka
Imisebenzi yokusuphavayisa uvivinyo lwezinto elabhorethri ezihambelana nobugebengu
Ubufakazi bezesayense obungavuni hlangothi enkantolo yamacala ukusekela ubushushisi noma abamele abamangalelwe kuphenyisiso bobugebengu
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Fakela imali ebekiwe okumele ikhokhelwe yerejistreshini (yobhaliso) ihambisane nesicelo.
Umkhandlu uzokurejista (uzokubhalisa) ebese ukhipha isitifikedi serejistreshini, uma weneliseka ukuthi unemfundo edingekayo nefanele kanye nesipiliyoni njengoba sibekwe ngoboMkhandlu.
Umphumela wohlolo ngeke waziswa kuze kube ngemuva komhlangano woMkhandlu.
Umkhandlu ungala ukurejista umenzi wesicelo ngaphansi kwezimo ezilandelayo:
o uma, ngemuva kokusungula koMthethosisekelo weRiphabhuliki yeNingizimu Afrika, 1996, (Act 108 of 1996) umenzi wesicelo watholakala enecala eRiphabhuliki noma kwenye indawo nakhona agwetshwa isikhathi esingaphezulu kwezinyanga ezintathu noma ifayini njengenye indlela yokugwetshwa.
o uma irejistreshini (ubhaliso) lomuntu lumisiwe ngenxa yesijeziso asinikeziwe ngaphansi kwalo Mthetho.
o uma umuntu ekhishwa esikhundleni sokwethembeka ngenxa yokungaziphathi kahle.
o uma umuntu etholwe yinkantolo njengohluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu nakhona engakabuyiselwa esimeni esihle ngezikweledu (non-rehabilitated insolvent), kanti ukuhluleka kwakhe ukukhokha izikweledu (insolvency) kwabangelwa wubudedengu bakhe ekwenzeni umsebenzi owela ngaphansi komkhakha enza isicelo seresjistreshini (sobhaliso) kuwo.
Umkhandlu uzokwazisa ngokubhala uma isicelo sakho sobhaliso (serejistreshini) sinqatshelwe.
Ungenza i-aphili ngesinqumo soMkhandlu kungakapheli izinsuku ezingu-30.
Kumele wenze isicelo kuMkhandlu, sokuvuselelwa kwerejistreshini (kobhaliso) okungenani, kusenezinyanga ezintathu ngaphambi kokuphela kwesikhathi sobhaliso (kwerejistreshini) yakho.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Phezulu
Izindleko
Ukufakela imali ngendlela ye-elektroniki kanye nokufakela imali ngokuqonde ngqo: Sicela usebenzise onobumba bokuqala bamagama (initials) kanye nesibongo ukuze kwaziwe imali ekhokhwayo ibuya kubani kwisitatimende sebhangi lethu ebese uthumela ubufakazi bokukhokha ngefekisi, kanye nekhasi lokuqala lefomu yesicelo kwinombolo ethi: 841 1057.
Imininingwane yeBhangi:
Inombolo ye-Akhawunti: 1644292289

Izincazelo
o amagama, imininingwane kamazisi kanye nesimo kwezomendo sabanikazi ababhalisile o amanani emiyalelo yenkantolo yokuvimbela ukwenziwa kokuthize (interdicts) okurekhodwe ngabantu abathize kanye nempahla (neprophathi)
o imininingwane yempahla edluliselwe kwabanye, okubandakanya izinsuku zokubhaliswa (zerejistreshini) kanye namanani entengiso o imininingwane ngamabhodi emali-mboleko yokuthenga impahla, okubandakanya amagama alabo bantu abanikezwe imali-mboleko kanye nenani lekhapitali yentengo

Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lokubhalisa kwi-inthanethi.
Cofoza kumagama athi (Submit your application) (fakela isicelo), lapho usuwenelisekile ukuthi ugcwalisa lonke ifomu ngendlela efanele.
Uzonikezwa i-user name (igama ozolisebenzisa ). Lena kuzoba yinombolo ye-akhawunti yakho kuzo zonke izinhloso ezibhekane nezemali. Uzonikezwa ne-(password) (igama ozolisebenzisa ukungena) (igama le-password), kumele ulishintshe ngokushesha ngemuva kwalokho.
Khokhela imali edingekayo ebekiwe. Imiyalelo yokusebenzisa ibhangi iveziwe kwi-i-Website.

Imali yokuphatha yasemahhovisi ngenyanga: Ngu-R10
Ukusesha (ukufuna) inani eliphansi ngu-R1 kanti eliphezulu ngu-R3 kuye ngesikali esehlayo esihambisana namanani  okusesha kwakho okwenziwa phakathi nenyanga
Ukungaphumeleli lapho usesha: Ngu-R1
Imali yokubuya uxhunyaniswe futhi (reconnection fee), uma i-akhawunti isalela emuva ngokukhokhelwa, ebese iyalengiswa (iyasaspendwa) ngemuva kwezinsuku ezingu-30: Ngu-R80

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Isicelo sokubhalisa njengomkhokhi wentela
Isicelo sokubhalisa njengomkhokhi wentela noma ukushintsha imininingwane obhalise ngayo yokukhokhela imali yentela yengeniso (yomholo)
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Uma ikheli lomuntu lishintsha ngemuva kokuba ebhalise ne-SARS njengomkhokhi wentela, kumele anikeze iSARS ikheli lakhe elisha ezinsukwini ezingu-60 ngemuva kokuba eshintshe ikheli.
Amafomu ahambisana nokukhokhelwa kweNtela yengeniso (Income tax Return) alethwa kwi-SARS ngonyaka, anaso isikhala sokubhalisa ukushintsha kwekhali, ukushintsha ibhangi, kanye neminye imininingwane yomuntu. Amanothisi amaningi akhishwa yi-SARS kanye namafomu okukhokha intela anezikhala zokufakela ulwazi ngokushintsha kwekheli. Leyo mininingwane yezinguquko zokushintsha kwekheli zifakelwa kuhla lolwazi lwe-SARS, lapho kulethwa inothisi noma amafomu.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa lonke ulwazi oludingekayo kwifomu lesicelo lapho ubhalisa njengomkhokhi wentela ne-SARS.
Qinisekisa ukuthi ulwazi maqondana nawe olufakelwa kwinothisi kanye namafomu entela eSARS, luyiqiniso. Uma ulwazi lungelona iqiniso noluqondile olufakelwe kwinothisi noma kumafomu entela, sicela ukuthi uluqondise (ululungise) ngaphambi kokubuyisela amafomu kwa-SARS.
Yenza izinquguko ngemininingwane yolwazi ngawe noma ngamakheli ngezindlela ezilandelayo:

Izincazelo
Inkampani yaphesheya ngeke yakwazi ukwenza isicelo sokubhalisa (serejistreshini) eNingizimu Afrila. Zinkampani ezibhalise lapha ezweni kuphela, isakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika, noma inhlangano esemthethweni ebhalise eNingizimu Afrika engenza isicelo sobhaliso (serejistreshini) lwemithi yezolimo.
The Registrar: U-Act 36 of 1947 kumele aziswe ngokubhala ngenhloso yokuqhuba imisebenzi yama-trial (imizamo/ ukulinga) okudingekayo ukubhalisa imithi yezolimo ukuze ahlele ukusebenza kwayo.
U-Registrar angadinga olunye ulwazi ukuze akwazi ukwenza isinqumo maqondana nokuthi ngabe imithi yamukelekile noma cha, lokhu ekwenzela izidingo zesizwe, ukufaneleka kanye nokusebenza kwayo kwezebhayoloji.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thola ifomu lesicelo kwi-website, ebese uligcwalisa ngokuphelele. Lapho kufanele, ulwazi olucelwayo lungafakelwa njengama-attachment aseceleni afakelwe izinombolo.
o ifomu lesicelo, elizofakelwa ngamathathu kanye nencwadi yencazelo ngaphezulu o ilebuli eyisiphakamiso ihambe ngamathathu o imali ebekiwe okumele ikhokhele ubhaliso (irejistreshini)
Uma imithi izongeniswa ngaphakathi, fakela okulandelayo:
o indawo lapho okuzokwenziwa khona ucwaningo lokuhlola o izithako ezikhona (active ingredients)
o noma yiluphi olunye ulwazi olungadingwa ngu-Registrar o inani lemithi (lomuthi) ozongeniswa ezweni.
Izicelo ezingagcwalisiwe ngokuphelele, zizobuyiselwa kumenzi wesicelo kanti ngeke samukela emahlombe ethu ukuphazamiseka okungabakhona.

Isicelo sobhaliso serejistreshini yelungelo lobuqa
Isicelo sobhaliso (serejistreshini) yelungelo lobuqambi bento entsha (patent)
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
I-patent inikeza uvikeleko bobuqambi kumnikazi we-patent. Ilungelo linikezwa isikhathi esinomkhawulo, okuvamise ukuba yiminyaka engu-20. Ukuvikelwa kwelungelo lobuqambi (patent protection) kusho ukuthi lobo buqambi ngeke benziwa ngenhloso yentengiso, ukusetshenziswa kwentengiso, ukusabalaliswa noma ukuthengiswa ngaphandle kwemvume yomnikazi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Fakela isicelo sesikhashana kwifomu elifanele - lokhu kungenziwa ngumenzi wesicelo ngosizo noma ngaphandle kosizo lommeli (attorney).
Fayila isicelo esigcwalisiwe - lokhu kumele kwenziwe ngommeli wobuqambi (patent attorney).
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kuthatha cishe izinyanga ezimbili zokuthi kubhaliswe (kurejistwe) ubuqambi bento entsha. Isicelo esiphelele sithatha cishe izinyanga eziyisithupha ukuthi siphethwe.

Usuku afaneleke akhokhe ngalo, ngeke lwaba ngaphambi komhlaka 4 kuJulayi 2001. Usuku afaneleka ngalo ukukhokha kubabhalisi abasha, luyoba ngosuku lapho umenzi-sicelo owafaneleka ngalo ukubhalisela iVAT.
Kumele wazise abe-SARS uma ushintsha imininingwane yebhangi.

Izincazelo
Uphoqelekile ukubhalisela (ukurejistela) i-VAT uma ingeniso olutholayo lokuthengisa impahla noma imali yokuthengisa amasevisi (izinkonzo) onikeza ngazo ingaphezulu kwenani lika-R300 000 ngonyaka noma kulindeleke ukuthi izokweqa leli nani.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Amadokhumende okumele afakelwe nesicelo ngalandelayo:
Uma kulibhizinisi langaphandle, imininingwane yomuntu oqokwe ukumela lelobhizinisi eNingizimu Afrika.
Amanye amadokhumende ngokunabile (zonke izicelo):
Isitatimende sebhangi sakamuva, isheke elikhanseliwe noma incwadi evela ebhangi eqinisekisa imininingwane yakho yebhangi laseNingizimu Afrika. Uma i-akhawunti yebhangi ingekho egameni lomthengisi, kumele kugcwaliswe ifomu le-VAT 119i.
Ilayisense yokuqhuba ibhizinisi uma ikhona kuleyo bhizinis, isibonelo, ilayisense yotshwala noma yokudweba.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Uzokwaziswa nalapho isicelo sakho singavunyelwanga.
Linda okungenani izinsuku ezingu-10 ukuthi ihhovisi legatsha lendawo libhekane nesicelo sakho. Isitifikedi sizoposelwa ekheleni olinikeze kwisicelo sobhaliso (serejistreshini) kanti futhi sizotholakala emavikini amabili isicelo sakho sivunyiwe. Abasebenzi bakwa-SARS abavunyelwe ukukwazisa ngomlomo inombolo yakho ye-VAT.

Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Izincazelo
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Xhumana nomnakekeli wempilo yezilwane kahulumeni kusifundazwe (kuprovinsi) oseduzane nawe ukwenza isicelo sokuhlola ngenhloso yokuthumela ngaphandle.
Uma isicelo samukelwa, ifasilithi iyabhaliswa kanti ebese kukhishwa isitifikedi sobhaliso sokuthunyelwa kwezilwana ngaphandle.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isikhathi esithathwayo ekuphethweni kwesicelo kuyahluka kusifundazwe ngesifundazwe (kuprovinsi ngeprovinsi).
Phezulu
Izindleko
Lokhu kuncike kwisifundazwe (kwiprovinsi).
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Amafomu awatholakali kulayini we-internethi. Xhumana nomnakekeli wempilo yezilwane kahulumeni kusifundazwe (kuprovinsi) oseduzane nawe noma uMnyango wezoLimo.

Izincazelo
Imvume yesihlobo inikezwa umuntu wangaphandle oyilunga lomndeni oseduzane wesakhamuzi noma ohlala eNingizimu Afrika unomphela. Leso sakhamuzi noma lowomhlali, kumele anikeze iziqinisekiso kwezezimali. Imvume yesihlobo isebenza isikhathi somkhawulo wokufinyelela eminyakeni emibili.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Khipha ubufakazi bokuba yilunga lomndeni elisondele kakhulu kwisakhamuzi noma umhlali osekelayo.
Fakela isiqiniseko se-chartered accountant noma ubufakazi obusekela amadokhumende aqinisa ukuthi isakhamuzi noma umhlali osekelayo unazo izimali noma inhlanganisela yezimali zokusekela eninazo. Imali yokusekela akumele ibe ngaphansi kuka-R5 000 ngenyanga kumuntu ngesikhathi sokuvakasha lokhu akusebenzi kowakwakho noma ingane encane encike kuwe.
Ungacelwa nokuthi ukhiphe elilodwa noma angaphezulu amanye alama dokhumende alandelayo:
o i-visa esebenzayo o izitifikedi zodokotela maqondana nomenzi wesicelo kanye nawo onke amalunga umndeni ahambisana nawe.
o isitifikedi sokuzalwa esiphelele (esingafingqwanga), ukufakazela ubuzali, lapho kufanele o isitifikedi somshado o ubufakazi bokuthi ninobudlelwane bobulingani noma bomshado, uma nishadile, isehlukaniso, idivosi noma bokushonelwa o ubufakazi be-adopshini (bokuthini ngeyakho ngokomthetho)
Isitifikedi samaphoyisa sokuba msulwa uma ungaphezu kweminyaka engu-21 maqondana nawo wonke amazwe alapho uke wahlala khona isikhathi esingaphezulu konyaka owodwa noma ngaphezulu kwaso, ukuthi sinikezwe ezinyangeni ezingu-12, uma singeke sanikezelwa ngaso leso sikhathi.
Imibiko yodokotela nabezama-x-ray, maqondana nomenzi wesicelo ngamunye. Imibiko yabenza ama-x-ray ngeke yadingeka ezinganeni ezingaphansi kweminyaka engu-12 noma abesimame abakhulelwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo

Izincazelo
Ilayisensi yakho eyikhadi iyaphela emva kweminyaka emihlanu wayithatha, kanti ifuna ukuvuselelwa ngalesisikhathi. Usuku iphela ngaso lubhaliwe elayisensini yakho, kodwa uzothunyelelwa isaziso sokuvuselela ngeposi
Ungayivuselela ilayisensi yakho nomanini lungakafiki usuku ephela ngalo. Ilayisensi entsha izophela emva kweminyaka emihlanu uyivuselele.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
o umazisi wakho, umazisi wokwesikhashana,noma ipasopoti yaseMzansi, kanye nelayisensi yekhadi endala.
o izithombe ezingezona zombala ezimbili zomazisi o imali enqunyiwe.
Isaziso: awungeke uvivinyelwe ukushayela kodwa uzohlolwa amehlo, uma amehlo akho angasaboni kahle, uyobekelwa eminye imithetho elayisensini yakho entsha.

Izincazelo
Zonke izimoto ezisebenzisa imigwaqo yomphakathi kumele zivuselele amalayisensi azo njalo ngomnyaka. Kungumsebenzi womnikazi wemoto ukuqinisekisa ukuthi ilayisensi yemoto yakhe iyavuselelwa. Uma ungayivuselelanga uzokujeziswa.
Abantu bazonikezwa izinsuku ezingu 21 zomusa uma bengakavuseleli ilayisensi yemoto esiphelile. Lesikhathi sibalwa kusukela ngosuku ilayisensi iphele ngalo. Ukuvuselelwa kwelayisensi kwenziwa kuziphathimandla zokubhalisa lapho yabhaliswa khona.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu I MVL1, isicelo selayisensi yemoto ihhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
Woza nomazisi wakho nencwadi yesaziso.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe ebhalwe encwadini oyithunyelwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo

Izindleko 
Imali yokufaka isicelo
Mpumalanga

Ifomu lesicelo selayisensi yemoto, MVL1, elitholakala ehhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.

Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Udokotela wezilwane angaqinisa ukuthi isilwane sivunyelwe ukuba ngumnikeli maqondana:
o nesimo sempilo ngokunabile o ukungabibikho kwesifo o iqophelo laso lembewu yesilisa.
Izicelo kumele zifike ku-Registrar kungakedluli izinsuku ezingu-30 ngaphambi kokwedlula kosuku lokuphela kwemvume. Lapho isicelo semukelwe kungakedluli izinsuku ezingu-90 ngemuva kokuphela kwemvume, kuzofuneka ukuthi kukhokhelwe enye imali okumele ikhokhelwe ukubhalisa.

Izincazelo
Ukuvuselelwa kobhaliso (kwerejistreshini) kuzonikezwa kuphela uma, ngesikhathi sobhaliso noma imvuselelo yobhaliso:
ukudidiyelwa (composition) komanyolo okukhulunywa ngakho, kungeke kwahluka kudidiyelo olubhalisiwe imininingwane kanye namagama amukelwe kwilebuli noma kumphongolo ngeke kwaguqulwa ngaphandle kwemvume ebhaliwe ka-Registrar imibandela ebekiwe ngesikhathi sobhaliso kumele ilandelwe ubhaliso okukhulunywa ngalo ngeke lwadluliselwa kwabanye osayizi bezikhwama noma bemiphongolo ngeke bashintshwa ngaphandle kwemvume ebhaliwe ebuya ku-Registrar.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Imvuselelo:
Ukukhansela:
o gcwalisa ifomu B ngamathathu uprinte ngokusebenzisa amaletha amakhulu kuphela o fakela isitifikedi sokuqala sobhaliso (serejistreshini).
Imininingwane yomenzi wesicelo:
o ifomu C kumele ligcwaliswe futhi lilethwe lihambisana nefomu A.

o gcwalisa isicelo kwi-Fomu A, ugcwalise amathathu futhi ubhale ngokuprinta kuphela ngamaletha amakhulu kuphela o isicelo kumele sithunyelwe ku: Act 36 of 1947
o usuku lokugcina lezimvuselelo ngumhlaka-30 kuJuni.

o gcwalisa iFomu B ngamathathu uprinte ngokusebenzisa amaletha amakhulu kuphela o fakela isitifikedi sokuqala (original) sobhaliso (serejistreshini).

Izincazelo
Abantu baseNingizimu Afrika abanenhloso yokwenza isicelo sokuba yizakhamizi zakwelinye izwe, elingavumeli ukuthi abantu babenamalungelo okuba yizakhamizi zamazwe amabili, kumele benze isicelo sokulahla ilungelo lokuba yisakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika. Lokhu kusho ukuthi ngeke usaba yisakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika ukusukela ngaleso sikhathi isicelo sakho samukelwa (sivunywa). Izingane ezincane ngeminyaka nazo ngeke zisaba yizakhamizi zaseNingizimu Afrika ngaphandle kokuba abazali bazo beselokhu beyisakhamizi zaseNingizimu Afrika.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa amafomu e-BI-246, BI-529, kanye no-BI-9 kunoma yiliphi ihhovisi lezaseKhaya (leZangaphakathi). Yenza isicelo kwinxusa (embassy) noma i-mission uma ungaphandle kwezwe.
Fakela onke amadokhumende omazisi aseNingizimu Afrika (isitifikedi sokuzalwa, idokhumende kamazisi, ipasipoti, isitifikedi somshado, kanjalo njalo) ehhovisi lezindaba zaseKhaya (leZangaphakathi), izindlu zamanxusa, noma i-mission.
Fakela izithombe ezimbili zosayizi kamazisi.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Le nkonzo (le sevisi) ingathatha amaviki angu-8 ukuya kumaviki angu-12.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Qaphela: Amafomu awatholakali kulayini we-internethi.  Sicela uxhumane nehhovisi eliseduze lonyango.

Izincazelo
Uma uthola imali yosizo lukahulumeni (imali yomxhaso kahulumeni), okungafanele uyithole, uzobhekana nomsebenzi wokuyibuyisela kuhulumeni ngaphandle kokuba aboMnyango wezeNtuthuko yaBantu beneliseka ngokuthi ubungazi ukuthi akufanele uyithole. Kulesi simo, imali yosizo lukahulumeni (social grant) kumele imiswe noma ikhanselwe, ngoba kuzothathwa ngokuthi lokhu yicebo lokukhohlisa ukuzizuzisa(fraud).
Ngabe le nkonzo isebenza kubani (kobani)?
Abantu abafumana iigranti abangafanelekanga bazifumane.
Izizathu zokumiswa kwezimali zosizo lukahulumeni:
Ukwamukelwa esikhungweni sikahulumeni
Uma imali yosizo ingalandwa izinyanga zize zibe zintathu ngokulandelana.
Uma umuntu engekho eNingizimu Afrika isikhathi esingaphezu kwezinyanga eziyisithupha.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Abomnyango bazokhansela inani elikweledwa nguwe, uma begculiseka ngokuthi ubungazi ukuthi akumele uyithole leyo mali.
UMqondisi-Jikelele (uDirector-General) woMnyango angathi ngokubona kwakhe afune izimali ngokufakela inqubo yecala lomthetho maqondana nalowo muntu.
UMqondisi-Jikelele (uDirector-General) woMnyango angavumela ukuthi ukukhokhwa kwemali kuqhubeke isikhathi esithize ngokulandela izimo ezithile ezibekwe ngumuntu ofuna ukuqhubeka nokukhokhelwa leyo mali yosizo. Isicelo kumele senziwe ezinsukwini ezingu-90 ngemuva kokumiswa kwemali yosizo lukahulumeni.
Uma umuntu efuna kubuyekezwe udaba lokumiswa kwemali yosizo lukahulumeni, kumele achaze ingeniso (imali ayitholayo) ngesikhathi sokwenza isicelo. Lokhu kuzoba yisisekelo esisetshenziswa ngabomnyango lapho wenza isinqumo sokuthi ngabe kumele isibuyekeze yini isimo sakho noma cha. Umenzi wesicelo uzokwaziswa ngomphumela wesicelo ezinyangeni ezingu-3 (ezintathu) kungakafiku usuku lokubuyekeza, noma usuku okwenziwa ngalo isitifikedi.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.
Phezulu

Amafomu atholakala kwihhovisi eliseduzane lengosi Yezenhlalakahle Yomphakathi wase Ningizimu Afrika.

Ukushayela nokufunda ukushayela

Ukufaka isicelo senye ilayisensi yokushayela emva kokulahlekelwa noma ukuntshontshelwa ilayisensi
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe

Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi

Izincazelo
Lomsebenzi wenzelwe abashayeli abanelungelo lokushayela kodwa belahlekelwe yilayisensi. Ukuze uthole enye ilayisensi emva kokulahlekelwa noma ukonakalelwa yilayisensi yiya ehhovisi labaphethe izimot ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yiya ehhovisi labaphethe izimot ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
o incwadi yomazisi wakho noma ipasipoti o izithombe ezingezona zombala zomazisi ezimbili.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Uzohlolwa amehlo kanti neminwe yakho izothathwa.
Unalo ilungelo lokufaka isicelo selayisensi yokwesikhashana, kodwa kuzodingeka ukuthi ungeze imali uphinde ubanikeze ezinye izithombe ezingezona zombala zomazisi ezimbili.

Amafomu okufaka isicelo selayisensi, ifomu DL1. Yiya ehhovisi labaphethe izimot ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Lomsebenzi uchaza indlela yokuthatha izinyathelo nokuqalisa uhlelo lokulamulela noma lokuvikela ukuhlukubezwa kwabantu abadala. Kunezindlela ezehlukahlukene zokuhlukubezeka abantu abadala bazithola bebhekene nazo. Nazi ziyalandela:
Ukuhlukubezeka okuthintana nomzimba: Noma ngayisiphi isenzo esinomphumela wokulimala noma ukufa komuntu omdala. Njengokumshaya ngampama, ukushaya nangenqindi, ukumshova, ukuphatha nje ngokudlubulundela, ukumsika ngomese, ukusebenzisa amakhemikhali noma indluzula ukumvimbela ukuthi enze utho, ukusebenzisa imithi engeyona noma eyeqile, noma ngayisiphi isenzo esibanga ukulimala, ukungaphatheki kahle komzimba, ukumphuzisa amaphilisi amlalisayo noma abanga ukufa.
Ukuhlukubezeka ngokwecansi: Noma ngayisiphi isenzo esinomphumela wokusebenzisa umuntu omdala abe yisigcila socansi noma ukuzithokozisa ngokwecansi ngomuntu omdala ngaphandle kolwazi lwakhe,nokuqondisisa kwakhe noma imvume yakhe.
Ukuhlukubezeka ngokwenqondo, ngemizwa noma ngezinkulumo: Indlela ephindaphidiweyo yokuphatha umuntu omdala kabi, ngehlazo okubangela ukuthi inqondo yomuntu omdala ingasebenzi kahle nomoya wakhe uphatheke kabi akhathazeke. Njengokumkhumbisa ukungamukeleki, ukumkhipha inyumbazana, ukumqindezela okuthile, ukumcisha uthando, nokuthuthuka kwenqondo, ukumbiza ngamaganyana angathokozisi, ukumenza inhlekisa, ukumethwesa amaqadlana angasile nokuhlala ubheke okuthile kuyena.
Ukuhlukubezeka ngasezimalini noma ukunqhaphaza ngezomnotho: Ukusebenzisa imali yomuntu omdalla yegranti, impahla yakhe, izinto zakhe azikhonzile noma imali yakhe ngokungekho emthethweni nome ngaphandle kwemvumo yakhe nolwazi lwakhe ukuthi kuzokwenzekani noma anganeliswa yilesenzo.
Ukungamkhathaleli: ukungamnakekeli ungamnikezi izidingo ezibalulekile ezigcina umzimba nenqondo. Njengokumncisha ukudla, ukuthokomala indawo yokuhlala izimpahla zokwembhatha, imithi yokwelapha, ukungamngcini ajabulile anomdlandla azikhathalele.
Ukumncisha amalungelo oluntu (ukwephula umthetho): Ukumephuca umuntu omdala, amalungelo oluntu njengokumnikeza inhlonipho, isithunzi sakhe, ukumnikeza ingasese lakhe ukumvumela azicabangele akufunayo akholwe akufunayo, abeke umbono wakhe, azikhulumele ngomlomo wakhe ngendlela ayithandayo aye lapho afuna ukuya khona. Njengobuthakathi (ikakhulu abesifazane asebabadala baziphatha njengabantu asebakhulile bese abantu bacabanga ukuthi bayathakatha) loku kunomphumela wokuthi babizwe ngokuthi bangabathakathi, bakhishwe inyumbazana, balinyazwe noma babulawe.
Noma ngangubani obona ukungakhathalelwa komuntu omdala noma ukugcilazwa kwakhe noma ukungaphathwa kahle komuntu omdala, noma umuntu onesizathu sokusola ukuthi lenzizinto ezibaliwe ziyenzeka noma sezenzekile.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Uma umuntu obikayo angunonhlalakahle ovele asebenza ngazo lezindaba, kumele aqhubeke nophenyo anikeze umbiko obhaliwe ophelele kwihhovisi lentuthuko lezenhlalakahle angakapheli amaviki amane.
Uma umuntu obikayo angeyena unonhlalakahle, kungumsebenzi womphathi omkhulu wononhlalakahle wehhovisi lentuthuko lezenhlalakahle ukuthi anikeze umuntu ofanele ukuthienze lomsebenzi alindele umbiko angakapheli amaviki amane.
Uma ukuhlukubeza kuphathelene nekhaya labadala, noma inhlangano okungeyona kahulumeni, noma inhlangano yomphakathi, unonhlalakahle kwelinye ihhovisi elikhweshelene naleyondawo kumele asebenze loludaba.
Uma isimo kungesona esichazwa lapha ngaphezulu, thola isifiso somuntu ohlukubezwayo. Ungqongqoshe wentuthuko kwezenhlalakahle, noma ilungu likhansela enkulu kumele ligunyaze omunye umuntu ukuthi alekelele.
Phezulu

Izincazelo
Le nkonzo (le sevisi) itholakala kunoma ngubani umuntu ohlukunyezwe wubugebengu.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Lapho umuntu ehlukunyezwe wubugebengu, angaxhumana namaphoyisa ngokushayela kwinombolo ethi: 10111 noma ngokuya esiteshini samaphoyisa esiseduzane (bheka imininingwane yokuxhumana) ukubika ubugebengu.
Uma iphoyisa libhekana nendawo okwenzeke kuyo ubugebengu, lizothatha isitatimende kumkhalazi kanye nofakazi uma bekhona.
Umkhalazi kumele aqinisekise ukuthi i-CAS number anikezwa yona uyayigcina lapho kunemibuzo ngekusasa maqondana necala lobugebengu.
Isikhulu sephoyisa sizokhulumisana naye ebese sithatha isitatimende kumkhalazi kanye nokubhala phansi ubugebengu obubikwayo kwi-CAS.
Idokodo lecala elibhalisiwe, linikezwa iphoyisa lophenyisiso (police detective) ozoqhubeka nokuphenyisisa. Yonke imibuzo kumele iqondiswe kule-detective.
U-detective oqondene necala uzophetha uphenyisiso ebese ethula idokodo kwinkantolo efanele ukuze iqule (ishushise) icala.
U-detective uzokwazisa umkhalazi ngokuthi kumele aye nini enkantolo ukuyolalela icala.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu

Uzosizwa masishane.
Phezulu

Ifa nempahla yomuntu oshonile

Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Kuba nefa (estate) lomuntu lapho umuntu eshona eshiya impahla kanye/noma idokhumende eyiwili, noma enenhloso yokuba yiwili. Ifa kumele liphathwe kanye nokwabiwa ngokulandela iwili yomuntu oshonile, kanti uma umuntu oshonile engashiyanga iwili, ngokulandela okubekwe kumthetho. Umthetho wezokuphathwa kwamafa abashonile we-Administration of Estates Act, 1965, ubeka inqubo okumele ilandelwe ekuphatheni ifa lomuntu oshonile.
Ifa lomuntu oshinile kumele libikwe emahhovisi ka-Master kungakapheli izinsuku ezingu-14 ngemuva kosuku umuntu ashona ngalo. Ukushona kungabikwa ngunoma ngubani umuntu olawula noma ophethe impahla yomuntu oshonile, noma olawula noma ophethe idokhumende yomuntu oshonile eyiwili noma esabuwili.
uma umuntu eshonele eNingizimu Afrika nakhona washiya impahla kanye/noma idokhumende eyiwili noma enenhloso yokuba yiwili uma umuntu eshonele ngaphandle kweNingizimu Afrika nakhona washiya impahla kanye/noma idokhumende eyiwili noma enenhloso yokuba yiwili.
Ngabe libikwa kuphi ifa lomuntu oshonile?
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Uma umuntu oshonile ebehlala eNingizimu Afrika ngesikhathi sokushona kwakhe, bika ifa ku-Master onegunya kuleyo ndawo lapho umuntu oshonile ebehlala khona ngesikhathi sokushona kwakhe.
Bika ifa ngokugcwalisa ifomu le-Form J294: Inothisi yokushona kanye namanye amadokhumende okubika kufakelwe kuhla olungezansi.
Form J192: Affidavit (isitatimende esifungelwe kwabomthetho): imininingwane yoshonelwe - egcwaliswa uma oshonile engashiyanga iwili esebenzayo
o uma umuntu eshona ngaphandle kwewili noma o uma iwili ingaqoki umuntu othize njengomabi wefa noma o uma umabi wefa owaqokwa ashona, noma enqaba ukuqokwa.
sitifikedi sokushona noma ikhophi yaso eqinisekisiwe:
o isitifikedi sokuqala somshado noma ikhophi yaso eqinisekisiwe - uma lokhu kusebenza o onke amawili akuqala noma ama-codicil (okusho ezinye izingxenye zokwengezela kuwili, ezishintsha iwili yakuqala noma ezikhansela ingxenye yayo) noma amadokhumende analeyo nhloso - uma ekhona o uhla lwabantu abakweleda umuntu oshonile - uma bekhona o isimemezelo esiqinisekisa ukuthi ifa alibikwanga kwelinye ihhovisi lika-Master noma kwamanye amahhovisi asebenzela u-Master.
o uma umuntu eshona ngaphandle kwewili noma o uma iwili ingaqoki umuntu othize njengomabi wefa noma o uma umabi wefa (executor) owaqokwa ashona, noma enqaba ukuqokwa.
Form J109: Ukwamukelwa kwe-trust yokuba ngumabi wefa - okumele igcwaliswe ngamabili ngumuntu (ngabantu) abaqokwe ukuba ngumabi wefa (ababi befa)
Fakela isicelo sakho esisayiniwe sihambisane namadokhumende alandelayo:
isitifikedi sokushona noma ikhophi yaso eqinisekisiwe isitifikedi sokuqala somshado noma ikhophi yaso eqinisekisiwe - uma lokhu kusebenza onke amawili akuqala noma ama-codicil (okusho ezinye izingxenye zokwengezela kuwili, ezishintsha iwili yakuqala noma ezikhansela ingxenye yayo) noma amadokhumende analeyo nhloso - uma ekhona.
Amahhovisi asebenzela u-Master (amahhovisi omantshi) anegunya uma umuntu oshonile engashiyanga iwili esebenzayo, kanti futhi ubungakanani befa lomuntu oshonile lingaphansi kuka-R50 000. Izincwadi zesigunyaziso zivumela ukuthi umuntu oqokiwe aphathe ifa ngaphandle kokulandela inqubo ebekwe kumthetho we-Administration of Estates Act, 1965.
Phezulu

Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thola iFomu B kwi-webiste ebese uligcwalisa ulifakele ngaphambi kokuba kukhokhelwe isamba semali kwilunga le-fund.
Nikeza ngenombolo ye-ID/kamazisi noma amanye amadokhumende (isibonelo, inombolo yepasipoti, noma inombolo yemvume yokusebenza) yelunga. Kubonelelwa kakhulu ukusetshenziswa kwenombolo ye-ID.
Nikeza ngekheli lendawo kanye nekheli leposi lelunga.
Bala ukuthi yini umholo welunga ngonyaka futhi lokhu ukwenza ngendlela eqonde ngqo ngendlela okungakhonakala ngayo. Ngeke kwabangaphansi kwesilinganiso somholo welunga lapho kubandakanywa nama-alawensi kanye namanye ama-benefit ezibonelelo kulo nyaka kanye nonyaka odlule.
Indlela engcono yokugcwalisa amafomu omyalelo wentela, ukuwenza ngokusebenzisa i-inthanethi kwi-website kwi-eFiling. Isicelo sakho somyalelo wentela ngale nkonzo sivamise ukwamukelwa kumahora angu-48.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu

Isicelo somyalelo wentela owenziwa kwi-inthanethi uvamise ukwamukelwa kumahora angu-48.

Izincazelo
Uma abaphathi besikhwama sokubekela imali bedinga ukukhokha inzuzo, kumele bagcwalise ifomu lomyalo wentela balimikise emnyango wezentela. Umyalo wokuphungula intela I (IRP 3) uzokhishwa ngabomnyango wezentela yaseNingizimu Afrika, lapho banikeza abaphathi besikhwama sokubekela imali noma umqashi, umyalo wokuthi kumele baphungule imali engakanani emalini yomqashwa, okuyimali yentela.

Izincazelo
Ummangali noma owakhinyabezeka ngenkathi kwenziwa ubugebengu, unalo ilungelo, ngokuphawula kwe section 299 A of the Criminal Procedure Act, 1977 (isigaba sika 299A somthetho wokuhanjiswa kwezinqubo zobugebengu ka 1977) lokuba khona noma athumele ozommela, uma kulalelwa isithembiso sokungabaleki sommangalelwa lapho isinqumo sizoba wukuthi adedelwe noma afakwe ngaphansi kweso labasemajele.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Ummangali noma owakhinyabezeka ngenkathi kwenziwa ubugebengu kufanele azise abaphathi bezasemajele ne bhodi elalela isithembiso sokungabaleki, ngokubhala incwadi, uma afisa ukuba khona noma afuna ukuthumela ozommela uma kulalelwa isithembiso sokungabaleki sommangalelwa.
Uma isicelo sommangali semukeleka, ibhodi izakwazisa ummangali ngokumbhalela incwadi eshoyo ukuthi loku kuzakwanzeka nini, nokuthi kumele bakhulumisane nobani. Lencwadi izakusho usuku nendawo lapho ibhodi yokulalela isithembiso sokungabaleki, izakuhlanganela khona.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ibhodi yokulalela isithembiso sokungabaleki izakuphendula ummangali, ngokumbhalela incwadi zingakapheli izinsuku eziyishumi nane.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Ayikho imali ekhokhwayo.

Izincazelo
Ilayisense yethelevishini inikeza umuntu igunya lokuthenga ithelevishini eyodwa ukuze abukele ithelevishini (TV). Ilayisense ivuselelwa njalo ngonyaka.
Ukuze uthole olunye ulwazi, ngena kwi website ethi www.tvlic.co.za
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Vuselela ilayisense yakho ePosini eliseduzane.
Fakela okulandelayo:
Ikhophi yenothisi yokuvuselela;
Ubufakazi bendawo ohlala kuyo kanye nekheli leposi, nezinombolo zocingo;
Khopha imali efanele yokuvuselela.

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Ezobudlelwane babaqashi nabasebenzi
Isimo
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi, hamba uye kwaboMnyango wezemiSebenzi.
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Abahlinzeki ngoqeqesho kumele bathathe isinqumo ngokuthi ngabe yiwaphi ama-learnership abanganikeza ngawo.
Uma abaqeqeshi sebekhethe i-learnership kumele bathole abaqasi abazonikeza abafundi ithuba lokusebenzela ukuthola isipiliyoni.
Abahlinzeki ngoqeqesho kanye nabaqashi abazonikeza abafundi ithuba lokuthola isipiliyoni kumele bavumelane ngokulandelayo:
o ngabe bangaki abafundi abazobaqeqesha;
o inqubo nemibandela (criteria) yokukhetha abafundi; kanye o nokuthi bazobathola kanjani abafundi.
Abahlinzeki boqeqesho kumele baqale uqeqesho ngosuku okuvunyelwane ngalo kusivumelwano se-learnership.

Izincazelo
Ukwehlukaniswa okusipesheli kwezimot kuyanikezwa izinhlobo ezithile zezimoto ezenza umsebenzi othile ezingavamile ukusebenzisa imigwaqo yomphakathi.
Imoto edonswa ngenye noma ugandaganda ziyakhululwa ukuthi zingakhokhi imali yelayisensi uma umnikazi wemoto engumlimi kanti nemot isetshenziselwa ukulima. Loku kuvunyelwe futhi uma imoto yakhiwe kabusha ukuthi ibe ngeyomjaho, noma umboniso.

Izincazelo
Ikhansela ithathe isinqumo sokuthi inikeze labo Bantu ithuba lokufaka isicelo sokubhalisela ukuba ngoSosayensi bendalo abawufundele lomsebenzi ngaphansi kwemigomo ethile.
Wonke umuntu ongenawo amaphepha omsebenzi avunyelwe noma aziwa yikhansela angasifaka isicelo.
Abafaki zicelo kumele:
bahlonishwe ngabasebenza nabo ngolwazi abanalo kulomsebenzi wabo abawukhethile kube nendima ethile abayidlalile kumphakathi waboSosayensi babe nolwazi oluyilona abalufundele olungekho ngaphansi kweminyaka eyishumi angezona izincamu ngaphambi kokuba bafake izicelo kulowomsebenzi abasifakela wona isicelo.
Isicwayiso: uma ngabe uke washiya lomsebenzi wakho owufundele eminyakeni eyishumi eyedlule, ungeke uvunyelwe ukubhalisa ngaphansi kwaloluhlelo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu elisemthethweni lokubhalisa.
Thumela ifomu elisayiniwe kanye incwadi elandisa ngokufunda nolwazi equkethe:
Amaripoti amathathu (wathumele ngeComputer) owabhalile noma lapho udlale indima ebalulekile ekuwabhaleni. Uma ngabe ungeke ukwazi ukuthumela amanye amaripoti ngoba kunezimfihlo ezithile, kuyodingeka ubufakazi bokuthi kubhalwe nguwena.
Ama-afidavithi aphuma kubantu ababili abakufakazelayo baphinde baqinisekise ukuthi lomsebenzi othi uyawazi ngepela kunjalo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ukubhalisa kungathatha amaviki angu 6 kuya ku 8 kuya ngokuthi amaphepha adingekayo aphelele yini.
Abantu ababili abakhethwe yikomidi elilulekayo lalowomsebenzi bazokuhlaba ngemibuzo ehambelana nesicelo sakho.
Umphumela wokuhlolwa kwakho uzokwaziswa emva komhlangano omkhulu wekhansela.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Imininingwane yeComputer yasebhange kanye nokufaka imali ngokuya ebhange: Sicela usebenzise amaletha okuqala amagama akho kanye nesibongo sakho ukuze ubonakalise inkokhelo yakho e-akhawuntini yethu ebhange ubese ufekisa ubufakazi bokukhokha nekhasi lokuqala lesicelo sakho ku 841 1057.

Izincazelo
Imvume yokufunda yidokhumende (yincwadi esemthethweni) enikeza abantu bangaphandle ilungelo lokufunda nezikhungo zemfundo ephakeme eNingizimu Afrika. Yenza isicelo ngokwakho kumahhovisi amanxusa (Embassy) eNingizimu Afrika noma kwi-Mission.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa isicelo kwifomu le-BI-159.
Khipha:
o incwadi esemthethweni noma ubufakazi obukhombisa ukuthi ubhalisile (urejistile) nesikhungo semfundo eNingizimu Afrika o imininingwane maqondana namalungiselelo endawo yokuhlala o ubufakazi bokuthi kunezimali ezanele zokukhokhela imfundo, imali yokuzinakekela kanye nezinye izindleko o isitifikedi samaphoyisa sokuba msulwa (sokungabi namacala) maqondana namazwe ohlale kuwo isikhathi esingaphezu konyaka owodwa. Isitifikedi kumele silethwe kungakapheli izinyanga eziyishumi nambili zokwenziwa kwesicelo, uma singekho ngaleso sikhathi wenza ngaso isicelo o ubufakazi bethikithi lokubuyela emuva noma isiqiniseko sokuthi uzobuyela emuva, lokhu kungenziwa ngemali yediphozithi elingana izindleko zethikithi lendiza yokuya kulelo lizwe obuya kulo noma ohlala kulo, uma kudingeka lokho o incwadi kadokotela ewubufakazi bokuthi umuntu owenza isicelo uphile kahle.
Abenzi bezicelo ngeke bakwazi ukungena eNingizimu Afrika ngaphambi kokuba izicelo zabo zemukelwe.
Abancane ngeminyaka kumele banikeze imininingwane yomuntu waseNingizimu Afrika ozoba ngumgadi (ngumnakekeli). Imininingwane kumele inikezwe ihambisana nencwadi yesiqiniseko, ubufakazi bemvume yokuhlala ebuya kubo bobabili abazali, noma ebuya kumzali oyedwa ongumnakekeli kanye nobufakazi bokuthi unguye yedwa ongumnakekeli.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Amaviki amathathu.
Phezulu

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Izimvume namalayisense
Isicelo semvume noma selayisense
Ukufaka isicelo sokudoba izinhlanzi ukuze uziphili
Ukufaka isicelo sokudoba izinhlanzi ukuze uziphilise nokukhululwa ukuthi wenze njalo
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ukudoba izinhlanzi ukuze uziphilise kusebenza emiphakathini eseduzane nolwandle imiphakathi ethembele kokudotshiwe ukuze idle, noma benalo ilungelo lokuthengisa noma bashintshise ngokunye. Okwamanje abaziphilisa ngokudoba izinhlanzi babhekwe ngokunikwa imvume yokudoba kuze kubekhona umgomo wezokudoba. Ukuze umuntu akwazi ukudobela ukuziphilisa kumele afake isicelo sokunikezwa imvume abese uyakhululwa ukuthi adobele ukuziphilisa.
Ukuze usebenzise ilungelo lakho lokudoba faka isicelo sokukhululwa ozolinikezwa ngungqongqoshe wegatsha eliphethe izindaba zenhlalo yendawo nezokuvakasha noma isiphathimandla esigunyazwe ukwenza lowomsebenzi ngaphasi kukasekisheni 18 womthetho wezilwane eziphila emanzini ka 1998 Marine Living Resources Act, Act No. 18 of 1998. Ukuze akhululwe umuntu kumele afake isicelo sakhe kwikomidi eseduze naye ebekelwe ukwenza lomsebenzi aphinde agcine izidingo ezibekiwe. Ngokomthetho akekho umuntu ozovele ayodobela ukuziphilisa ngaphandle kwemvume.
Imvume iyavuselelwa njalo ngomnyaka, uma uhluleke ukuthobela umthetho wokukhululwa ukuthi udobe imvume yakho izokumiswa, noma ichithwe noma ilungelo lakho lihoxiswe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Faka isicelo sokukhululwa ukuthi udobe kwikomidi ephethe lowomsebenzi eseduze nawe uma imvume yakho isiphelile njalo ekupheleni komnyaka.
Abaqalisayo ukucela imvume kumele bagcine izidingo ezibekiwe zokudobela ukuziphilisa ngaphambi kokuthi banikezwe imvume.
Ofaka isicelo kumele asho ukuthi uzodoba izinhlanzi ezinjani.
Kuyahlolwa ukuthi imvume yakho iyisima kanjani ngaphambi kokuthi unikezwe imvume yomnyaka olandelayo, uma kukhona abangakufezi okubekiwe, bayasulwa ohlwini lwabantu abanemvume.
Izisebenzi zegatsha zibiza umhlangano womphakathi ukuzonikeza imvume kubo bonke ababhalisele ukudoba.
Izisebenzi zegatsha zichaza izimiselo zemvume kanye nokuthi kumele badobe okungakanani.
Bonke abanikezwe imvume bayasayina ohlwini ukuze bakhombise ukuthi bayemukele imvume.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha inyanga yinye noma ngaphezudlwana ukusebenza isicelo sakho, kuzoya ngokuthi zingakanani izicelo ezifakiwe.

Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thola iFomu B kwi-webiste ebese uligcwalisa ulifakele ngaphambi kokuba kukhokhelwe isamba semali kwilunga le-fund.
Nikeza ngenombolo ye-ID/kamazisi noma amanye amadokhumende (isibonelo, inombolo yepasipoti, noma inombolo yemvume yokusebenza) yelunga. Kubonelelwa kakhulu ukusetshenziswa kwenombolo ye-ID.
Nikeza ngekheli lendawo kanye nekheli leposi lelunga.
Bala ukuthi yini umholo welunga ngonyaka futhi lokhu ukwenza ngendlela eqonde ngqo ngendlela okungakhonakala ngayo. Ngeke kwabangaphansi kwesilinganiso somholo welunga lapho kubandakanywa nama-alawensi kanye namanye ama-benefit ezibonelelo kulo nyaka kanye nonyaka odlule.
Indlela engcono yokugcwalisa amafomu omyalelo wentela, ukuwenza ngokusebenzisa i-inthanethi kwi-website kwi-eFiling. Isicelo sakho somyalelo wentela ngale nkonzo sivamise ukwamukelwa kumahora angu-48.

Izincazelo
Ilayisensi yokushayela okwesikhashana inikezwa abantu abakwazi ukushayela kodwa:
o balindele ilayisensi yabo eyikhadi o bafake isicelo sokuvuselela ilayisensi yabo o balahlekelwe yilayisensi yabo.
Lelayisensi iyaphela emuva kwezinyanga eziyisithupha kanti itholakala kuwo onke amasenta okuvivinyela ukushayela.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
o umazisi wakho noma ipasipoti o izithombe ezingezona zombala zencwadi yomazisi ezimbili.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.

Ezokuthutha nokuhamba
Isicelo senombolo yokubhalisa imoto
Ukufaka isicelo senombholo yokubhalisa encwadini yezimoto

Izincazelo
Inombholo yokubhalisa encwadini yezimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni inikezwa abantu abangebona baseMzansi abangenawo umazisi wakuleli, kanti umsebenzi wayo wukuthi iyinombholo emukelekile esetshenziselwa imininingwane yasemgwaqweni. Lenombholo iyafuneka uma umuntu ogeyena waseMzansi efuna ukubhalisa imoto lapha eMzansi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Vakashela ehhovisi labaphethe izimoto ezihamba emgwaqweni eliseduze nawe.
o ikhophi enesitembu samaphoyisa sepasipoti noma sephomede yokuhlala kuleli okwesikhashana o umazisi okhishwa yisiphathimandla esaziwayo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha amaviki angu 6 ukusebenza isicelo sakho.

Izinkonzo Kubantu
Ezemidlalo nokungcebeleka
Ezokungcebeleka imidlalo nokuphumula
Elayisense yethelevishini
Isimo
Ilayisense yethelevishini inikeza umuntu igunya lokuthenga ithelevishini eyodwa ukuze abukele ithelevishini (TV).
Lapho uthenga ilayisense yeThelevishini okokuqala, ukhokha inani lemali yonyaka egcwele okungu-R225. Intengo yelayisense yesaphulelo kwabathize (concessionary licence) ngu-R65 ngonyaka.
Abantu abalandelayo bavunyelwe ilayisense yesaphulelo kwabathize:
Noma ngubani oneminyaka engaphezulu kuka-70 noma ngaphezu kwayo, ongahlali nabantu abasebenzayo ancike kubo noma angancikile kubo.
Izinhlangano zidinga ilayisense yethelevishini, kwithelevishini nethelevishini ngayinye enayo enhlanganweni. Emakhaya badinga ilayisense eyodwa kuwo wonke amathelevishini abanawo endlini.
Umthengisi wethelevishini kumele azise abathengi ukuthi ngeke bathenga ithelevishini ngaphandle kokukhipha ubufakazi belayisense yethelevishini. Abafuna ukuthenga amathelevishini bangenza isicelo samalayisense iPosini eliseduzane, noma bacele umthengisi ukubathengela ilayisense.
Ilayisense ivuselelwa njalo ngonyaka.
Ukuze uthole olunye ulwazi, ngena kwi website ethi www.tvlic.co.za
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Yenza isicelo ePosini eliseduzane noma kumthengisi onegunya lokukhipha amalayisense eTV.
Fakela okulandelayo:
Inombolo yakho kaMazisi (ye-ID)
Ifomu lesicelo kanye nemali yesicelo
Ubufakazi bendawo ohlala kuyo kanye nekheli leposi, nezinombolo zocingo
Ungenza nesicelo kwi-inthanethi ekhelini elithi:www.tvlic.co.za

Izincazelo
Imali engabuyiselwa emuva yesicelo engu-R500 kumele ikhokhwe ngokheshi noma ngesheke eliqinisekiwe yibhangi noma ngokuyifakela kwi-akhawunti ngendlela ye-elektroniki.
Ubhaliso (irejistreshini) ngeyesikhathi sonyaka owodwa, ukusukela ngosuku okukhishwa ngalo isitifikedi sobhaliso (serejistreshini).
Ukudiliva izincwadi ezifakelwe amakheli
Ukudiliva amaphasela obunzima bekhilogramu eyodwa nengaphansi
Ukukhishwa kwezitembu zokuposa
Ukunikezwa kwamabhokisi okuposa emigwaqeni kanye namabhokisi amaposi.
Abantu ziqu ngokwabo kanye namabhizinisi bakhululekile ukwenza izicelo zelayisense zokuqhuba amabhizinisi eposi yemisebenzi yeposi engagodliwe (engabekelwe eceleni).
Imali engabuyiselwa emuva yesicelo engu-R500 kumele ikhokhwe ngokheshi noma ngesheke eliqinisekiwe yibhangi noma ngokuyifakela kwi-akhawunti ngendlela ye-elektroniki.
Ubhaliso (irejistreshini) ngeyesikhathi sonyaka owodwa, ukusukela ngosuku okukhishwa ngalo isitifikedi sobhaliso (serejistreshini).
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi, ngena kwi-
UMnyango wezokuXhumana
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo uwenze abemathathu.
Khokhela imali yesicelo ngokheshe, noma isheke eliqinisekiwe yibhangi
Fakela ifomu lesicelo kwaboMnyango wezokuXhumana kanye nobufakazi bokuthi ukhokhile
Thumela ubufakazi bokukhokha ngefekisi kule nombolo: 12 427 8536.

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Ezobudlelwane babaqashi nabasebenzi zinyunyane nezinhlangano zabaqashi
Isicelo sokusungula iforamu yasemsebenzini
Isicelo sokusungula iforamu yasemsebenzini
Isimo
Amaforamu asemsebenzini asungulelwa okulandelayo ukuqhubela phambili izidingo zabasebenzi emisebenzini, kodwa hhayi amalunga enyunyane kuphela;
ukukhuphula izinga lezokusebenza kahle ngezinga eliphezulu;
ukubonisana nabasebenzi ngezinto ezithile; kanye nokubandakanya abasebenzi ekuthathweni kwezinqumo ngokuhlanganyela kwezinye izinto.
Iforamu yasemsebenzini ingasungulwa noma kukuphi lapho okunabasebenzi abangaphezulu kwekhulu khona. Leli nani alibandakanyi abasebenzi abangomininjela bezinga eliphezulu. Isicelo singenziwa kuphela uma iforamu yasemsebenzini ingekho, kanti ingenziwa ngokulandela umthetho wezobudlelwane babaqashi nabasebenzi we-Labour Relations Act, 1995.
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi, hamba uye kwaboMnyango wezemiSebenzi.
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Inyunyane kumele ifakele ikhophi yesitifikedi esiqinisekisiwe yesivumelwano sezingxoxo phakathi kwabaqasi nabasebenzi (collective agreement), esiwubufakazi bokuthi yamukelwe ngumqashi.
Omele inyunyane (abamele izinyunyane) kumele agcwalise (kumele bacwalise) ifomu le-LRA 5.1.
Ikhophi laleli fomu kumele linikezwe olunye uhlangothi olithintekayo (umqashi) njengobufakazi kanti ikhophi yefomu kumele ibandakanywe ngokufakela la madokhumende alandelayo:
Ikhophi lesilipi esisayiniwe uma ikhophi ihanjiswe ngesandla
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha izinsuku ezingu-30 ukusungula iforamu.
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe

Ukuthuthela eNingizimu Afrika noma ukuvakashela
Ilungelo lokuhlala isikhashana
Isicelo semvume yokusebenza
Isicelo semvume yokusebenza
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Imvume yokusebenza yidokhumende esemthethweni enikeza abantu ababuya ngaphandle ilungelo lokusebenza nanoma yiyiphi inhlangano eNingizimu Afrika. Imvume yokusebenza inikezwa abantu ababuya ngaphandle kuphela.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Abenzi bezicelo zayo yonke inhlobo yemvume yokusebenza kumele bafakele izicelo kumahhovisi abameli bamazwe eNingizimu Afrika abangaphandle (diplomatic representative office), kuzwe umenzi wesicelo asuka kulo, noma ehhovisi lerijini (lesifunda) loMnyango lapho umenzi wesicelo azoqashwa kulo.
Fakela okulandelayo:
o ipasipoti esebenzayo ezinsukwini ezingu-30 eRiphabliki o isitifikedi sokugonywa esidingeka ngokulandela umthetho o imibiko yempilo kanye neyezama-x-ray (medical and radiological reports). Lokhu akudingekile kwizingane ezingaphansi kweminyaka engu-12 kanye nabesimame abakhulelwe akumele babenalo mbiko weze-x-ray o isitifikedi somshado (uma ushadile), iafidavithi (incwadi efungelwe ngokomthetho), isitifikedi sokushona (uma owakwakhe eshonile) noma incwadi yedivosi o izitifikedi zokuzalwa zezingane ezihambisana nomenzi wesicelo o isitifikedi samaphoyisa sokuba msulwa (clearance certificate) somenzi wesicelo sawo onke amazwe umenzi wesicelo kanye nowakwakhe abahlale kuwo, ukusukela kwiminyaka engu-18, kanye nezingane ezincike kuye ezineminyaka engu-18 ukuya kwephezulu, ezizobe zinomenzi wesicelo eRiphabliki o isitifikedi sogonyo lwesifo se-yellow fever uma uzohamba noma unenhloso yokuhamba udlule endaweni evamise ukuba nesifo se-yellow fever.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Asikho isikhathi esibekiwe (esinqunyiwe).
Phezulu
Izindleko
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
